<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种基于信息论的热力学第二定律推广边界已被提出，但在测量有噪声、反馈有时间关联的真实条件下，这些边界的相对优劣尚不清楚。作者旨在系统比较不同信息度量在非理想条件下的表现。

Method: 在欠阻尼反馈控制系统中进行实验与理论研究，采用马尔可夫测量但非马尔可夫控制序列，比较三种代表性边界：基于传递熵的边界、基于不可用信息的边界和基于马尔可夫互信息的边界。

Result: 没有一种边界是普遍最优的。测量噪声优先损害依赖详细轨迹统计的信息度量，而基于瞬时相关量的度量相对鲁棒。轨迹依赖边界迅速恶化，导致马尔可夫互信息边界在较宽噪声范围内超越不可用信息边界，出现交叉现象。

Conclusion: 依赖详细轨迹统计的信息论描述在真实条件下存在根本性局限。该研究为超越理想化反馈协议的信息热力学提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Information engines exploit feedback to extract work from thermal fluctuations, extending the second law of thermodynamics through information-theoretic bounds. While several such bounds have been proposed, their relative performance under realistic conditions---where measurements are noisy and feedback is temporally correlated---remains largely unclear. Here, we experimentally and theoretically investigate this problem in an underdamped feedback-controlled system with Markovian measurements but non-Markovian control sequences. We compare three representative bounds derived from transfer entropy, unavailable information, and Markovian mutual information, and find that none is universally optimal. Instead, measurement noise preferentially affects information measures that rely on detailed trajectory statistics, while leaving quantities based on instantaneous correlations comparatively robust. As a consequence, trajectory-dependent bounds deteriorate rapidly, giving rise to a crossover in which the Markovian mutual-information bound becomes tighter than the unavailable-information bound over a broad range of measurement noise. Our results reveal a general limitation of information-theoretic descriptions that rely on detailed trajectory statistics in realistic settings and provide a unified perspective on information thermodynamics beyond idealised feedback protocols.

</details>


### [76] [Quantum Chaos and Diffusive Transport from Geometric Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28579)

TL;DR: 该文证明了几何随机性本身即可作为产生量子混沌与扩散输运的独立机制：在具有均匀耦合的随机树状分层图上，层大小决定混沌行为——大面积层导致量子混沌、能级排斥和扩散输运，而准一维极限下则出现局域态与退局域态共存，导致能级排斥受抑但弹道输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌和扩散输运传统上依赖微观无序或多体相互作用来研究。作者试图揭示：仅凭几何随机性（无微观无序、无多体相互作用）是否足以产生这些现象，从而确立几何随机性作为一种基本且独立的量子混沌生成与调控机制。

Method: 研究非相互作用量子粒子在具有均匀耦合的随机局域树状分层图（random locally tree-like layered graphs）上的行为。这些图可视为规则正方晶格或梯子的结构无序推广，亦可等价视为多组分一维链，链间具有随机链接。通过调控层的尺寸（从大面积层到准一维极限），分析能级统计和输运性质的变化。

Result: （1）大面积层情况：出现鲁棒的量子混沌、能级排斥（level repulsion）和扩散输运。（2）准一维极限：大量局域态与退局域态共存，能级排斥受抑制，但退局域态驱动弹道输运（ballistic transport）。

Conclusion: 几何随机性是产生和调控量子混沌的一种基本且独立的机制，有效图维度决定了混沌动力学的有无，无需借助微观无序或多体相互作用。

Abstract: The physics of quantum chaos and diffusive transport is typically studied in settings with microscopic disorder or many-body interactions. In this Letter, we demonstrate that these phenomena can arise purely from geometric randomness. By studying non-interacting quantum particles on random locally tree-like layered graphs with uniform couplings, we show that the geometric randomness and effective graph dimensionality dictates the presence of chaotic dynamics or lack thereof. These graphs can be considered as structurally disordered generalisations of regular square lattices or ladders, or equivalently as multi-component one-dimensional chains with random links between the components. We find that an extensive layer size yields robust quantum chaos, level repulsion, and diffusive transport. Conversely, in the quasi-one-dimensional limit, we find the coexistence of extensive number of localised and delocalised states -- this leads to suppressed level repulsion accompanied by the latter driving ballistic transport. These results establish geometric randomness as a fundamental and independent mechanism for generating and tuning quantum chaos.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过分析能级间距的功率谱，重新审视一维准周期势场中的金属-绝缘体相变。研究发现，无相互作用时功率谱始终呈布朗噪声特征，无法区分相态；而引入相互作用后，金属相呈粉红噪声、绝缘相呈布朗噪声，功率谱成为有效的相变探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在无相互作用体系中难以通过谱统计清晰捕捉金属-绝缘体相变特征，需要寻找新的、更灵敏的相变指标，尤其是在相互作用与无相互作用两种情形下均能可靠工作的诊断工具。

Method: 采用自旋-1/2粒子模型，在一维晶格上施加在位准周期势，并引入可调最近邻相互作用。核心分析方法是对连续能级间距序列做傅里叶变换，取其模平方得到功率谱，结合能级间距分布、能级数方差以及能量本征态结构进行综合分析。

Result: 无相互作用时，功率谱在所有区域均呈布朗噪声（1/f²）行为，无法反映相变；引入相互作用后，金属相功率谱变为粉红噪声（1/f），绝缘相保持布朗噪声，形成鲜明对比。能级间距分布和能级数方差的分析也支持上述结论。此外，统计量和本征态结构在两种情形下均能可靠指示相变。

Conclusion: 功率谱在相互作用体系中是探测金属-绝缘体相变的有效且灵敏的工具，其噪声颜色的转变标记了相变的发生。同时，能级统计和本征态结构在更广泛的条件下（无论有无相互作用）均可作为相变的可靠判据。

Abstract: We revisit the metal-insulator transition in a one-dimensional lattice subjected to an on-site quasiperiodic potential, modeled using spin-1/2 particles with tunable nearest-neighbor interactions. To characterize the transition, we examine the power-spectrum, defined as the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of consecutive level spacings. In the absence of interactions, the power-spectrum fails to capture clear signatures of the transition, consistently exhibiting brown-noise-like behavior across regimes. In contrast, the interacting case reveals a marked change: the metallic phase displays spectral fluctuations consistent with pink noise, while the insulating phase is characterized by brown noise. These results are supported by analyses of the level spacing distribution and the level number variance. Additionally, we demonstrate that both statistics and the structure of energy eigenstates provide reliable indicators of the transition, in both interacting and non-interacting scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the minimal model of a two dimensional Brownian dimer consisting of two overdamped monomers, trapped in an isotropic harmonic potential and mutually coupled by a non-reciprocal harmonic spring that violates Newton's action-reaction principle. The exact steady state probability distribution and current are explicitly calculated for the zero-rest-length limit of the spring, which eventually maps our model to another non-equilibrium phenomenon, called Brownian gyration. For a spring with finite rest length, these quantities are calculated numerically.

</details>


### [79] [Ensemble theory of thermodiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27839)

Result: 该模型在硬核气体系统中，对宽范围外加温度梯度下的非平衡质量分布均能给出准确预测；同时可利用解析表达式直接估算Soret系数等输运性质。

Conclusion: 路径系综理论为耦合热-扩散输运提供了一种统一且高效的框架：平衡态标定即可替代大量非平衡模拟，兼具预测准确性与计算经济性，在复杂流体的输运性质研究中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Heat flow sustained by a temperature difference generates a gradient of matter as observed, for example, in atomistic simulations of hard-core gases. Abstract: The Mpemba effect, wherein a system prepared farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, has been extensively investigated in a wide range of physical systems. In contrast, its role in biologically relevant non-equilibrium processes remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate anomalous relaxation in the six-state chemomechanical network model of the Kinesin molecular motor under both equilibrium and non-equilibrium conditions. We first establish the existence of the Mpemba effect in chemical equilibrium and show that many of its qualitative features can be understood from the underlying free-energy landscape. We then examine the effects of mechanical and chemical driving, showing that breaking detailed balance primarily reshapes the Mpemba phase diagram without qualitatively altering the relaxation phenomenology over the physically relevant parameter regime. Finally, we demonstrate that the relaxation of the motor velocity also mirrors the anomalous relaxation of the underlying stochastic dynamics, thereby identifying an experimentally accessible signature of the Mpemba effect. Our results establish molecular motors as a promising baseline for studying anomalous relaxation in living systems and suggest a broader framework for exploring the Mpemba effect in non-equilibrium biochemical networks.

Abstract: Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality provides an example of macroscopic scaling generated by microscopic violation of detailed balance. While one- and two-dimensional realizations have been explored in driven condensates and growing interfaces, demonstrating KPZ scaling in three spatial dimensions remains a major challenge. Here we propose a three-dimensional exciton-polariton crystal as a platform for observation of 3D KPZ universality. Starting from a stochastic driven-dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a condensate formed in a three-dimensional photonic-crystal lower-polariton band, we eliminate the massive density and reservoir modes and obtain an effective $3+1$-dimensional KPZ equation for the condensate phase. Numerical simulations of both the KPZ equation and the full driven-dissipative polariton model show an intermediate-asymptotic regime in which the first-order coherence obeys $-\ln |\gone(0,Δt)|\propto |Δt|^{2β}$ and $-\ln |\gone(Δr,0)|\propto |Δr|^{2χ}$, with exponents consistent with the $3+1$ KPZ benchmarks $β= 0.1845$, $χ= 0.3135$. Our results identify three-dimensional polariton crystals as a controllable quantum fluid route to higher-dimensional nonequilibrium universality.

</details>

This bridging behavior arises because, in the critical interval, the dynamics are governed by avalanches.

Result: 该缩放理论成功描述了相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落：在小尺度上系统表现为相干行为（与量子简单对称排斥过程一致），在大尺度上恢复非相干扩散输运（与宏观涨落理论兼容）。介观区域提供了两者之间的平滑内插，超越了标准涨落流体力学的适用范围。

Conclusion: IQSEP模型及其介观缩放理论提供了一个统一的框架，能够涵盖从量子相干到经典非相干扩散输运的完整跨尺度物理图像，尤其填补了中间尺度量子相干涨落描述的理论空白，对理解相互作用量子多体系统中的输运现象具有重要意义。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process (IQSEP), a family of models describing the stochastic quantum hopping of charged particles along the edges of a lattice, with hopping amplitudes that depend on the occupations of neighbouring sites. In the absence of interactions, they reduce to the standard quantum simple symmetric exclusion process, exhibiting coherent diffusive transport. For interactions of order one, they capture incoherent diffusive transport and its fluctuations, characterized by density-dependent diffusivity and mobility, making contact with the macroscopic fluctuation theory. By rescaling the interaction strength appropriately with the lattice mesh, we define a mesoscopic scaling regime that retains a finite coherence length in the continuous thermodynamic limit. This regime interpolates between coherent behavior at small length scales and incoherent behavior at large scales. The resulting scaling theory accounts for fluctuations of quantum coherences in interacting diffusive systems, going beyond the scope of standard fluctuating hydrodynamics.

Abstract: Structural heterogeneity constrains collective dynamics in complex systems. However, its analytical tractability out of equilibrium remains limited. In this work, we study a class of kinetic Ising neural networks driven out of equilibrium by a homeostatic feedback loop between the neuronal excitability and the population firing rate. Using a Curie-Weiss heterogeneous mean-field approximation validated by Monte Carlo simulations, we provide an analytical characterization of how a macroscopic synchronized limit cycle emerges via an Andronov-Hopf bifurcation on heterogeneous networks. We derive closed-form phase boundaries and show that the onset of oscillations is explicitly controlled by network heterogeneity through the degree moment ratio. Degree heterogeneity decouples the spiking rate per neuron m from the spiking rate per synapse u, generating physical phenomena absent in homogeneous systems. These include (i) kinematic waves of sequential, degree-ordered activations propagating from the network periphery to the hubs, and (ii) a low-temperature phase-separated state emerging via a pitchfork bifurcation. We prove that for highly heterogeneous topologies, this phase-separated fixed point stabilizes and dynamically destroys the synchronized limit cycle. These results provide a mathematical framework for understanding how heterogeneity regulates macroscopic oscillations and out-of-equilibrium transitions in neural networks

</details>

Method: 作者首先推导出描述Riviera模型动力学的无穷层级动力学方程；随后引入一种系统化的截断闭合方案，该方案可在任意有限阶实现；最后逐阶求解所得有限系统，分析其收敛行为，并在高阶下提取堵塞密度及全时间演化。

Abstract: The Riviera model is a random sequential deposition model for house building on a lattice, in which a house cannot be built when both nearest-neighbor sites are already occupied. In one dimension, an attempted deposition is therefore rejected whenever the target site is an isolated vacancy. Despite the apparent simplicity of this rule, no exact analytical solution of the original model is currently known. We derive an infinite hierarchy of kinetic equations governing its dynamics and introduce a systematic closure scheme that can be implemented at arbitrary finite order. Solving the resulting systems order by order, we show that the corresponding kinetics converges rapidly toward the exact behavior. At high order, the method yields an exceptionally accurate estimate of the jamming density while retaining explicit analytical expressions for the full time-dependent evolution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Baik-Ben Arous-Péché (BBP) 阈值等方法要求特征值必须从噪声体中清晰分离，但实际中信号可能具有广泛秩且嵌入准连续谱区，无法形成孤立尖峰。因此需要一种不依赖尖峰分离、能在随机矩阵普适类附近直接追踪谱变形的信号检测判据。

Method: 采用重整化群（RG）框架，将信号检测问题映射为有效场论。核心思路是检验描述谱尾部自由度集体行为的高斯不动点的稳定性：若高斯不动点在RG流下不稳定，则表明信号存在。同时分析信号引发的正则维度的标度依赖性，将其解释为维度相变现象。

Result: 该框架能够一致地给出检测下限（lower limit of detection），无需依赖特征值的尖峰分离。通过稳定性分析可成功识别准连续谱区内信号的存在与否，且信号诱导的标度依赖性表现为维度相变。

Conclusion: 重整化群方法为高维信号检测提供了概念简洁且超越传统BBP阈值局限的判据，将信号检测重新表述为高斯不动点的稳定性问题，并将其与维度相变联系起来，为广泛秩信号的检测奠定了理论框架。

Abstract: We review the renormalization group framework for signal detection in high-dimensional data, tailored to the regime where the signal may be of extensive rank and does not separate from the noise bulk as isolated spikes. The framework provides a conceptually simple criterion for distinguishing signal from noise within a quasi-continuous spectral region near a random-matrix universality class. This scenario lies beyond the reach of standard methods such as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché threshold, which requires eigenvalues to be cleanly separated from the bulk. The renormalization group approach, by contrast, directly tracks spectral deformations and consistently yields a lower limit of detection without relying on spike separation. We review results that identify the presence of a signal by testing the stability of the Gaussian fixed point of an effective field theory for the collective behaviour of the degrees of freedom in the spectral tail, where the signal resides. We also discuss how the scale dependence of the canonical dimension, induced by the signal, manifests as a dimensional phase transition.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在各类任务中表现卓越，但其内部动态机制仍不为人所充分理解。现有的可解释性方法多从静态表征或线性角度出发，缺乏对LLM深层非线性动力学行为的系统性刻画，因此需要引入混沌理论工具来揭示其内在运行规律。

Method: 作者采用非线性动力学与混沌理论的分析工具：1）通过文本轨迹和隐藏状态轨迹分析，考察邻近轨迹对初始条件的敏感性，使用多种距离度量验证发散模式；2）对Transformer各子模块（自注意力、前馈网络、归一化、残差连接）进行精确雅可比分析，量化各组件对扰动的扩张与抑制效应；3）利用递归图（recurrence plots）比较LLM与经典混沌系统（如Lorenz吸引子）的结构相似性；4）对隐藏状态空间进行维度分析，检测 fractal 结构。

Result: 研究发现：LLM展现出间歇性、跳跃式的邻近轨迹发散与有界演化相结合的混沌特征，且不同距离度量结果一致。雅可比分析表明，自注意力和前馈网络负责扩张和传播扰动，而层归一化和残差连接则抵消扩张、促进稳定。递归图揭示LLM与Lorenz吸引子等经典混沌系统在结构上具有相似性。维度分析在隐藏状态空间中检测到 fractal 结构，尤其在模型最后几层最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM的内部动态本质上具有混沌特性，注意力机制引入的非线性耦合是驱动该混沌行为的关键机制。模型的稳定结构（归一化与残差连接）与混沌驱动力（注意力、前馈网络）之间存在动态平衡。这一发现为理解LLM的工作原理提供了全新的非线性动力学视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet their internal dynamics remain poorly understood. In this work, we apply the tools of nonlinear dynamics and chaos theory to LLMs. By analyzing both text and hidden state trajectories, we demonstrate that LLMs exhibit hallmark signatures of chaos, including strong sensitivity to initial conditions, manifested as intermittent, jump-like divergence of nearby trajectories combined with bounded evolution, with consistent results across different distance metrics. An exact Jacobian analysis of the Transformer's sub-blocks shows that self-attention and the feed-forward network expand and propagate perturbations, while normalization and residual connections counteract this expansion and promote stability. Recurrence plots show structural similarities between LLMs and canonical chaotic systems such as the Lorenz attractor, while dimension analysis reveals fractal structures in the hidden state space, particularly pronounced in the last layers. We propose that the nonlinear coupling induced by attention mechanisms plays a key role in driving this chaotic behavior.

</details>

### [88] [Poset-refined majorization relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28061)
*Alexander E. Guterman,Michael M. Wolf*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文通过将特征值/奇异值的完美对齐排序放松为偏序，在基变换矩阵允许LU分解的条件下，加强了Ky Fan控制不等式、Horn对数控制不等式和von Neumann迹不等式等经典结果，并给出在张量积和Kronecker和等方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典矩阵控制不等式通常要求特征值或奇异值完全对齐的排序，这一限制条件过于严格。作者试图通过放松对齐条件为偏序，探索能否在更一般的条件下获得更强的控制关系。

Method: 作者将经典控制关系中对特征值/奇异值的完美排序（全序）放松为偏序结构，关键条件是基变换矩阵关于该偏序存在LU分解近似。在此框架下，利用偏序约束下的LU分解性质，重新推导并加强了原有的矩阵不等式。

Result: 获得了Ky Fan控制关系、Horn对数控制关系和von Neumann迹不等式的精细化版本。作为应用，给出了任意数量张量因子情形的可分离Ky Fan控制关系的简短证明，并将其推广到任意矩阵张量积之和的情形；还将结果应用于(反)对称幂的和以及Kronecker和的乘积的控制关系中。

Conclusion: 通过引入偏序对齐和LU分解近似的框架，经典矩阵控制不等式可以被显著加强和推广，这一方法具有广泛的应用前景，覆盖了张量积、对称幂、Kronecker和等多个重要矩阵分析领域。

Abstract: Several classical majorization relations for sums or products of matrices involve a majorizing vector of perfectly aligned eigenvalues or singular values. By relaxing the order of alignment to a partial order, we show that the majorization can be strengthened, provided the change-of-basis matrices admit an LU-approximation with respect to this partial order. In this way, we obtain refined versions of Ky Fan's majorization relations, Horn's log-majorization relation, and von Neumann's trace inequality. As an application, we give a short proof of the separable Ky Fan majorization relation for an arbitrary number of tensor factors and extend it to a sum of tensor products of arbitrary matrices. Further applications concern majorization relations for sums of (anti-)symmetric powers and for products of Kronecker sums.

</details>

### [89] [Nonlinear Fourier spectral signatures of rogue waves observed in Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27734)
*Zhihao Zhang,Yankai Huang,Tiantian Li,Denglong Wang,Jie Peng*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文利用非线性傅里叶变换（NFT）和Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，研究了BEC系统中两类一阶怪波的离散谱结构。揭示了高斯波包和Peregrine孤子事件均由离散孤子模的相干动力学支配，并提出了基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 怪波在连续背景上的形成机制已有调制不稳定性框架，但在BEC等零边界条件物质波系统中，怪波的形成机制和非线性谱结构尚不明确，亟需从可积系统角度进行谱分析。

Method: 基于聚焦非线性薛定谔方程的可积结构，采用非线性傅里叶变换（NFT）和Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，对高斯波包诱导的极端局域化事件和实验观测的Peregrine孤子进行非线性谱分析和Darboux重构。

Result: （1）高斯初态下，初始宽度增大会增加离散本征值数量，导致从基态孤子、束缚态向圣诞树状怪波结构转变；（2）Peregrine孤子中，局域扰动重塑离散谱构型和相位演化，使多个束缚孤子模相干聚焦；（3）揭示了高阶怪波的谱机制，并提出基于离散谱相位匹配的逆谱工程方法。

Conclusion: 在零边界条件的BEC物质波系统中，怪波形成由离散孤子模的相干动力学支配。该非线性谱视角为理解和控制怪波形成提供了新框架。

Abstract: Modulation instability provides an important framework for understanding rogue wave (RW) formation on continuous backgrounds. However, the formation mechanism and nonlinear spectral structures of RWs in Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions remain largely unexplored. Here, we employ the nonlinear Fourier transform (NFT), based on the integrable structure of the focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation and the Zakharov-Shabat scattering problem, to investigate two representative classes of first-order RWs in BEC systems. Through nonlinear spectral analysis and Darboux reconstruction, we demonstrate that both Gaussian-wave-packet-induced extreme localization events and experimentally observed Peregrine solitons are governed by the coherent dynamics of discrete soliton modes encoded in the nonlinear spectrum. For Gaussian initial states, increasing the initial width leads to an increasing number of discrete eigenvalues, resulting in a transition from fundamental solitons and bound states to Christmas-tree-like RW structures. For experimentally observed Peregrine solitons, localized perturbations reshape the discrete spectral configuration and phase evolution, enabling coherent focusing of multiple bound soliton modes. Furthermore, we reveal the spectral mechanism of higher-order RWs and propose an inverse spectral-engineering approach based on discrete-spectrum phase matching. Our results provide a nonlinear spectral perspective for understanding and controlling RW formation in matter-wave systems with vanishing boundary conditions.

</details>


### [90] [Mean-Field Theory of Chiral Active Model B: Arrested Coarsening and Chiral Fingering Instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27305)
*Kristian Blom,Uwe Thiele*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文从主方程出发，推导并分析了手性Ising模型的平均场理论，揭示了手性偏置如何导致各向异性粗化、矩形畴形态以及手性诱导的停滞粗化，并在连续极限下得到具有手性电流的非平衡活性场论，发现了手性指进不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为手性Ising模型建立系统的平均场理论框架，揭示微观手性偏置如何通过粗粒化过程在连续介质层面产生非平衡手性电流，并理解其对相粗化动力学和界面稳定性的影响。

Method: (1) 从2×2自旋块顺时针/逆时针旋转的主方程出发，导出空间分辨平均磁化强度的离散演化方程；(2) 在离散层面分析手性偏置对相粗化的影响；(3) 取连续极限得到活性场论（具有松弛Model-B型动力学+手性电流的结构）；(4) 对径向对称团簇进行线性稳定性分析，识别手性指进不稳定性。

Result: 离散层面：手性偏置导致畴的各向异性粗化，畴呈现近矩形形状，并最终出现手性诱导的停滞粗化。连续层面：粗粒化显式表明微观旋转偏置产生了局域在界面处的切向电流。线性稳定性分析揭示了手性指进不稳定性——界面的角向扰动被放大，最终导致径向不对称的旋转态或无序态。

Conclusion: 微观手性旋转偏置通过粗粒化在连续介质层面产生界面局域化的切向手性电流，使系统永久偏离平衡态；该电流不仅导致各向异性的畴形态和停滞粗化，还引发手性指进不稳定性，使径向对称团簇失稳并演化为旋转或无序状态。

Abstract: We derive and analyze a mean-field theory of the chiral Ising model recently introduced by Wang, Pietzonka, and Jülicher in "Edge Currents Shape Condensates in Chiral Active Matter", arXiv:2603.20064. Starting from the master equation for clockwise and counterclockwise rotations of 2x2 spin blocks, we first obtain spatially discrete evolution equations for the spatially resolved average magnetization. On this discrete level, we show that a chiral bias strongly affects phase coarsening: domains coarsen anisotropically, develop nearly rectangular shapes, and eventually display chirality-induced arrested coarsening. Taking the continuum limit of these equations yields an active field theory that has the structure of a relaxational Model-B-type dynamics supplemented by a chiral current that permanently drives the system out of equilibrium. The coarse graining explicitly shows how microscopic rotational bias generates tangential currents localized at interfaces. Using this continuum theory, we perform a linear stability analysis of radially symmetric clusters and identify a chiral fingering instability in which angular perturbations of the interface are amplified and eventually lead to radially asymmetric rotating states or disordered states.

</details>

### [91] [Bose-Einstein condensation and superfluidity on a fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27470)
*Vira Shyta,Flavio S. Nogueira,Ashley M. Cook*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对易（模糊）球面上的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）和超流性。作者发现非对易性有利于有序相，从而增强了BEC和超流性，使得临界温度高于普通对易球面S²的情况。特别值得注意的是，在大球极限下，模糊球面上的正常流体分数表现出温度的线性依赖关系（~T），而非通常二维系统中的~T³行为，这一线性关系让人联想到铜氧化物高温超导体中的Uemura定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根据Hohenberg定理，二维空间中在有限温度下不存在BEC，但超流性却可以出现。作者旨在探索紧凑二维空间上——特别是非对易（模糊）球面上——BEC与超流性的行为，考察非对易性引入的额外尺度如何影响有序相的热力学性质。

Method: 将标量玻色场提升为N×N矩阵，定义在非对易（模糊）球面上，其中维度N与非对易参数相关。分别分析了理想玻色气体和弱相互作用玻色气体中的BEC临界温度，并将其与对易球面S²的结果进行比较。进一步研究了弱相互作用玻色系统的超流响应。为处理超流中的涡旋，作者论证了即使在普通球面上涡旋的集体坐标也会诱导非对易性，并将涡旋缺陷的概念扩展到模糊球面上。最后通过超流密度计算，比较了模糊球面与对易球面在热力学极限下的差异。

Result: （1）非对易性有利于有序相，增强了BEC和超流性；（2）无论理想气体还是弱相互作用气体，模糊球面上BEC的临界温度均高于对易球面S²的情况；（3）在大球极限下，模糊球面上的正常流体分数呈现温度的线性依赖（~T），而对易球面S²上呈现通常二维系统的~T³行为；（4）这一线性依赖关系直接源于非对易性，与铜氧化物高温超导体中的Uemura定律类似。

Conclusion: 非对易性在热力学极限下具有实验上的可观测效应：模糊球面拥有不同于平面和对易球面S²的热力学极限。非对易性增强有序相的趋势，以及正常流体分数线性温度依赖这一与高温超导Uemura定律相似的特征，暗示非对易几何可能在凝聚态物理中具有重要的实验相关性。

Abstract: According to Hohenberg's theorem, Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) in two dimensions is impossible for any temperature $T>0$. By contrast, superfluidity does occur in two dimensions at finite temperatures; it emerges due to the breaking of Galilei invariance. Here we consider BEC and superfluidity on a compact two-dimensional space taking the form of a non-commutative ("fuzzy") sphere, where the scalar bosonic fields are promoted to $N\times N$ matrices. The dimension $N$ is related to the non-commutativity parameter of space and introduces an additional scale into the system. We find that non-commutativity favors ordered phases and so enhances BEC and superfluidity. We analyze BEC in ideal and weakly interacting Bose gases on a fuzzy sphere, finding in each case that the critical temperature of BEC is greater compared to that found in the case of a commutative sphere $S^2$. Then we investigate the superfluid response of weakly interacting Bose systems. To account for vortices in a superfluid, we show that, even on an ordinary sphere, the collective coordinates of vortices induce non-commutativity. With this in mind, we extend the definition of vortex defects to an inherently non-commutative sphere studied here, where the notion of a point is untenable. The non-commutativity is expected to be experimentally relevant to BEC and superfluidity since the fuzzy sphere has a thermodynamic limit distinct from the one defined over a plane, unlike the $S^2$ case. The significance of this difference is illustrated by the superfluid density calculation indicating that, in the large sphere limit, the normal fluid fraction on the fuzzy sphere yields a linear in $T$ dependence, while on a commutative $S^2$ it exhibits the usual two-dimensional $\sim T^3$ behavior. This linear dependence, arising directly from non-commutativity, is reminiscent of Uemura's law in cuprate high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>


### [92] [Observation of Moiré Time Crystal in Floquet-driven Rydberg Atomic Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27999)
*Shuai Shi,Dong-Yang Zhu,Yu Yang,Chu-Rong Pan,Jing-Wen Tang,Ya-Peng Zhang,Yan-Li Zhou,Wei-Tao Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Bang Liu,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文报道了首次实验实现的莫尔时间晶体，通过双频驱动场激发里德堡原子系统中的长程相互作用与耗散，产生了拍频梳状的莫尔图案，并绘制了相图，展示了其对激光失谐的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔时间晶体作为一种非平衡量子相，理论上由时间晶体内禀振荡与另一频率相干干涉形成，具有超长拍周期等独特性质，但此前尚无实验实现。本文旨在填补这一空白，在驱动量子多体系统中首次实验观测并表征莫尔时间晶体。

Method: 作者采用双色（bichromatic）驱动场施加于里德堡原子系统，利用两个不同频率的驱动之间的失配，结合长程里德堡相互作用和耗散效应，产生叠加亚谐波周期性与基频的拍频梳状莫尔图案。通过改变其中一个驱动频率，实验上绘制了系统相图，并测试了体系对激光失谐的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验成功观测到由两个失配驱动形成的莫尔图案，其频谱随驱动频率变化呈现交错特征。相图显示存在一个鲁棒区域，在此区域内莫尔时间秩序能够抵抗激光失谐扰动而持续存在。

Conclusion: 所报道的莫尔时间晶体不仅为探索涌现的慢-快动力学和合成时空对称性提供了可控平台，也为在驱动量子多体系统中构建复杂时间秩序开辟了新途径。

Abstract: A Moiré time crystal is a non-equilibrium quantum phase emerging from the coherent interference of two distinct frequencies, at least one being the intrinsic oscillation of a symmetry-broken time crystal. Its hallmark is an ultra-long beat period, reflecting a time-domain mapping of the Moiré fringes that arise from mismatched spatial lattices. However, to date, no experimental realization of such a Moiré time crystal has been reported. In this work, by applying a bichromatic driving field with two distinct frequencies, we demonstrate that the interplay between long-range Rydberg interactions and dissipation gives rise to a unique comb-like Moiré pattern characterized by a beat-note comb, which superimposes subharmonic periodicity and fundamental frequencies. This Moiré pattern formed by two mismatched drives is staggered in the spectrum as the frequency of one driver changes. We experimentally map the phase diagram of the system and identify a robust region where the Moiré temporal order persists against perturbations in laser detuning. The reported Moiré time crystal not only provides a controllable platform for exploring emergent slow-fast dynamics and synthetic space-time symmetries but also opens avenues for engineering complex temporal order in driven quantum many-body systems.

</details>

### [93] [PASS: Perturbation augmented space group structure sampling for transferable Fe-O machine learning interatomic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28000)
*Zixiong Wei,Fei Shuang,Poulumi Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出PASS方法生成高质量小晶胞数据集，结合ACE框架开发了首个可迁移的Fe-O体系机器学习原子间势，并通过多尺度验证展示了其在Fe氧化模拟中的准确性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-O体系具有复杂的结构和磁性，生成高质量的第一性原理训练数据集极具挑战性，而准确的原子模拟又依赖于可靠的原子间势，因此需要一种系统性的方法来开发可迁移的Fe-O机器学习原子间势。

Method: 提出Perturbation Augmented Space group structure Sampling (PASS)方法，生成包含少于10个原子的小晶胞结构的广泛代表性数据集；基于原子簇展开(ACE)框架，开发了首个可迁移的Fe-O体系机器学习原子间势(MLIP)。

Result: 通过体相、表面和界面性质的系统验证，ACE MLIP在纯Fe和Fe-O体系中均表现出良好的准确性和能力；在大规模Fe氧化模拟中成功复现了FeO-like结构的形成。

Conclusion: PASS方法能够产生准确且可迁移的MLIP，该势函数既能捕捉氧化物生长的反应复杂性，又在计算上适用于扩展体系，为Fe氧化原子模拟提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Accurate atomistic modelling of iron (Fe) oxidation requires a reliable interatomic potential, which necessitates an extensive and representative first-principles dataset for training the interatomic potential. However, Fe-oxygen (O) system is known for its structural and magnetic complexity, rendering the generation of high-quality dataset challenging. In this work, we propose the Perturbation Augmented Space group structure Sampling (PASS) method to generate extensive and representative dataset consisting of small-cell structures with less than 10 atoms. We present a systematic approach to developing a first of its kind transferable machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) for Fe-O system based on the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) framework. We thoroughly validate the accuracy and capability of the ACE MLIP across both pure Fe and Fe-O systems through bulk, surface, and interface properties. We showcase the formation of FeO-like structure in large-scale Fe oxidation simulation using the ACE MLIP. This work demonstrates that the PASS method yields an accurate and transferable MLIP which is capable of capturing the reactive complexity of oxide growth while remaining computationally practical for extended systems.

</details>


### [94] [Unconventional and Fragile Magnetic Exciton in a van der Waals Quantum Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27695)
*Kai-Xuan Zhang,Min Zhang,Minjae Kim,Yong-Hyun Kim,Junghyun Kim,Heejun Yang,Pyeongjae Park,Chaebin Kim,Mangesh Diware,Junik Hwang,Youjin Lee,Byeong-Gwan Cho,Hyeong-Do Kim,Tae-Yeong Koo,Chunhua Chen,Mingtao Li,Xujie Lü,Wenge Yang,Kee-Hoon Kim,Seung-Ho Baek,Hyeonsik Cheong,Sung-Keun Lee,Beom Hyun Kim,Christopher Lane,Jian-Xin Zhu,Zhaorong Yang,Young-Woo Son,Je-Geun Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用静水压作为调控手段，发现NiPS3中尖锐的磁激子光致发光峰在仅0.4 GPa下即被显著抑制、1.5 GPa下完全淬灭，且该明-暗转变不伴随磁性、晶体或电子结构重构，揭示磁激子的光学亮度来源于高阶关联机制而非化学无序或磁序弱化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是解答NiPS3磁激子为何在名义上自旋禁戒的跃迁中仍表现出极尖锐且光学明亮的特性，以及这种明亮性究竟是源于化学无序、磁序弱化、晶格修饰还是激子本身的内禀不稳定性——这些答案将对理论模型构成严格约束。

Method: 以静水压作为洁净、连续、可逆的原位调控参数，结合光致发光(PL)光谱、拉曼光谱、X射线吸收谱(XAS)、核磁共振(NMR)谱学以及第一性原理多体计算，系统追踪压力下磁激子的演化及相应的磁、晶格、电子结构变化。

Result: 0.4 GPa即显著抑制尖锐PL峰，1.5 GPa完全淬灭且可逆；该过程无磁性、晶体学或电子重构发生，尽管Néel温度有所升高。表明磁激子光学亮度独立于化学无序、晶格膨胀及磁序弱化，指向高阶关联机制。

Conclusion: NiPS3明亮磁激子由高阶关联机制（如激子配对、晶体场调控的自旋-轨道混合、对称性破缺）所主导，为纠缠磁激子在关联量子磁体中的未来检验提供了框架。

Abstract: The recently discovered magnetic exciton in the van der Waals (vdW) antiferromagnet NiPS3 exemplifies these phenomena, exhibiting several distinctive characteristics. Despite extensive investigation, much of its physics remains unresolved, with key questions about why the NiPS3 magnetic exciton is so sharp and optically bright despite the nominally spin-forbidden transition, posing significant challenges to a proper understanding and practical manipulation of the exciton. An urgent question is to what extent it is due to chemical disorder, magnetic weakening, lattice modification, or intrinsic instability of the bright exciton itself: answers to which will put stringent constraints on possible theoretical models. Here we address these questions using hydrostatic pressure as a clean, continuous, reversible, and in-situ tuning parameter. We find that the sharp photoluminescence peak is drastically suppressed by as little as 0.4 GPa and completely quenched by 1.5 GPa, with demonstrating its reversibility. Crucially, this bright-to-dark conversion occurs without magnetic, crystallographic, or electronic reconstruction despite an increase in the Neel temperature, as established by Raman, X-ray absorption, nuclear magnetic resonance spectroscopy, and first-principles many-body calculations. Our results demonstrate that the optical brightness of the magnetic exciton is independent of chemical disorder, lattice expansion, and weakening of magnetic order, indicating that a higher-order correlated mechanism governs the bright exciton. We further propose experimentally constrained microscopic scenarios involving exciton pairing, crystal-field-controlled spin-orbit mixing, and symmetry breaking, providing a framework for future tests of entangled magnetic exciton in correlated quantum magnets.

</details>


### [95] [Optimization of magneto-electric properties in Lead-free (x)Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 - (100-x)BaTiO3 based composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27717)
*Rajeev Dwivedi,Samanway Mohanta,Ashutosh Anand,Abhinash Tripathy,Najnin Bano,Dharmendra Kumar,Hemant Singh,R. Venkatesh,Sachin Gupta,Dinesh Kumar Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无铅多铁复合材料 (x)Co₁.₂Ti₀.₂Fe₁.₆O₄ - (100-x)BaTiO₃ 的合成与性能，发现30%铁氧体含量、1200°C烧结条件下可获得最高磁电耦合系数(~1.28 mV/cm·Oe)，揭示了成分与烧结工艺协同优化对提升磁电耦合的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发环境友好的无铅多铁复合材料，通过系统调控成分和烧结温度来优化磁电耦合性能，以满足新一代低功耗多功能器件（如非易失性存储器、磁场传感器、自旋电子器件等）的应用需求。

Method: 采用固相反应法合成(x)Co₁.₂Ti₀.₂Fe₁.₆O₄-(100-x)BaTiO₃ (x=10,20,30)系列复合材料；利用X射线衍射结合Rietveld精修确认相组成；通过显微结构分析评估致密化和晶粒生长；系统表征不同成分和烧结温度下的介电、铁电、磁性和磁电耦合性能。

Result: XRD与Rietveld精修证实四方相BaTiO₃与立方尖晶石相CTFO共存；高温烧结促进致密化和晶粒生长，增强两相间耦合；铁电测试显示因导电相漏导导致损耗型P-E回线；磁性随铁氧体含量增加而增强；30CTFO-70BTO在1200°C烧结时获得最高磁电系数~1.28 mV/cm·Oe，归因于磁致伸缩与压电响应的最优平衡及界面应变传递的改善。

Conclusion: 同时优化掺杂改性成分与烧结条件是提升块体多铁复合材料磁电耦合性能的关键策略，所制备的无铅复合材料在下一代低功耗多功能器件（如FeRAM/MRAM、磁场传感器、自旋电子器件和致动器）中具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a systematic study of lead-free multiferroic composites of (x)Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 - (100-x)BaTiO3 (x = 10, 20, 30), which were synthesized by a solid-state reaction method to investigate the effects of composition and sintering temperature on their structural , electrical, magnetic, and magnetoelectric (ME) properties. X-ray diffraction along with Rietveld refinement confirms the coexistence of tetragonal BaTiO3 (BTO) and cubic spinel Co1.2Ti0.2Fe1.6O4 (CTFO) phases. Microstructural analysis shows that densification and grain growth are better at higher sintering temperatures, leading to better coupling between the two phases. Dielectric and ferroelectric studies indicate lossy polarization-electric field (P-E) behaviour due to leakage from the conductive phase, while magnetic properties show increased magnetization with increasing ferrite content. All composites exhibit ME coefficients, which depend on the composition and sintering conditions; the highest ME coefficient (~1.28 mV/cm.Oe) was observed for the 30CTFO - 70BTO composite sintered at 1200 °C. This improvement is due to the optimal balance between magnetostrictive and piezoelectric responses and improved interfacial strain transfer. These results demonstrate that simultaneous optimization of dopant-modified composition and sintering conditions is essential for achieving improved magnetoelectric coupling in bulk multiferroic composites. Moreover, the results demonstrate the potential of lead-free composites for multifunctional device applications in next-generation, low-power technologies, including high-density non-volatile memory (e.g. FeRAM/MRAM), magnetic field sensors, spintronic devices, and actuators.

</details>


### [96] [Intertwined magnetoresistance and Hall multifunctionality in a non-coplanar magnetic Weyl semimetal DyB4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28283)
*Long Chen,Yulin Shen,Songxue Chi,Seunghoon Song,Yang Zhang,Jian Liu,Haidong Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DyB4是一种磁性Weyl半金属候选材料，同时展现出极大磁电阻、手性反常负磁电阻、大反常霍尔电导和拓扑霍尔效应四重多功能性，源于其巡游载流子与局域4f磁矩的弱耦合协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常磁输运响应的多种机制（轨道运动、动量空间拓扑、实空间自旋手性）通常难以在单一材料中共存，因为其底层物理要求往往相互竞争。本文旨在寻找一种能将高迁移率线性色散能带与可调谐非共面磁性相结合的材料平台。

Method: 以受挫稀土四硼化物DyB4为研究对象，综合运用中子衍射解析其磁场可调磁结构（包括非共面自旋构型和PT对称破缺相），结合第一性原理计算揭示其陡峭线性色散和场致Weyl点，并通过磁输运测量系统表征其多重量子输运现象。

Result: 实现了四重磁输运多功能性的罕见组合：(1)极大磁电阻；(2)类手性反常负磁电阻；(3)由内禀Berry曲率与斜散射协同贡献的大反常霍尔电导；(4)标量自旋手性驱动的拓扑霍尔效应。这些现象源于巡游载流子与局域4f磁矩各自独立又弱耦合的角色分工。

Conclusion: DyB4作为一个4f电子平台，成功关联了轨道输运、动量空间Berry曲率和实空间自旋手性，为多功能磁性拓扑材料的设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Anomalous magneto-transport responses provide complementary probes of orbital motion, momentum-space topology, and real-space spin chirality, yet their integration into a single material remains rare because their underlying requirements often compete. A promising materials-design strategy is to realize a magnetic Weyl semimetal that combines linearly dispersive high-mobility bands with tunable non-coplanar magnetism while limiting spin-dependent scattering. Here we identify DyB4, a frustrated rare-earth tetraboride, as a magnetic Weyl semimetal candidate that embodies this strategy and hosts intertwined magnetoresistance and Hall multifunctionality. Neutron diffraction reveals a sequence of field-tunable magnetic states, including non-coplanar spin configurations and PT-symmetry-broken phases. First-principles calculations identify steep linear dispersions and field-induced Weyl points near the Fermi level. Magneto-transport measurements establish a rare fourfold combination of extremely large magnetoresistance, chiral-anomaly-like negative magnetoresistance, large anomalous Hall conductivity arising from cooperative intrinsic Berry curvature and skew scattering, and scalar-spin-chirality-driven topological Hall responses. This multifunctionality arises from the distinct yet weakly coupled roles of itinerant carriers and localized 4f moments, which enable high-mobility transport, field-induced Weyl topology, and non-coplanar magnetism. DyB4 therefore provides a 4f-electron platform for correlating orbital transport, momentum-space Berry curvature, and real-space spin chirality, suggesting a route toward multifunctional magnetic topological materials.

</details>


### [97] [High-Field EPR/ENDOR of N/Be Centers for Defect Engineering in 6H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27862)
*Yuliya Ermakova,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Margarita Sadovnikova,Fadis Murzakhanov,George Mamin,Sergey Nagalyuk,Evgeny Mokhov,Marat Gafurov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用 W 波段脉冲 EPR 和 ENDOR 技术，系统研究了氮和铍共掺杂 6H-SiC 单晶中的施主-受主自旋缺陷，揭示了其超精细结构、自旋弛豫特性及离域化自旋密度分布，验证了双杂质功能共掺杂的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC 在极端条件电子学和量子技术中具有重要应用前景，但此前对具有不同功能角色的双杂质共掺杂体系中自旋缺陷的微观结构和量子相干特性尚缺乏深入理解，需要高分辨率谱学表征。

Method: 对氮和铍（浓度均为 10¹⁸ cm⁻³）共掺杂的 6H-SiC 单晶进行 W 波段（94 GHz；B=3.4 T）连续波和脉冲 EPR 及 ENDOR 测量，结合 TRIPLE 共振谱分析超精细耦合与核自旋子空间耦合。

Result: 脉冲 EPR 识别出不同晶格位置的氮施主和铍受主，获得了其相位相干时间和自旋-晶格弛豫时间；ENDOR 解析了缺陷电子与周围 Si、C 原子核（含远距离配位层）的超精细相互作用，揭示了超胞内高度离域化的自旋密度分布；TRIPLE 谱证实了不同配位层的耦合核自旋子空间。

Conclusion: 在 6H-SiC 中成功实现氮和铍双杂质共掺杂，在保持晶格结构特征的同时赋予了不同的功能角色，为 SiC 基量子器件的缺陷工程设计提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Silicon carbide (SiC) in its various structural modifications is widely used in power semiconductor electronics, operating under extreme conditions of high temperature, high voltage, and intense radiation. The discovery of spin defects (S>0) with unique optical and coherent properties has further positioned SiC as a promising platform for quantum technologies. Here, we investigate a 6H-SiC single crystal co-doped with nitrogen and beryllium at concentrations of 1018 cm-3, using continuous-wave and pulsed electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) and electron-nuclear double resonance (ENDOR). To enhance spectral resolution, experiments were conducted in the W-band (94 GHz; B = 3.4 T). Pulsed EPR identified nitrogen donors and beryllium acceptors in various lattice positions, allowing for the determination of their phase coherence and spin-lattice relaxation times. ENDOR measurements elucidated the electron-nuclear interactions with the local silicon and carbon environment, including distant coordination spheres. The observed hyperfine structures indicated highly delocalized spin density within the supercell. The TRIPLE resonance spectra verify coupled nuclear spin subspaces from different coordination spheres due to defect spin density. These results demonstrate the feasibility of incorporating dual impurities with distinct functional roles while preserving the crystal lattice`s structural features.

</details>

### [98] [Geometry-Induced Domain-Wall Pinning and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Asymmetry in Nominally One-Dimensional Rydberg Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27358)
*Elijah Pelofske,Frank Barrows,Cristiano Nisoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在QuEra的中性里德堡原子量子计算机Aquila上，通过准绝热演化对一维模型进行实验，发现由于范德华相互作用与里德堡阻塞效应的耦合，导致了纯1D伊辛模型未预测的现象，如畴壁钉扎和Z₂对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中性里德堡原子量子处理器上，实际物理相互作用（范德华力、里德堡阻塞）如何导致超出理想化1D伊辛模型的新奇量子现象，尤其是在非平凡几何构型下的表现。

Method: 在QuEra的Aquila量子计算机上，使用准绝热演化方法，对两种不同几何构型的一维模型进行实验：一个包含33个原子的闭合三角形轮廓，以及一个包含47个原子且带有人工原子空位的正方形轮廓。通过硬件微调来平衡测量统计量，使其接近零净磁化强度。

Result: 发现纯1D伊辛模型未预测的效应：(1) 畴壁钉扎现象；(2) 由于范德华相互作用与里德堡阻塞的耦合导致Z₂对称性破缺。特别是，在原子空位周围和正方形轮廓顶点处的原子强烈偏好里德堡态，导致有效模型在空位处形成铁磁键。

Conclusion: 中性里德堡原子处理器的2D平面几何限制与实际长程相互作用，使得即使设计为一维的模型也会涌现出超越理想1D伊辛模型的丰富物理现象，这对基于该平台的量子模拟具有重要的实验启示。

Abstract: We perform experiments on QuEra's neutral-Rydberg-atom Aquila quantum computer, using quasi-adiabatic evolution on 1-dimensional models. We use hardware fine-tuning to balance the measurement statistics close to zero net magnetization, in two different geometric outlines: one closed triangular model with 33 atoms, and one 47-atom square with an engineered atom vacancy. The nature of this processor restricts the geometry of the atoms that can be programmed to a 2D plane. We report effects not predicted by the pure 1D Ising model, such as pinning of domain walls and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry breaking due to the interplay between van der Waals interactions and the Rydberg blockade. In particular, atoms around the atom vacancy and at the vertices of the 1-dimensional square-outline model strongly prefer the Rydberg state, leading to the effective model having a ferromagnetic bond across the vacancy.

</details>


### [99] [Designing tight frames for quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27247)
*Luis Quezada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了调和紧框架（harmonic tight frames）在量子测量中的实现，利用群表示论和交换群分类定理对其进行刻画，研究了框架元素作为量子态的可分离性与最大纠缠条件，并给出了利用Fourier矩阵和置换矩阵在量子电路中实现循环群调和框架作为POVM的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量设计（尤其是POVM）在量子计算中至关重要，而调和紧框架作为一种特殊量子测量，需要系统的理论工具来刻画其结构、可分离性以及实际电路实现方案。

Method: 采用群表示论方法，从不可约表示出发，利用对称性定义群框架（group frames）作为群酉作用下的轨道；通过交换群分类定理及其不可约表示的特征标分析调和框架；研究纯态可分离性的充要条件；运用Naimark定理设计POVM；利用Fourier矩阵与置换矩阵构造量子电路。

Result: 基于交换群结构定理给出了调和框架的完整刻画及其可分离性的充要条件；针对二分系统，得到了子系统维度上最大纠缠的必要条件；成功构造了利用Fourier矩阵和置换矩阵实现循环群调和框架作为POVM的量子电路。

Conclusion: 将理论成果成功应用于量子计算机的简单实例，并提出了改进电路设计以及将方案推广至所有调和框架的未来研究方向。

Abstract: The aim of this thesis is to explore the implementation of a special kind of quantum measurements, so called harmonic tight frames. To achieve this goal, representation theory is addressed, emphasizing its construction from irreducible representations and the relations they satisfy. These tools simplify the study due to the existence of symmetries, leading to the concept of group frames, defined as orbits under the unitary action of a group and characterized by their symmetry groups. Among the various groups, the simplest are the abelian ones, giving rise to harmonic frames, which are analyzed through the classification theorem of abelian groups and the characters of their irreducible representations. In the quantum mechanics context, the frame elements can be interpreted as pure states of a system. Therefore, the necessary and sufficient conditions for separability are explored, as separable states are easier to implement due to their local nature. Alternatively, when viewing frames as measurements, the concept of POVMs and Naimark's theorem are studied as key tools for designing measurements in quantum computers. Using this knowledge, a characterization of the harmonic frames is given from the abelian group structure theorem, which allows obtaining a necessary and sufficient condition for their separability. The conditions of maximum entanglement of these states for bipartite systems are also studied, obtaining a necessary condition on the dimensions of the subsystems. Finally, a quantum circuit is obtained that allows implementing the harmonic frames associated to cyclic groups as POVMs using a Fourier matrix and a permutation matrix. We conclude by giving simple examples where the results are applied to quantum computers and proposing future avenues of research that could serve to improve the circuit design and extend it to the case of all harmonic frames.

</details>


### [100] [Is the H Atom Surrounded by A Cloud of Virtual Quanta Due to the Lamb Shift?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27257)
*G. Jordan Maclay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Feynman和Power的真空能量观点，结合群论计算，推导出氢原子兰姆位移中每个频率真空涨落所对应的空间区域大小。结果表明，基态氢原子周围存在一个宏观尺度的正真空能量区域，其半径与波长成正比，对低能涨落可达宏观尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兰姆位移是原子物理中的基本相互作用，传统上有多种计算方法。作者旨在从Feynman-Power的真空能量变化视角出发，定量描述每个频率的真空涨落对兰姆位移贡献的空间分布，从而揭示原子周围真空能量区域的几何特征。

Method: 采用Feynman和Power的方法，将兰姆位移等同于氢原子存在引起的折射率变化所导致的真空能量改变；结合群论计算，导出每个频率ω的真空涨落对兰姆位移的贡献，进而得到该频率下真空能量区域的空间尺度表达式。

Result: 基态氢原子周围存在正真空能量区域，低频率下延伸至远超出原子尺度。对于能量E=ℏω小于1 eV的涨落，正能区半径约为14.4/E Å；对于波长λ的真空涨落，半径为(α/2π)λ。能量-时间不确定关系预测的最大可能半径比该值大1/(4α)倍。

Conclusion: 兰姆位移可被理解为原子周围一个稳态虚量子云的表现；该虚量子云对长波涨落具有宏观尺度，揭示了量子真空涨落与原子相互作用的非局域特性。

Abstract: \abstract{The Lamb shift, one of the most fundamental interactions in atomic physics, arises from the interaction of H atoms with the electromagnetic fluctuations of the quantum vacuum. The energy shift has been computed in a variety of ways. The energy shift, as Feynman and Power demonstrated, equals the change in the vacuum energy in the volume containing the H atoms due to the change in the index of refraction arising from the presence of the H atoms. By using this result and a group theoretical calculation of the contribution to the Lamb shift from each frequency of the vacuum fluctuations, we can obtain an expression for the size of the region of vacuum energy for each frequency \texorpdfstring{$ω$}{ω} around the H atom due to the Lamb shift. The ground state atom is surrounded by a region of positive vacuum energy that extends well beyond the atom for low frequencies. This region can be described as a steady state cloud of virtual quanta. For energies \texorpdfstring{$E=\hbarω$}{E=hbar omega} eV less than 1 eV, the radius of the positive energy region is approximately 14. 4/E Angstroms. For a vacuum fluctuation of wavelength \texorpdfstring{$λ$}{lambda} the radius is \texorpdfstring{$(α/2π)λ$}{(alpha/2pi) lambda}. Thus, for long wavelengths, the region has macroscopic dimensions. The energy-time Uncertainty Relation predicts a maximum possible radius that is larger than this by a factor of \texorpdfstring{$1/ 4α$}{1/(4 alpha)}.}
  \keyword{Bethe; radiative shift; shift spectral density; spectral volume; vacuum fluctuations; vacuum field; Lamb shift; QED; energy field, renormalization, zero point fluctuations; hydrogen atom}.

</details>


### [101] [LLM-Guided Initialization for Accelerated Hybrid Quantum-Classical Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27262)
*Riza Alaudin Syah,Irwan Alnarus Kautsar,Haza Nuzly Bin Abdull Hamed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了AdaInit方法——利用大语言模型为参数化量子电路生成初始参数，以缓解贫瘠高原问题。在乳腺X线影像分类任务上，AdaInit相比随机初始化实现了梯度方差提升14.6倍、收敛速度加快160倍，并保持同等准确率，验证了其在医学影像场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法中广泛存在的贫瘠高原现象（barren plateaus）导致代价函数梯度随电路深度增加而指数衰减，严重削弱参数化量子电路的可训练性。现有初始化策略缺乏对参数空间几何结构的利用，因此需要探索更智能的初始化方法，将优化器引导至可训练区域。

Method: 作者采用Zhuang和Cunningham提出的AdaInit（自适应初始化）的简化单次查询变体，结合NVIDIA CUDA-Q的GPU加速仿真。核心流程为：使用大语言模型为量子神经网络生成初始参数，将其应用于DMR-IR乳腺X线影像数据集的二分类任务。同时，作者从参数化电路景观几何的角度提供了理论分析。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) AdaInit的梯度方差为0.0095，是随机初始化（0.0006）的14.6倍；(2) 收敛时间从176秒降至1.1秒，加速约160倍；(3) 分类准确率均为61.4%，未损失性能；(4) 单次LLM查询即可产生有信息量的参数，无需迭代精炼。

Conclusion: AdaInit在医学影像场景中得到验证，证明了LLM引导初始化能够将优化器置于参数空间的可训练区域，有效缓解贫瘠高原问题。该方法与GPU加速量子后端兼容良好，以极低开销提供了切实的加速效果，为提升变分量子算法的可训练性提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms often encounter barren plateaus, where cost gradients decay rapidly with increasing circuit depth, undermining the trainability of parameterized quantum circuits. This paper evaluates AdaInit (Adaptive Initialization), proposed by Zhuang and Cunningham, which uses large language models to propose initial parameters for quantum neural networks. We study a simplified single-query AdaInit variant paired with GPU-accelerated simulation in NVIDIA CUDA-Q and apply it to binary classification on the DMR-IR mammography dataset. AdaInit delivers 14.6 times higher gradient variance at initialization than random initialization (0.0095 vs. 0.0006), producing 160 times faster convergence (1.1s vs. 176 s) while maintaining the same classification accuracy of 61.4 percent. We provide theoretical analysis grounded in the geometry of parameterized circuit landscapes and show empirically that LLM-guided initialization places the optimizer in trainable regions of parameter space. Beyond performance, our results indicate that a single LLM query can yield informative parameters without iterative refinement, suggesting a low-overhead path to improved trainability. The findings validate AdaInit in a medical imaging setting and demonstrate its compatibility with GPU-accelerated quantum backends for practical speedups.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum State Discrimination With Stabilizer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27339)
*Benjamin Stratton,Santiago Zamora,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在稳定子电路（固定和自适应）测量约束下的最小误差量子态区分问题，证明固定稳定子电路不增加区分能力而自适应电路可以，推导了单量子比特系统下两类电路的成功概率解析表达式，并将非稳定子性纳入测量算符，最终应用于量子随机接入码和酉合成保真度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态区分的成功概率既取决于被区分的态，也取决于允许的测量方式。物理上受限制的测量（如稳定子电路可实现的测量）在实际量子信息处理中普遍存在，但在此约束下的量子态区分理论尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者首先分析固定稳定子电路和自适应稳定子电路在任意稳定子态辅助下的区分能力，证明固定电路无额外增益而自适应电路有。然后聚焦单量子比特系统，推导任意（非稳定子）辅助态下两类电路成功概率的解析表达式。进一步，将非稳定子性直接纳入测量算符，将优化问题表述为半定规划（SDP），并推导稳定子极限与Helstrom极限之间的解析插值界。最后应用于量子随机接入码和有限魔法态数量下的酉合成保真度。

Result: （1）固定稳定子电路即使辅以任意稳定子态辅助也不增加区分能力，而自适应电路可以；（2）单量子比特下两类电路的成功概率解析表达式揭示了二者性能差距持续存在；（3）非稳定子测量算符情形下得到了SDP公式及稳定子-Helstrom极限间的解析界；（4）在量子随机接入码和魔法态辅助酉合成中展示了应用。

Conclusion: 稳定子电路架构下的测量约束对量子态区分能力有本质限制：固定电路无法超越稳定子测量，而自适应电路可以突破。非稳定子性（魔法）资源的引入能平滑插值于稳定子极限与Helstrom极限之间，这为理解量子资源理论与量子操作约束之间的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The task of quantum state discrimination provides an operational characterization of distinguishability and plays a central role in quantum information science. Since the achievable success probability in quantum state discrimination depends both on the states being discriminated and on the allowed measurements, it is natural to study discrimination under physically motivated measurement constraints. Here, we investigate minimum-error quantum state discrimination under measurements implementable by both fixed and adaptive stabilizer circuits. Given arbitrary stabilizer-state ancillas, we show that fixed stabilizer circuits provide no additional discrimination power, whereas adaptive circuits do. Then, focusing on single-qubit systems, we derive analytical expressions for the success probability across both circuit classes when supplied with arbitrary (non-stabilizer) ancillas, showing that the performance gap between fixed and adaptive circuits persists. We further consider discrimination with non-stabilizerness incorporated directly into the measurement operators. Here, the optimization is formulated as a semidefinite program, and analytical bounds interpolating between the stabilizer and Helstrom limits for qubits are derived. Finally, we illustrate applications in quantum random access codes and bounds on unitary synthesis fidelity with a finite number of magic states.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum vs Classical Erasure: Equal Bounds but Unequal Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27341)
*Jan Neuser,Jake Xuereb,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Marcus Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 统一分析了有限资源下经典与量子信息擦除的热力学：在理想极限下两者擦除成本相同，但在实际有限资源约束下，达到同等擦除质量的量子系统需要更多控制、更大能隙和更长操作时间，经典系统具有根本性的热力学优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landauer界限虽适用于经典与量子系统，但仅在无限资源的理想极限下可饱和。实际有限资源擦除方案远未达到该界限，且经典与量子系统在实践中的差异缺乏统一的第一性原理理解。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法，统一描述有限资源下经典与量子系统的信息擦除过程；通过证明理想极限下经典与量子擦除成本等价，并对比两者在有限控制、能隙和操作时间约束下的实际表现，导出权衡关系。

Result: 在理想极限下，量子与经典比特擦除成本相同；但在实际实现中，达到同等擦除质量时量子系统需要更强的控制能力、更大的可及能隙和更长的操作时间；经典协议在更弱的约束下即可实现量子协议同等的擦除效果，并揭示了相应的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 经典系统在有限资源信息擦除中享有若干基本热力学优势，这解释了为何实际擦除方案无法达到Landauer界限。

Abstract: Irreversibility has a fundamental thermodynamic cost, erasing information inevitably generates heat. This connection is quantified by the Landauer bound, which gives the minimum dissipation needed to erase a single bit of information. While this bound applies in both classical and quantum settings, it is saturated only in idealised limits of infinite resources. Here, we provide a unified first principles description of finite-resource erasure in both classical and quantum systems. We begin by proving the communal folklore that in the idealised regime the erasure cost of a bit encoded in a quantum or classical system is the same. Despite this, we show that their practical implementation differs substantially: achieving comparable erasure quality in quantum systems requires more control, larger accessible energy gaps and longer operation times. Classical protocols can achieve the erasure of a comparable quantum protocol under far weaker constraints which we expose in trade-off relations. Our results explain why practical erasure schemes fall short of Landauer's bound and show that classical systems enjoy several fundamental thermodynamic advantages.

</details>


### [104] [Restrictions on non-Clifford fault tolerance and ruling out beyond-SQL quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27342)
*Constantin Cedillo Vayson de Pradenne,Ishaan Kannan,Harald Putterman,Jordan Cotler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了稳定子码中横向非Clifford逻辑门的一系列限制，并将其应用于量子计量学。核心结果包括：距离d≥3的稳定子码若支持Clifford层级第D层的横向逻辑门，则生成元必含权重至少为2^D的校验；横向小角度旋转的逻辑作用要求存在权重Ω(1/(n|θ|²))的不可约稳定子；推广的计量学no-go定理表明，恒定强度的信号对齐噪声排除了超越SQL的任何渐近优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学理论上可实现超越标准量子极限(SQL)的二次加速，但信号对齐噪声在实际条件下会消除这一优势。通过量子编码使物理信号横向作用于逻辑门，是绕过已知no-go结果的一条潜在路径。因此，理解横向非Clifford门的限制对量子计量学和容错量子计算都至关重要。

Method: 本文采用稳定子码理论和Clifford层级框架进行分析。首先针对支持第D层横向逻辑门的距离d≥3稳定子码，证明其生成元集合中必须存在高权重校验。其次，分析横向单比特小角度旋转的逻辑作用条件，建立旋转角度与不可约稳定子权重之间的定量关系。最后，在不依赖量子Cramér-Rao界的更一般设定下，证明信号对齐噪声的广义计量学no-go定理，覆盖偏倚估计、非稳定子编码、量子存储、中间测量与自适应控制等情形。

Result: （1）对距离d≥3且支持Clifford层级第D层横向逻辑门的任意稳定子码，每个稳定子生成元集合必含权重≥2^D的校验；r层级联实现满足r ≤ ⌊log₂(n/D)⌋，迫使r=1，排除了超越SQL计量中的级联方案。（2）横向单比特小角度旋转θ仅在校验包含权重Ω(1/(n|θ|²))的不可约稳定子时才能在n比特码上诱导非平凡逻辑作用；低于此权重的单比特错误会与所有稳定子或逻辑Pauli对易，仅被高权重校验检测，其伴随式无法从低权重正规子测量中容错重构。由于超越SQL的横向传感要求|θ|=o(n⁻¹/²)，伴随式提取所需校验权重随n发散。（3）广义no-go定理：恒定强度信号对齐噪声排除了AC或DC传感中超越SQL的任何渐近优势，即使使用偏倚估计、非稳定子或近似编码、量子存储、中间测量或自适应控制。

Conclusion: 横向非Clifford门的严格限制使得通过量子编码实现超越SQL的鲁棒量子计量面临根本性障碍。稳定子码中高权重校验的必要性直接阻碍了容错伴随式提取，且级联结构在大规模传感中不可行。更一般的no-go定理进一步表明，信号对齐噪声对量子计量优势的破坏在极其宽松的条件下依然成立，揭示了这一限制的普适性与深刻性。

Abstract: Quantum metrology promises a quadratic speedup over the standard quantum limit (SQL), but signal-aligned noise is expected to preclude this advantage in realistic settings. A potential route around known no-go results is to encode the sensors in a quantum code where the physical signal acts transversally as a logical gate. Understanding restrictions on transversal non-Clifford gates is therefore central to both quantum metrology and fault-tolerant quantum computation. Here, we prove such restrictions and apply them to transversal sensing. For any stabilizer code of distance $d\ge 3$ supporting a transversal logical action in level $D$ of the Clifford hierarchy, every stabilizer generating set must contain a check of weight at least $2^D$. Moreover, any $r$-level concatenated realization satisfies $r\leq \lfloor \log_2 n/D\rfloor$, forcing $r=1$ and ruling out concatenation when applied to beyond-SQL metrology. We then show that transversal single-qubit rotations by a small angle $θ$ can only induce a nontrivial logical action on an $n$-qubit code if its checks include irreducible stabilizers of weight $Ω(1/(n|θ|^2))$. Here, many single-qubit errors commute with every stabilizer or logical Pauli below this weight and are only detected by a high-weight check, so their syndromes cannot be fault-tolerantly reconstructed from low-weight normalizer measurements. Since beyond-SQL transversal sensing requires $|θ| = o(n^{-1/2})$, the weight of checks required for syndrome extraction diverges with $n$. Finally, we prove a broader metrological no-go theorem that avoids the assumptions of the quantum Cramér-Rao bound: constant-strength signal-aligned noise rules out any asymptotic advantage over the SQL in AC or DC sensing, even with biased estimators, nonstabilizer or approximate encodings, quantum memory, intermediate measurements, or adaptive control.

</details>


### [105] [Area-Information Trade-Offs in Acceleration Radiation from Atoms Falling into Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27388)
*Yusef Maleki,Gustavo Valdivia-Mera,Carlos R. Ordonez,Horacio E. Camblong,Marlan O. Scully*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在视界增亮加速辐射（HBAR）通道中建立了信息处理的几何理论，将辐射视界面积变化作为辐射场所携信息的熵预算，推导出面积-代价定律、Fano不等式约束下的可靠性条件以及Fisher信息速度极限，最终确立了联系黑洞热力学、信息几何与量子信息的"比特每面积"原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黑洞热力学中的视界面积极限与量子信息理论相统一，为HBAR通道中的信息处理提供基于辐射视界面面积变化的熵预算框架，从而建立信息容量、可靠性与统计演化之间的定量联系。

Method: 基于视界附近原子-场相互作用的量子光学描述及HBAR热力学对应关系，在近稳态热饱和区域推导面积-代价定律；利用Fano不等式将译码错误概率转化为面积需求；引入Fisher信息速度极限约束辐射场的统计演化，并给出关联生成所需时间的下界。

Result: 得出辐射场可获取的经典信息及其与环境之间的互信息均受限于辐射视界面积预算；Fano不等式给出了给定译码错误概率下的面积需求；Fisher信息速度极限界定了辐射场统计演化的速率，并提供了关联生成的最小持续时间。

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一的"比特每面积"原理，将黑洞热力学、信息几何与量子信息三者贯穿于HBAR框架之中，为视界辐射的信息处理能力提供了基本的几何-热力学约束。

Abstract: We develop a geometric theory of information processing in the Horizon-brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR) channel, in which the radiative horizon-area change provides an entropy budget for the information carried by the radiation field. Building on the quantum-optical description of atom--field interactions near the horizon and the resulting HBAR thermodynamic correspondence, we derive area-cost laws in the near-steady, thermally saturated regime. The accessible classical information and the mutual information generated between the radiation field and its environment are bounded by the associated radiative horizon-area budget. Reliability is incorporated through Fano's inequality, which translates a prescribed decoding error probability into an area requirement. We further derive Fisher-information speed limits that constrain the statistical evolution of the radiation field and place a lower bound on the duration required for correlation generation. Together, these results establish a bits-per-area principle linking black-hole thermodynamics, information geometry, and quantum information in the HBAR framework.

</details>


### [106] [Bosonic quantum communication beyond the thermal threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27449)
*Francesco Anna Mele,Giuseppe Catalano,Marco Fanizza,Vittorio Giovannetti,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 波色热衰减器的量子容量长期未知。本文证明经典的单模高斯态下界恰为所有单模高斯态的上确界，但发现非高斯态可实现严格更优的相干信息，从而在信道非反可退化但高斯优化相干信息为零的参数区域证明了量子容量的正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波色热衰减器的量子容量由相干信息的正则化给出，但Holevo-Werner于1999年建立的热态下界是否可超越一直悬而未决。研究者试图突破高斯态框架，在高噪声区域探索能否实现正量子容量。

Method: 首先从理论上证明单模高斯态的相干信息上确界恰为Holevo-Werner下界；然后构造显式的秩二非高斯输入态（仅支持六个Fock能级），解析证明其相干信息为正；进一步通过数值优化搜索更优的非高斯态族。

Result: 在ν=1、η=0.8处，显式非高斯态给出至少4.7×10⁻⁴量子比特/信道使用的相干信息；数值优化可达8.4×10⁻³。在ν=1时，非高斯输入将量子容量正性下推至η=0.7841，远超反可退化阈值η≤0.75。

Conclusion: 非高斯态可严格超越高斯态在波色热衰减器中的相干信息表现，开辟了高噪声区域的波色量子通信新可能，修正了长期以来的高斯最优性直觉。

Abstract: The quantum capacity of the bosonic thermal attenuator, which is given by the regularization of its coherent information, is unknown. The seminal work of Holevo and Werner established in 1999 the standard one-use lower bound obtained from input thermal states. We first prove that this long-standing lower bound is the exact supremum over all single-mode Gaussian states and then show that, crucially, a non-Gaussian state can do better. As a consequence, we prove positivity of the quantum capacity in a parameter region where the channel is not antidegradable, yet its coherent information optimized over single-mode Gaussian states vanishes. For example, with one thermal photon in the environment and at transmissivity $η=0.8$, the coherent information is non-positive for every single-mode Gaussian input. We give an explicit rank-two non-Gaussian state, supported on only six Fock levels, whose coherent information is certified to be at least $4.7\times10^{-4}$ qubits per channel use. This short witness is far from numerically optimal: a numerical optimization over fixed non-Gaussian families reaches at least $8.4\times 10^{-3}$ qubits per channel use at the same point. More generally, at $ν=1$, using non-Gaussian inputs we certify positivity of the coherent information, and therefore of the quantum capacity, down to $η=0.7841$; by contrast, the channel is antidegradable, and hence has zero quantum capacity, for $η\leq0.75$. Overall, our work identifies new high-noise regimes in which bosonic quantum communication is possible.

</details>


### [107] [Coherent excitation of a two-state system by a Lorentzian field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27466)
*Aida Shiroyan,Genko S. Vasilev,Nikolay V. Vitanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究洛伦兹包络脉冲驱动二能级系统的相干激发，利用合流Heun函数得到精确解，并通过Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas方法给出弱耦合和强耦合极限下的渐近行为，揭示强耦合下的功率窄化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索洛伦兹时间包络脉冲对二能级量子系统的相干激发行为，特别是缺乏精确解析解情况下，需要发展系统的渐近分析方法来描述跃迁概率在弱耦合与强耦合区域的物理特性。

Method: 首先建立常载波频率洛伦兹包络场驱动的二能级系统微分方程，发现其可用合流Heun函数（confluent Heun functions）精确局部表示。在此基础上，基于两个相关复跃迁点及其干涉，构建Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas（DDP）描述框架来近似跃迁概率。进而分别在弱耦合和强耦合极限下直接分析DDP作用量，导出渐近表达式。

Result: 获得了跃迁概率在弱耦合和强耦合极限下的显式渐近表达式，涵盖振荡相位、振荡包络、远失谐线型以及近共振行为。在强耦合渐近下，发现共振奇π脉冲的线宽与峰值Rabi频率的倒数成正比，即展现出洛伦兹脉冲特有的功率窄化（power-narrowing）特征。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹包络脉冲驱动的二能级系统可借助合流Heun函数与DDP方法得到完整的渐近解析描述，强耦合下的功率窄化行为是洛伦兹脉冲形状的直接体现，该理论框架为理解此类脉冲的相干激发动力学提供了系统的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the coherent excitation of a two-state quantum system by a field with a Lorentzian temporal envelope and constant carrier frequency. The associated differential equation admits an exact local representation in terms of confluent Heun functions. For the transition probability we develop a Dykhne-Davis-Pechukas (DDP) description based on the two relevant complex transition points and their interference. The DDP action is analyzed directly in the weak- and strong-coupling limits, yielding explicit asymptotic expressions for the oscillation phase, oscillation envelope, far-detuned line shape, and near-resonant behavior. In particular, the strong-coupling asymptotics imply a linewidth proportional to the inverse peak Rabi frequency for resonant odd-$π$ pulses, thereby exhibiting the power-narrowing characteristic of the Lorentzian pulse.

</details>


### [108] [Closed Timelike Curve Decoding on Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27473)
*Sai Nandan Morapakula,Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在Deutsch闭合类时曲线（D-CTC）一致性回路中嵌入Hayden-Preskill/Yoshida-Kitaev恢复映射的有限维电路模型。通过寄存器路由构造，将Deutsch映射显式化为两次SWAP操作与加扰器-解码器的组合，揭示CTC寄存器的诱导映射为替换信道，其唯一不动点为消息态。作者在量子硬件上实现了Lloyd型后选择解码器电路，并提出经典反馈迭代方案，利用Qiskit仿真和IBM硬件数据量化了单量子比特实例中的解码保真度、后选择开销、路由相关噪声及量子几何敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deutsch的CTC模型通过不动点条件描述时序违反寄存器，但其物理实现和与量子信息处理协议（如Hayden-Preskill恢复）的关联尚未在具体电路层面得到清晰刻画。本文旨在构建一个将Hayden-Preskill/Yoshida-Kitaev恢复映射嵌入CTC一致性回路的显式电路模型，以弥合量子引力中CTC理论与可实验模拟的量子电路之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者采用寄存器路由构造方法：初始SWAP将入射CTC态移至空闲转储寄存器，加扰器（scrambler）和解码器作用于其余活跃寄存器，最终SWAP将恢复消息写回CTC寄存器。当活跃分支成功恢复消息时，CTC寄存器上的诱导映射为替换信道 σ↦ρ_M，唯一不动点为消息态 ρ_M。在此基础上，实现了Lloyd型后选择解码器电路，并在量子硬件上运行；同时提出基于实验估计映射的经典反馈迭代方案，利用Qiskit仿真和IBM硬件数据进行单量子比特实验，评估解码保真度、后选择开销、路由相关噪声及量子几何敏感性。

Result: 通过寄存器路由构造，Deutsch映射被显式实现为SWAP-加扰-解码-SWAP序列；数学上证明CTC寄存器的诱导映射是替换信道，不动点为消息态。在IBM量子硬件和Qiskit仿真中，单量子比特实例的量化结果给出了解码保真度、后选择成功率（开销）、不同路由方案的噪声表现差异，以及量子几何敏感性（可能关联于量子Fisher信息或Fubini-Study度规）的测量数据。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将Hayden-Preskill/Yoshida-Kitaev恢复映射嵌入D-CTC一致性回路，并通过寄存器路由构造使Deutsch映射在电路层面显式化。诱导映射为替换信道这一结论从量子信息角度为D-CTC提供了新的理解。量子硬件实现验证了Lloyd型后选择方案在当前NISQ设备上的可行性，经典反馈迭代方案则为实验测量CTC映射提供了实用工具，同时揭示了路由方案对噪声的显著影响。

Abstract: Deutsch closed timelike curves (D-CTCs) are described by a fixed-point condition for a chronology-violating register. We study a finite-dimensional circuit model that places a Hayden--Preskill/Yoshida--Kitaev recovery map inside such a consistency loop. A register-routing construction makes the Deutsch map explicit: an initial SWAP moves the incoming CTC state to an idle dump register, the scrambler and decoder act on the remaining active registers, and a final SWAP writes the recovered message back to the CTC register. When the active branch recovers the message, the induced map on the CTC register is the replacement channel \(σ\mapsto ρ_M\), with the unique fixed point \(ρ_M\). We implement the associated Lloyd-type post-selected decoder circuits on quantum hardware and formulate a classical-feedback iteration for the experimentally estimated map. Qiskit simulations and IBM-hardware data for single-qubit instances quantify decoder fidelity, post-selection overhead, routing-dependent noise, and quantum-geometric susceptibility.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Interference as a Proposal Mechanism for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27509)
*Donald J. Jacobs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QIPS 利用种子条件量子电路产生局域化干涉图样，作为 QUBO/Ising 优化的有限采样提议分布。在 18 至 29 比特的六类基准测试中，QIPS 在 top-K 覆盖率、命中率等多项指标上展现出与匹配的经典对照组相当的竞争力，验证了局域化量子干涉作为一种资源高效提议机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子干涉效应能否作为一种资源高效的提议机制，用于计算量子优化中的组合优化问题（QUBO/Ising），在保持与经典方法可比性能的前提下，利用量子电路的独特优势生成候选解。

Method: 提出 QIPS 框架：1) 使用种子条件量子电路生成局域化干涉图样，作为有限采样的提议分布；2) 采用固定的两层门基电路架构，每个电路 100 次采样；3) 从提议分布中采样候选比特串，经典评分后更新精英前沿（低能量解集）；4) 总提议数正比于 nb；5) 设置匹配的经典对照组（保持相同的搜索循环、前沿更新规则和提议预算）。

Result: 在 18 ≤ nb ≤ 29 的六类基准测试家族上，QIPS 在 top-K 覆盖率、命中率、多重性、希尔伯特空间覆盖率和二元秩等多项指标上，保持了与经典对照组相当的有竞争力的进展，且希尔伯特空间维度随 2^{nb} 增长。

Conclusion: 局域化量子干涉可作为一种资源高效的提议机制用于计算量子优化，在有限采样预算下实现与经典方法竞争的性能。

Abstract: Quantum Interference Proposal Search (QIPS) uses seed-conditioned quantum circuits to generate localized interference patterns as finite-shot proposal distributions for QUBO/Ising optimization. Candidate $n_b$-bit strings are sampled from these distributions, scored classically and used to update an elite frontier of low-energy solutions. QIPS uses a fixed two-layer gate-based circuit architecture with 100 shots per circuit while the Hilbert-space dimension grows as $2^{n_b}$. Across six benchmark families with $18 \le n_b \le 29$, QIPS maintains competitive progress relative to a matched classical control that preserves the same search loop, frontier update rule and proposal budget, with total proposals proportional to $n_b$. Performance is assessed using top-$K$ coverage, hit rate, multiplicity, Hilbert-space coverage and dyadic-rank metrics. The results identify localized quantum interference as a resource-efficient proposal mechanism for computational quantum optimization.

</details>


### [110] [Floquet Abelian Multicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27521)
*Alexey A. Kovalev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Floquet AMC码，通过将AMC复合体的商格表示提升至时空并旋转ZX网络中的电路-时间方向，将高维同调冗余转化为仅由两比特XX/ZZ测量组成的周期调度。构造了广义自行车码和level-2 AMC4实例，获得参数为[[108,6,5]]、[[144,6,8]]、[[324,6,10]]的Floquet记忆体，在EM3噪声模型下估计伪阈值约1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMC码具有紧凑量子LDPC结构和单次纠错能力，但其原始权重为6的稳定子直接测量实现复杂。作者希望设计一种仅用两比特测量即可实现高维同调冗余的Floquet方案，避免直接测量高权重稳定子，同时保留拓扑保护特性。

Method: (1) 从有限阿贝尔群代数上的AMC复体出发，导出商格表示；(2) 将该格提升至时空，并在ZX网络中旋转电路-时间方向；(3) 当check和data蜘蛛节点具有偶价并允许时间定向局部端口匹配时，网络分解为XX和ZZ两比特测量的周期调度；(4) 构造广义自行车码和level-2 AMC4实例，计算瞬时稳定子群和嵌入距离；(5) 使用局部Pauli-web探测器模板和束搜索解码，在EM3噪声模型下评估性能。

Result: 获得Floquet记忆体参数：[[108,6,5]]、[[144,6,8]]、[[324,6,10]]；在测量原生EM3噪声模型下，估计伪阈值约1.2%。

Conclusion: Floquet AMC码提供了仅用两比特测量实现高维同调冗余的紧凑方案，无需直接测量原始权重6稳定子，在保持拓扑保护的同时显著简化了实验实现。

Abstract: Abelian multicycle (AMC) codes are compact quantum low-density parity-check codes whose multiblock chain-complex structure provides redundant low-weight stabilizers and supports single-shot error correction. We introduce Floquet AMC codes by deriving a quotient-lattice representation of a general level-$j$, $D$-dimensional AMC complex over a finite Abelian group algebra, lifting this lattice to spacetime, and rotating the circuit-time direction in the associated ZX network. When the check and data spiders have even valence and admit a time-oriented local port matching, the network decomposes into a periodic schedule of native two-qubit $XX$ and $ZZ$ measurements. We construct generalized-bicycle and level-$2$ AMC4 examples, determine their instantaneous stabilizer groups, and compute their embedded distances by minimizing over all inequivalent circuit cuts. For AMC4 instances locally equivalent to four-dimensional toric codes, we obtain Floquet memories with parameters $[[108,6,5]]$, $[[144,6,8]]$, and $[[324,6,10]]$. Local Pauli-web detector templates and beam-search decoding under the measurement-native EM3 noise model yield an estimated pseudothreshold of approximately $1.2\%$. These results provide compact measurement-only realizations of higher-dimensional homological redundancy without directly measuring the original weight-six stabilizers.

</details>


### [111] [Noise-resilient and Scalable Quantum Error Correction for Nuclear Spin Qubits in Silicon with Electron Shuttling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27527)
*Wayne M. Witzel,Matthew D. Grace,Dwight R. Luhman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出电子对干涉测量（EPI）协议，利用量子点阵列中的等电子核自旋量子比特，通过电子对的分裂与穿梭实现核自旋宇称到电子单态/三重态的相干转移，结合全局NMR和超精细诱导Z旋转，构建适用于CSS量子纠错的完备通用门集，且对电荷噪声具有极低敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中核自旋量子比特虽具有极长寿命和低噪声敏感性，但其孤立性导致控制和测量极为困难。现有方案在实现高保真度控制和测量的同时保持噪声鲁棒性方面存在不足，亟需一种既能克服操控挑战又能维持噪声鲁棒性的新协议。

Method: 作者提出电子对干涉测量（EPI）协议：在含等电子核自旋量子比特的量子点阵列中，将一对电子初始化为单态基态，随后将其分裂并穿梭至含核自旋的量子点。通过该过程实现核自旋宇称到电子单态/三重态的相干转移，从而完成测量。全局NMR用于基矢变换和动力学去耦，结合选择性超精细诱导的Z_π旋转，构成完备的通用量子计算门集，并针对CSS量子纠错码进行了定制设计。

Result: 该方案展示了对电荷噪声的极低敏感性，并系统研究了直流和交流磁场不均匀性的影响，发现其敏感性强依赖于磁场不均匀性的相对强度。门集被证明对CSS量子纠错具有天然的适配性，且整体方案保持了对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EPI协议有效解决了硅中核自旋量子比特操控与测量困难的核心挑战，同时保持了其固有的噪声鲁棒性优势。该方案为基于硅量子点的可扩展、容错量子计算提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Nuclear spin qubits in silicon are well-isolated from their environment. Consequently, they have very long lifetimes and low sensitivity to noise, but this also suggests that control and measurement is challenging. We introduce electron pair interferometry (EPI), a protocol to overcome this challenge and maintain robustness to noise. EPI is implemented using an array of quantum dots with isoelectronic nuclear spin qubits located in the dots. A pair of electrons are initialized into a singlet ground state, split apart, and shuttled to the dots containing nuclear spin qubits. We show it is possible to coherently transfer the parity of the nuclei onto the measurable state of singlet/triplet-encoded electrons. Global nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) can be used to change bases and implement dynamical decoupling (DD). Combined with selective hyperfine-induced $Z_π$ rotations, our gate set is complete for universal quantum computation, tailored to Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum error correction, and robust to noise. We discuss very low sensitivity to charge noise and study the sensitivity to both DC and AC magnetic field inhomogeneity which depends strongly on their relative strengths.

</details>


### [112] [Efficient learning of bosonic unitaries beyond the Gaussian class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27534)
*Xiaobin Zhao,Quntao Zhuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在玻色子酉过程学习中建立了一个"可处理性边界"：一般m模非高斯酉过程需要指数级资源(Ω(E^{2m}))来学习，但两类广大的非高斯家族——t掺杂高斯酉过程和高斯可纠缠酉过程——仅需多项式资源。关键在于不可约的多模非高斯关联，而非单纯的非高斯性或纠缠本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模量子过程因高维度和高斯类之外的复杂纠缠结构而难以学习。作者试图厘清：学习的根本障碍究竟是非高斯性本身，还是非高斯性与多模纠缠之间不可约的混合。这一问题对量子信息处理、量子传感和量子计算中的噪声表征具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用前向（forward-only）协议，使用相干态探针、高斯操作、局部外差探测和经典后处理，首先识别全局高斯混合部分，将剩余任务约化为单模或少模学习。技术上，论文证明了一个多模量子Darmois-Skitovich定理（模式扩展的无源网络仅在输入为高斯态时保持乘积结构），以及一个非高斯过程的几乎必然激活定理（非高斯酉过程对几乎所有相干输入态产生非高斯输出）。还提出了利用未校准相干探针学习酉过程的方法。

Result: 建立了三条核心结果：(1)一般m模酉过程在每模能量≤E时至少需要Ω(E^{2m})次信道使用来学习；(2)t掺杂高斯酉过程和高斯可纠缠酉过程可用多项式资源学习，尽管它们可同时具有广泛的非高斯性和强多模纠缠；(3)学习的复杂性源于非高斯性与纠缠的不可约混合，而非任一资源单独造成。

Conclusion: 学习的复杂性来源于非高斯性与纠缠的不可约混合，而非任一资源单独作用。这一"可处理性边界"为理解量子多体系统的可学习性提供了新视角，并提出了一套适用于广泛非高斯类别的实用学习方案。

Abstract: Multimode quantum processes are generally difficult to learn, due to the large dimensionality and complex entanglement structure beyond the Gaussian class. Here, we show that the fundamental obstruction is not non-Gaussianity itself, but the buildup of irreducible multimode non-Gaussian correlations. We establish a tractability frontier for bosonic unitary learning: a general $m$-mode unitary with input energy at most $E$ per mode requires at least $Ω(E^{2m})$ channel uses, whereas two broad non-Gaussian families---$t$-doped Gaussian unitaries and Gaussian-entanglable unitaries---can be learned with resources polynomial in $m$. The latter can exhibit both extensive non-Gaussianity and strong multimode entanglement. Our forward-only protocols use coherent-state probes, Gaussian operations, local heterodyne detection, and classical post-processing to identify the global Gaussian mixing and reduce the remaining task to single- or few-mode learning. The analysis also yields a multimode quantum Darmois--Skitovich theorem showing that mode-spreading passive networks preserve product structure only for Gaussian input states, an almost-sure activation theorem for non-Gaussian processes showing that non-Gaussian unitaries yield non-Gaussian outputs for almost all coherent input states, and a method for learning unitaries from uncalibrated coherent probes. Our results identify that complexity of learning arises from irreducible mixing of non-Gaussianity and entanglement, rather than either resource alone.

</details>


### [113] [Exploring the use of quantum computing for facilitating spatially and temporally resolved models of a biological cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27571)
*Muralikrishnan Gopalakrishnan Meena,Dileep Kishore,Jerry M. Parks,Luke Bertels,Dilipkumar N. Asthagiri,Travis Humble,Thomas L. Beck,Mitchel J. Doktycz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展望了量子计算在全细胞模拟中的潜力，从原子-分子建模、代谢调控网络到全细胞空间建模三个层级进行了复杂性分析，识别出量子算法在理论上可提供显著加速的领域，并讨论了数据编码、系统条件、测量约束及量子-HPC混合集成等实际瓶颈，为量子加速全细胞建模绘制了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全细胞模拟需在原子分辨率下整合数千种蛋白质和代谢物，跨越飞秒到小时的时间尺度和纳米到微米的空间尺度，经典计算架构无法在完整细胞周期内以全原子分辨率完成此类模拟，因此需要探索量子计算是否能提供可行的替代路径。

Method: 本文采用前瞻性评述方法，在三个层级（原子-分子建模、代谢与调控网络、全细胞空间建模）上对经典算法与量子算法进行复杂性理论分析，识别在特定算法假设下量子加速的理论优势区间，并系统梳理了近中期含噪量子架构与容错量子架构的算法设计进展。

Result: 分析表明，在特定算法假设下，量子算法在代表性生物学问题上存在理论上显著加速的区间。同时识别出关键瓶颈：数据编码开销、系统条件化、测量约束以及量子计算与经典高性能计算的混合集成。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同勾勒出一条量子加速全细胞建模的路线图，并指出了此类多尺度框架未来可能带来的生物学洞见。

Abstract: Whole-cell simulation, modeling all of a cell's functional systems over its life cycle, is an outstanding challenge in computational biology. Even the simplest living cell contains thousands of interacting proteins and metabolites (on the order of trillions of atoms) whose full functional dynamics spans roughly five orders of magnitude in space (nm to $μ$m) and nearly nineteen in time (fs to hours). Further, many of the governing physical and chemical properties remain incompletely characterized. Simulating such complex systems at fully atomistic resolution over a full cell cycle is computationally intractable on classical architectures, raising a central question: Can quantum computing offer a viable path to whole-cell simulations that integrate molecular- and systems-level complexity? This Perspective examines the potential of quantum computing across three hierarchical scales: atomistic-molecular modeling, metabolic and regulatory networks, and whole-cell spatial modeling. We present a complexity analysis comparing classical and quantum algorithms for representative biological problems, identifying regimes of substantial theoretical speedup under specified algorithmic assumptions. We highlight algorithmic developments designed to leverage both near-term exploratory and fault-tolerant quantum architectures, and discuss practical bottlenecks: data encoding overhead, system conditioning, measurement constraints, and hybrid quantum-HPC integration. Together, these results outline a roadmap for quantum-accelerated whole-cell modeling and the biological insights such multiscale frameworks may eventually enable.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum LDPC codes with design rate 1/5 and good performance below 1000 physical qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27644)
*Yifan Hong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种设计速率为1/5、校验权重为9的新型量子LDPC码族，在0.1%电路级噪声下，配合GPU加速的Relay-BP译码，仅需数百物理量子比特即可逼近teraquop存储体系，且译码延迟约1-2ms，适用于囚禁离子和中性原子处理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常速率量子LDPC码虽在渐近极限下承诺恒定的空间开销，但发现具有良好实际性能的有限长码实例仍具挑战性。作者旨在填补这一空白，为近期容错量子计算机提供实用的有限长常速率量子LDPC码。

Method: (1) 采用具有非阿贝尔群对称性（Z_l ⋊ Z_m）的设计速率1/2的经典LDPC码的平衡积构造量子码；(2) 为可重构原子阵列定制了基于简单贪婪调度器的症状提取电路；(3) 构建了关于群对称性等变的逻辑Pauli基，以压缩编码手术的设计空间；(4) 使用GPU加速的Relay-BP译码器进行解码。

Result: 在0.1%空闲无关的电路级噪声下，仅需数百物理量子比特即接近teraquop存储体系；Relay-BP译码平均延迟约1-2ms；单轮原子重排时间约30-60ms（基于当前硬件规格），并有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: 该工作进一步推进了常速率量子LDPC码在近期容错量子计算机中的实用性，所引入的群对称经典码构造及等变逻辑基方法也具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Constant-rate quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes promise fault-tolerant quantum computation with constant spatial overhead in the asymptotic limit. Nonetheless, discovering finite-length code instances with good practical performance remains challenging. We introduce a new family of quantum LDPC codes with design rate $1/5$ and check weight $9$ that approaches the teraquop memory regime per qubit-round with several hundred physical qubits, under idling-free circuit-level noise of strength $0.1\%$ and GPU-accelerated Relay-belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoding with average latencies around 1-2 ms, a regime relevant to trapped-ion and neutral-atom processors. The construction involves the balanced product of classical LDPC codes with design rate $1/2$ that share non-abelian $\mathbb{Z}_\ell \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_m$ group symmetries, which may be of independent interest for classical error correction. We build syndrome extraction circuits tailored to reconfigurable atom arrays using a simple greedy scheduler, with single-round rearrangement times around 30-60 ms using present hardware specifications, and substantial room for future improvements. We also construct logical Pauli bases that are equivariant with respect to the group symmetry, which can significantly compress the design space for code surgery. Together, these results further advance the practicality of constant-rate quantum LDPC codes for near-term, fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [115] [Quadruply Bonded Mo2 Molecules: An Innate Emitter-Resonator Quantum System in Free Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27683)
*Miao Meng,Ying Ning Tan,Zi Cong He,Zi Hao Zhong,Jia Zhou,Yu Li Zhou,Guang Yuan Zhu,Chun Y. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示四重键Mo₂单元可作为一个天然的"发射体-谐振器"集成量子系统，在常温下将可见光子囚禁于两个钼原子之间，产生极小模式体积的局域强电磁场，展现出真空拉比劈裂和Mollow三重态等量子光学现象，将腔量子电动力学拓展至分子科学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子电动力学（QED）的原理拓展至分子系统，探索分子本身能否同时充当发射体和谐振器的集成量子平台，从而摆脱传统量子光学实验对外部光学腔的依赖，在自由空间和常温条件下实现量子光学实验。
