Method: 以三种具有四重键的Mo₂配合物为研究对象，利用共振荧光光谱技术，在常温下观测Mo-Mo间电荷转移跃迁与局域散射场的相干耦合。通过边带激发方式，将单分子及N分子系综共振耦合至散射光，产生覆盖宽波长范围的分立光学模式，并与Ni₂基体系中的极化激元跃迁进行对比验证。

Result: Mo₂单元（Mo-Mo键长约2.1Å）能在常温下将可见光子囚禁于两个钼原子之间，产生极小模式体积的量子化局域电磁场。共振荧光谱中观测到真空拉比劈裂和Mollow三重态，证实了相干耦合。边带激发下产生了一系列覆盖宽波长范围的分立光学模式，且极化激元跃迁与Ni₂基体系完全一致。

Conclusion: Mo₂分子被确立为一个独立的"发射体-谐振器"集成量子系统，使得利用常规光谱仪器在自由空间中进行量子光学实验成为可能。该工作将量子电动力学拓展至分子科学，为理解金属-金属键、分子物理及光-物质相互作用提供了全新视角。

Abstract: In recent decades, significant progress has been made in constructing and studying individual quantum systems based on two level atoms (molecules) and photons. Here we demonstrate that the quadruply bonded Mo2 unit, with a MoMo bond distance as short as 2.1A, functions as an innate emitter resonator molecular quantum system capable of trapping visible light photons between the two molybdenum atoms under ambient conditions, thereby generating an intense quantized local electromagnetic field with an extremely small mode volume. The resonance fluorescence spectra of three Mo2 complexes indicate that the intermetallic Mo-Mo charge transfer transition is coherently coupled to the local scattering field, exhibiting vacuum Rabi splitting and Mollow triplets. Resonant coupling of single molecules and N-molecule ensembles to the scattered light through sideband excitation produces a sequence of discrete optical modes spanning a broad wavelength range, with polaritonic transitions identical to those observed in Ni2 based systems. These results establish the Mo2 molecule as an independent emitter resonator integrated quantum system that enables quantum optical experiments in free space using conventional spectroscopic instrumentation. This work extends quantum electrodynamics into molecular science, providing new insights into metal metal bonding, molecular physics, and light matter interactions.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum coherence and entanglement in wireless quantum batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27718)
*Jun-Jie Jiang,Jin-Di Cao,Yu-Fei Zhang,Meng-Long Song,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由结构化玻色环境介导的无线量子电池系统的充电动力学与热力学性能，揭示了非马尔可夫记忆效应与耦合对称性在调控能量传输中的不同作用，并阐明了两种相干性在功提取中的分工角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索环境辅助量子能量存储器件中，非马尔可夫性、耦合对称性与量子相干性等资源对充电动力学和热力学性能的影响机制，为设计高效量子电池提供理论指导。

Method: 采用统一资源理论框架，分别分析马尔可夫弱耦合区与非马尔可夫强耦合区下的动力学行为，考察一阶相干性与l₁-范数相干的演化，结合纠缠与储能同步振荡、耦合不对称/对称条件下的能流调控，以及基于ergotropy的热力学分析。

Result: 在马尔可夫弱耦合区，l₁-范数相干的动态重构弥补了一阶相干的单调衰减以维持能量传输；在非马尔可夫强耦合区，环境回流诱导纠缠与储能的协同共振；不对称耦合（偏向电池）在无记忆环境中优化能量获取，而强相互作用下对称耦合激活暗态保护机制，将能量囚禁在无退相干子空间；ergotropy分析表明一阶相干性确立了非相干功的激活阈值，l₁-范数相干则是相干功提取的显式燃料。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫记忆效应与耦合对称性是调控无线量子电池能量传输的关键控制参数，不同类型的量子相干性在功提取中承担不同角色，为环境辅助量子储能器件的工程设计提供了精细的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the charging dynamics and thermodynamic performance of a wireless quantum battery system mediated by a common structured bosonic environment. By employing a unified resource-theoretic analysis, we elucidate the distinct roles of non-Markovian memory effects and coupling symmetry in regulating energy transfer. In the Markovian weak-coupling regime, we identify a transformative mechanism where the dynamic reconstruction of $l_1$-norm coherence compensates for the monotonic decay of first-order coherence to sustain energy transport. Conversely, the non-Markovian strong-coupling regime facilitates a cooperative resonance, characterized by the synchronized oscillation of entanglement and stored energy induced by environmental backflow. Furthermore, we reveal that coupling symmetry acts as a critical control parameter: while asymmetric coupling favoring the battery optimizes energy gain in memoryless environments, symmetric coupling under strong interactions unlocks a dark-state protection mechanism, effectively trapping energy within a decoherence-free subspace. Finally, a thermodynamic analysis based on ergotropy demonstrates that first-order coherence establishes the activation threshold for incoherent work, whereas $l_1$-norm coherence serves as the explicit fuel for coherent work extraction. These findings provide a refined theoretical framework for engineering environment-assisted quantum energy storage devices.

</details>


### [117] [Bridging continuous control and Floquet driving for charging many-body spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27985)
*Sebastián V. Romero,Xi Chen,Yue Ban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于自旋系统的量子电池研究，涵盖充电与功提取协议、外部因素影响、Floquet工程驱动方案，以及实验实现与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子信息和量子热力学的发展，微观尺度能量存储的理解已被重塑。量子电池利用量子相干性、关联及多体动力学，为传统宏观化学电池提供了概念性替代方案，亟需系统梳理该领域的进展。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了：(1) 基于自旋的量子电池中已有的充电和功提取协议；(2) 外部因素（如退相干、温度等）对性能的影响；(3) 连续驱动与周期驱动（Floquet工程）自旋链之间的理论联系；(4) 现有实验实现方案及其在近中期量子技术中的可扩展性评估。

Result: 综述表明，自旋量子电池可通过多种协议实现能量存储与提取，Floquet工程为周期驱动系统提供了有效理论框架，且连续驱动与周期驱动自旋链之间存在内在联系；目前已有多项实验方案被提出，展示出在现有量子技术平台上的可实现性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 自旋量子电池是量子器件中能量存储与传输的有前景方向，Floquet工程驱动的自旋链为其提供了统一的理论平台。当前实验方案已展示出良好的可实现性，但仍需在可扩展性和实际性能优化方面进一步探索。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum information and quantum thermodynamics have reshaped the understanding of energy storage at the microscopic scale, paving the way toward protocols for storing and transferring energy in quantum devices. These systems, known as quantum batteries, offer a conceptual alternative to conventional macroscopic chemical batteries by exploiting quantum coherence, correlations, and many-body dynamics. By navigating the landscape of established spin-based quantum batteries, we review existing charging and work-extraction protocols, as well as the impact of external factors on their performance. Motivated by Floquet-engineered proposals, we further establish a connection between continuously and periodically driven spin chains as platforms for quantum energy storage. Finally, we survey experimental realizations and proposals, highlighting their implementability and scalability in current and near-term quantum technologies.

</details>


### [118] [Phase Retrieval in $\mathbb C^4$ Requires Exactly Eleven Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27719)
*Meng Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用微分拓扑中的示性类与上同调群，证明了 C^4 中相位恢复所需的最小强度测量数为 11，从而终结了一个长期悬而未决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，C^4 中相位恢复所需的最小强度测量数量是 10 还是 11 一直悬而未决。该问题直接关联纯态量子 tomography 中信息完备 POVM 所需的最小元素数，以及区分纯态所需的正交基数量。

Method: 利用微分拓扑中的示性类 (characteristic classes) 和上同调群 (cohomology groups) 工具，证明不存在任何包含 10 个向量的族能够具备相位恢复性质，从而给出紧的下界。

Result: 证明了 C^4 中不存在 10 个向量构成的族具有相位恢复性质；结合 Vinzant 已给出的 11 向量构造，精确最小值为 11。推论包括：C^4 上信息完备的秩一 POVM 恰好需要 11 个元素；三个正交基不足以唯一区分 C^4 中所有纯态，因此恰好需要四个正交基。

Conclusion: C^4 相位恢复的最小测量数被精确确定为 11，同时彻底解决了 Carmeli 等人留下的关于纯态区分所需正交基数目的问题：恰好四个正交基是充分且必要的。

Abstract: Determining the minimal number of intensity measurements required for phase retrieval in $\mathbb{C}^4$ has been a long-standing open problem. Prior to this work, the best-known results implied that this minimum was either $10$ or $11$. In this paper, we leverage characteristic classes and cohomology groups from differential topology to prove that no family of $10$ vectors in $\mathbb{C}^4$ possesses the phase retrieval property. Combining our lower bound with Vinzant's explicit eleven-vector construction establishes that the exact minimum is $11$. Our result yields a significant consequence for pure state quantum tomography, namely, a rank-one POVM on $\mathbb{C}^4$ requires exactly $11$ elements to be informationally complete for pure states. This further implies that three orthonormal bases are insufficient to uniquely distinguish all pure states in $\mathbb{C}^4$. Because four orthonormal bases are already known to be sufficient, we conclude that exactly four bases are required, thereby completely resolving the problem left in [C. Carmeli, T. Heinosaari, J. Schultz, A. Toigo, Eur. Phys. J. D].

</details>


### [119] [Scaling theory of decoherence in Dicke superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28034)
*Nico S. Bassler,Julian Lyne,Javier Cuerda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文发展了一种包含局域退相和自发辐射的标度理论，揭示了 Dicke 超辐射中从完全集体、部分集体到独立辐射的三种标度机制，并指出局域退相干可在增大 N 的情况下阻止 N² 标度的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体相干性的存活取决于关联积累与退相干之间的竞争。在 Dicke 超辐射中，集体辐射如何受局域退相和自发辐射影响，其标度行为尚未有系统的理论描述。作者旨在建立一个标度理论来刻画这种竞争，并确定从集体到独立辐射的转变边界。

Method: 作者发展了包含局域退相（local dephasing）和自发辐射（spontaneous emission）的标度理论（scaling theory），对 N 个辐射体的 Dicke 超辐射系统进行分析，导出不同参数区域下的强度标度行为，并识别出完全集体、部分集体和独立辐射三种标度机制。

Result: 得到了三种标度机制：完全集体机制（峰值强度 ∝ N²）、部分集体机制和独立辐射机制。完全集体机制的边界定义了一个瞬态可观测量中的连续相变。关键发现是：局域退相干可以阻止 N² 标度的出现，即使 N 持续增大。

Conclusion: 多体相干性的存亡取决于关联积累与退相干之间的精细竞争。局域退相干的存在意味着，单纯增加辐射体数目 N 并不足以保证超辐射的 N² 集体增强效应，系统可能在达到完全集体行为之前就被退相干所主导。

Abstract: The survival of many-body coherence depends on the competition between correlation buildup and decoherence. In Dicke superradiance, collective emission builds up correlations, producing a peak intensity scaling as $N^2$ for $N$ emitters. We develop a scaling theory including local dephasing and spontaneous emission and obtain fully collective, partially collective, and independent-emitter scaling regimes. The boundary of the fully collective regime defines a continuous phase transition in a transient observable. Local decoherence can prevent $N^2$ scaling despite increasing $N$.

</details>


### [120] [Exact Incompatibility-Breaking Criterion for Unital Qubit Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27757)
*Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了噪声量子比特信道使所有POVM联合可测的条件。对单位信道给出了精确的不相容性破缺判据，证明投影测量足以确定边界；对非单位信道，构造了非对称父POVM并给出充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究噪声量子比特信道在何种条件下能使所有正算子值测度（POVM）变得联合可测，即信道的不相容性破缺（incompatibility-breaking）问题。这通过量子导向（steering）与联合可测性的对应关系，直接关联到量子比特态的不可导向性判据。

Method: 利用量子导向与联合可测性的对偶对应关系（steering–joint-measurability correspondence）。对单位量子比特信道，通过分析投影测量的不相容性来完全刻画边界；对非单位信道，构造一个一般非对称的父POVM（parent POVM），推导充分的信道不相容性破缺条件。

Result: 对任意单位量子比特信道，给出了精确的不相容性破缺判据，并证明投影测量就已确定任意POVM的边界。通过对应关系，得到所有具有最大混合边际的两量子比特态的精确POVM导向边界，Werner态作为特例被恢复。对非单位信道，给出了显式的充分不相容性破缺条件，进而得到任意两量子比特态的充分不可导向性判据。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了单位量子比特信道的不相容性破缺问题，并为非单位情形提供了充分条件，深化了对量子不相容性、量子导向与信道噪声之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We study when a noisy qubit channel renders all positive-operator-valued measures (POVMs) jointly measurable. For every unital qubit channel, we derive the exact incompatibility-breaking criterion and prove that projective measurements already determine the boundary for arbitrary POVMs. Through the steering--joint-measurability correspondence, this result gives the exact POVM-steering boundary for all two-qubit states with maximally mixed marginals, with the Werner states recovered as a special case. For nonunital qubit channels, we construct a generally asymmetric parent POVM and obtain an explicit sufficient incompatibility-breaking condition, which in turn yields a sufficient unsteerability criterion for arbitrary two-qubit states.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Magnonics: Quantum States Generation and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27771)
*Zi-Xu Lu,Xuan Zuo,Xin-Lei Hei,Gang Liu,Zeng-Xing Liu,Qi Guo,Peng-Bo Li,Jie Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于铁磁材料中磁振子的混合量子系统的最新进展，重点关注磁振子量子态的生成及其在量子信息、量子传感、暗物质探测等领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子混合系统在过去十年取得显著发展，但需要系统梳理强耦合实验基础、磁振子量子态的制备协议及其广泛应用前景，以指导该领域的未来研究方向。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，首先介绍腔磁振子学中的代表性强耦合实验（强耦合是实现相干量子控制和量子态制备的前提），随后回顾Fock态、猫态、压缩态、纠缠态等多种磁振子量子态的制备方案，最后讨论其潜在应用。

Result: 磁振子已实现与微波光子、光学光子、超导量子比特、声子、自旋及铁磁体质心运动等多种系统的耦合；基于强耦合，已发展出制备Fock态、猫态、压缩态和纠缠态等多种磁振子量子态的理论协议，并展示了在宏观量子研究、量子信息科学、量子传感、磁振子量子器件和暗物质探测等领域的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 量子磁振子学领域已取得丰硕成果，强耦合为磁振子量子态工程奠定了基础，未来该方向在基础量子物理研究和实际量子技术应用中均有广阔发展空间。

Abstract: Hybrid systems based on magnons in ferromagnetic materials, such as yttrium iron garnet, have achieved remarkable development in the last decade. These include the coupling of magnons to microwave and optical photons, superconducting qubits, phonons, spins, the center-of-mass motion of a ferromagnet, etc. Here, we review both the experimental and theoretical progress in this field, focusing on the generation of magnonic quantum states and their applications in a broad range of fields. Since the strong coupling is a prerequisite for achieving coherent quantum control of magnons and preparing magnonic quantum states, we start by introducing representative strong-coupling experiments in cavity magnonics, then review a series of protocols for creating various magnonic quantum states, such as Fock, cat, squeezed, and entangled states, and discuss their potential applications in macroscopic quantum studies, quantum information science, quantum sensing, magnonic quantum devices, dark matter detection, and so on. Finally, we summarize the review and give an outlook for the future study of quantum magnonics.

</details>


### [122] [Resource-efficient quantum-selected configuration interaction for molecular properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27777)
*Suprava Sahoo,Abdul Kalam,Kenji Sugisaki,V. S. Prasannaa,B. P. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子计算框架，通过识别主导费米子激发算符并进行参考态保真度损失分析，构建紧凑哈密顿量，在III A族单氟化物体系上实现了近二次方的哈密顿项数缩减。在20量子比特的TlF体系中，电路深度和双量子比特门数均减少98%以上，硬件运行结果与完全活性空间组态相互作用方法的永久电偶极矩匹配度达99.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子选择的组态相互作用方法在噪声量子设备上实现全电子哈密顿量时，电路复杂度随体系规模快速增长，限制了其可扩展性。需要一种既能保持计算精度又能显著降低计算开销的方法。

Method: 提出基于主导费米子激发算符识别和参考态保真度损失分析的紧凑哈密顿量构建框架。具体为：通过实时演化参考波函数来识别重要行列式基函数，在此基础上筛选主导激发算符，结合保真度损失分析进一步压缩哈密顿量项数，最后在选定的子空间内对角化约化哈密顿量矩阵。

Result: 1. 在III A族单氟化物（BF、AlF、GaF、InF、TlF）上实现近二次方的哈密顿项数缩放改进；2. 20量子比特TlF体系中电路深度和双量子比特门数均减少98%以上；3. 在IBM Marrakesh处理器上成功运行AlF和TlF硬件实验（最多20量子比特）；4. 量子硬件得到的永久电偶极矩与完全活性空间组态相互作用值匹配度超过99.99%。

Conclusion: 该方法在含噪中等规模量子设备上展示了良好的可扩展性，通过紧凑哈密顿量构建有效降低了量子电路复杂度，同时保持了高精度计算结果，为量子计算在分子电子结构模拟中的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The quantum-selected configuration interaction identifies important determinantal basis functions through real-time evolution of a reference wavefunction and diagonalizing the Hamiltonian matrix in the resulting selected subspace. However, implementing the full electronic Hamiltonian on noisy quantum devices leads to rapidly increasing circuit complexity, limiting its scalability. To address this issue, we identify the dominant fermionic excitation operators and perform reference-state fidelity loss analysis to construct a compact Hamiltonian, reducing computational overhead while retaining high precision. Applied to Group IIIA monofluorides (BF, AlF, GaF, InF, and TlF), the proposed framework achieves a near-quadratic improvement in Hamiltonian-term scaling, enabling resource-efficient simulations. We employ this framework to compute the relativistic ground-state energies and permanent electric dipole moments (PDMs) of the systems under consideration. After validating the framework via simulations, we demonstrate hardware execution for AlF and TlF on the IBM Marrakesh processor using active spaces of up to 20 qubits. For a 20-qubit TlF system, the reduced Hamiltonian yields a reduction of higher than $ 98\%$ in both circuit depth and two-qubit gate counts, with the resulting PDMs from quantum hardware matching complete active space configuration interaction values within $99.99\%$. These results demonstrate the scalability of this approach on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices.

</details>


### [123] [Lifting Lifted Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28621)
*Yuta Hirasaki,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群扩张和图提升的系统性方法来构造提升积（LP）码族，能在保持Tanner图局部结构的同时增大码尺寸，并利用链映射与上链映射关联族内的码参数、逻辑算子及容错逻辑门。应用方面获得了更优码参数，实现了更低空间开销的code-surgery gadgets，并提出了针对提升聚类循环码的并行积手术。最后，作者建议将提升作为为代数定义的qLDPC码建立热力学族的系统性第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升积码是量子纠错码中的重要类别，但现有构造在码参数优化、容错逻辑门实现的空间开销以及缺乏类似拓扑码的"热力学族"结构等方面仍有不足。作者希望借助群扩展和图提升提供一种统一框架，系统性地构造LP码族，改善码参数、降低容错操作开销，并为代数型qLDPC码建立热力学极限奠定基础。

Method: 核心方法是基于群扩展（group extensions）和图提升（graph lifts）构造LP码族。具体来说：1）利用群扩展增大码尺寸，同时通过图提升保持Tanner图的局部结构不变；2）通过链映射（chain maps）和上链映射（cochain maps）关联族内不同码之间的码参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑门gadgets；3）提出并行积手术（parallel product surgery）用于提升聚类循环码；4）利用相干信息（coherent information）的有限尺寸交叉行为探索热力学族的定义。

Result: 1）获得了比此前报道更优的LP码参数；2）证明code-surgery gadgets可通过链映射在不同有限提升之间迁移，并在多个情形下以更低空间开销实现；3）成功开发了针对提升聚类循环码的并行积手术；4）对若干基码和选定提升，相干信息展现出有限尺寸交叉，表明提升可作为定义热力学族的第一步，但同时也揭示需要额外条件来确定唯一族。

Conclusion: 基于群扩展和图提升的构造为LP码族提供了统一且系统性的框架，不仅能优化码参数、降低容错操作的空间开销，还为代数型qLDPC码定义热力学族开辟了新路径。然而，仅凭提升本身尚不足以唯一确定热力学族，还需进一步的条件约束。

Abstract: Lifted product (LP) codes form an important class of quantum error correcting codes with favorable code parameters. We introduce a systematic construction of LP code families based on group extensions and graph lifts. The construction increases the code size while preserving the local structure of the Tanner graph, and relates code parameters, logical operators, and fault-tolerant logical-operation gadgets within the families through chain and cochain maps.
  As a first application, we obtain LP codes with better code parameters than previously reported ones. We then demonstrate that code-surgery gadgets can be transferred across the selected finite lifts through chain maps and, in several cases, implemented with lower space overhead. We also develop parallel product surgery for lifted clustered cyclic codes. Finally, we propose lifting as a systematic first step toward defining thermodynamic families for algebraically defined qLDPC codes without an underlying Euclidean lattice. For several base codes and selected lifts, coherent information exhibits finite-size crossings, while our results also indicate that additional conditions are needed to determine a unique family.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum machine learning interatomic potential: Application of variational quantum algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27841)
*Kohei Numata,Wataru Mizukami,Kosuke Mitarai,Keisuke Fujii,Yutaka Imamura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将量子电路学习（一种混合量子-经典机器学习算法）应用于机器学习原子间势（MLIP），用于预测分子能量。通过量子迁移学习架构重新训练ANI模型，量子电路增强的神经网络在特定条件下比纯经典网络精度略高，尤其在预训练模型精度尚有提升空间时效果更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子机器学习技术在机器学习原子间势领域的应用潜力，验证量子电路是否能提升分子能量预测的精度，为量子计算在计算化学中的应用提供新思路。

Method: 采用量子迁移学习架构（Mari et al., Quantum, 2020），将量子电路嵌入经典神经网络中对ANI模型进行重新训练；使用量子电路模拟器评估数值精度，并与纯经典神经网络进行对比。

Result: 在特定条件下，嵌入量子电路的模型比全经典神经网络精度略有提升；当预训练模型的精度尚有改进空间时，量子增强模型的效果更为显著。

Conclusion: 量子电路嵌入经典MLIP神经网络可带来精度增益，尤其适用于预训练模型优化空间较大的场景。该发现有望推动量子机器学习在MLIP领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: This study applied quantum circuit learning, a commonly used hybrid quantum-classical machine learning algorithm, to a machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) for predicting the energies of molecules in molecular datasets. We retrained the ANI model using the quantum transfer learning architecture [Mari et al., Quantum, 4:340, 2020] and evaluated numerical accuracy with a quantum circuit simulator. The evaluation confirmed that inserting a quantum circuit into the classical neural network of the MLIP yielded slightly higher accuracy than the fully classical neural network under certain conditions. In particular, the model incorporating a quantum circuit was more effective when the pretraining model had room for improvement in accuracy. These findings may contribute to advancing the application of quantum machine learning for MLIPs.

</details>


### [125] [Even-harmonic generation through nonequilibrium steady-state symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27847)
*Milad Jangjan,Arman Kashef,Siamak Pooyan,Stefan Scheel,Dieter Bauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在反演对称系统中，通过非平衡稳态耗散动力学打破高次谐波产生的奇次选择定则，实现偶次谐波的产生，并将偶次谐波强度与输运电流直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，具有反演对称性的体系HHG只能产生奇次谐波。作者希望在不修改哈密顿量的前提下，探索打破这一对称性选择定则的新机制，并建立非平衡电荷输运与HHG选择定则之间的联系。

Method: 研究采用边界驱动的SSH链模型，将其耦合到源极和漏极两个库。基于Lindblad主方程的相关矩阵方法，求解出携带有限DC电流的非平衡稳态密度矩阵，进而计算HHG响应谱。尽管SSH哈密顿量保持反演对称性，但电流携带的稳态密度矩阵不具备该对称性。

Result: 非平衡稳态密度矩阵的反演对称性破缺导致发射谱中出现偶次谐波。偶次谐波的强度由输运电流直接调控，二者呈正相关关系。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种纯粹依靠非平衡稳态对称性破缺来产生偶次谐波的新机制，为在中心对称量子系统中通过超快非线性光谱学手段探测输运电流提供了可行途径。

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) in inversion-symmetric systems is typically restricted to odd harmonics by symmetry. Here, we show that this selection rule can be broken without modifying the underlying Hamiltonian. We investigate a boundary-driven Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain coupled to source and sink reservoirs and demonstrate that dissipative dynamics generates a nonequilibrium steady state carrying a finite DC current. While the SSH Hamiltonian retains inversion symmetry, the current-carrying steady-state density matrix does not, leading to the emergence of even harmonics in the emitted spectrum. Using a correlation-matrix approach based on the Lindblad master equation, we obtain the steady state and calculate the resulting HHG response. We find that the intensity of the even harmonics is directly controlled by the transport current, establishing a link between nonequilibrium charge transport and HHG selection rules. Our results uncover a mechanism for even-harmonic generation that relies solely on nonequilibrium steady-state symmetry breaking and provide a route to probing transport currents through ultrafast nonlinear spectroscopy in centrosymmetric quantum systems.

</details>


### [126] [Bayesian Networks of Density Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27876)
*Steffen Lauritzen,Piotr Zwiernik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有向无环图（DAG）上贝叶斯网络的量子类似物，区分了两种构造方式：内在构造（从联合态及其条件独立性质出发）和外在构造（按序组合局部量子核）。作者证明了内在构造中多种马尔可夫性质的等价性，并指出外在构造中核的可恢复性存在障碍。此外，证明了序不变核族恰好对应于传递DAG，并为每个正定态关联了一个对数候选态，证明其在DAG马尔可夫等价下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典贝叶斯网络的概念推广到量子领域，系统性地建立量子有向无环图（DAG）上正定密度算符的马尔可夫性质理论框架，明确区分内在和外在两种构造路径，并澄清两者之间的关系与差异。

Method: 在有限维张量积希尔伯特空间上，定义正定密度算符的内在构造（基于联合态的条件独立性质）和外在构造（按DAG箭头相容的拓扑排序，依次组合局部量子核）。通过证明有序、局部和全局有向马尔可夫性质的等价性，以及熵、递归因子分解和对数刻画，建立内在构造的理论基础。在三量子比特系统中构造反例，展示外在构造中核的可恢复性障碍。利用传递DAG刻画序不变核族的充分必要条件。最后，利用对数映射为每个正定态和DAG关联候选态，证明其亚归一性及迹一条件下的有向马尔可夫性质，并分析DAG马尔可夫等价下的不变量。

Result: （1）内在构造中有序、局部和全局有向马尔可夫性质等价，并与熵刻画、递归因子分解刻画、对数刻画等价。（2）外在构造总能产生归一化态并从所有先前系统上的条件态中恢复每个核，但从顶点及其父节点的边际上未必能恢复同一核；三量子比特例子展示了这一障碍。（3）序不变性恰好对传递DAG成立：每个序不变核族产生内在有向马尔可夫态。（4）每个正定态和DAG的对数候选态是亚归一的，迹一候选态是有向马尔可夫态；候选态和超额全局信息在DAG马尔可夫等价下不变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子DAG贝叶斯网络的严格数学框架，揭示了内在（基于条件独立）和外在（基于序贯组合）两种构造之间的微妙关系：外在构造不一定满足内在马尔可夫性质，除非DAG是传递的且核族序不变。对数候选态为连接一般正定态与有向马尔可夫态提供了桥梁，且具有DAG马尔可夫等价不变性，为量子因果推断奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study quantum analogues of Bayesian networks on a directed acyclic graph (DAG), distinguishing two constructions for positive definite density operators on finite-dimensional tensor-product Hilbert spaces. The intrinsic construction starts from a joint state and its conditional-independence properties. The extrinsic construction assembles a state sequentially from prescribed local quantum kernels, following an ordering compatible with the arrows of the DAG. For the intrinsic construction, we prove the equivalence of the ordered, local, and global directed Markov properties, together with entropy, recursive-factorization, and logarithmic characterizations. The extrinsic construction always gives a normalized state and recovers each kernel as a conditional on all preceding systems. The same kernel, however, need not be recovered from the marginal on the vertex and its parents; a three-qubit example exhibits this obstruction. We prove that independence of the chosen topological ordering is sufficient exactly for transitive DAGs: every order-invariant kernel family then yields an intrinsically directed Markov state. Finally, we associate a logarithmic candidate with every positive definite state and DAG, prove that it is subnormalized, and show that the trace-one candidate is a directed Markov state. Both the candidate and the excess global information are invariant under DAG Markov equivalence.

</details>


### [127] [Beating the Bad-Cavity Limit via Auxiliary-Emitter Linewidth Squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27878)
*Youke Xu,Zeyang Liao,Xue-hua Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在劣质腔中引入两个具有相反失谐的非全同辅助发射体，通过亚辐射模式压缩有效腔线宽，在腔频率处产生超窄透射窗口，从而使目标发射体即便在裸腔处于弱耦合区时也能表现出强耦合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔QED中实现强耦合需要高腔品质因子或超小模式体积，两者难以兼得，这一固有权衡严重制约了实际应用。作者旨在打破此限制，在劣质腔中也能实现强耦合物理。

Method: 在劣质腔中引入两个具有相反失谐的非全同辅助发射体，构成混合系统。该系统支持一种亚辐射模式（subradiant mode），可大幅压缩有效腔线宽，从而在腔频率处形成一个超窄透射窗口。目标发射体置于这一工程化环境中，即可在裸腔仍处于弱耦合区的情况下展现出强耦合特征。

Result: 目标发射体表现出延长的真空拉比振荡和可分辨的自发辐射分裂——这是强耦合的明确标志，尽管裸腔本身仍处于弱耦合区。该方案成功将劣质腔转化为实现强耦合物理的有效平台。

Conclusion: 该方案打破了腔QED中品质因子与模式体积的传统权衡，为量子计算和量子传感提供了潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Strong coupling in cavity QED is conventionally achieved at the expense of either high cavity quality factors or ultrasmall mode volumes, a trade-off that fundamentally constrains practical implementations. Here, we circumvent this limitation by introducing two nonidentical auxiliary emitters with opposite detunings into a bad cavity. This hybrid system supports a subradiant mode that significantly squeeze the effective cavity linewidth, creating an ultra-narrow transmission window at the cavity frequency. As a result, a target emitter placed in this engineered environment exhibits prolonged vacuum Rabi oscillations and resolved spontaneous emission splitting, which are clear signatures of strong coupling, even though the bare cavity remains in the weak-coupling regime. Our scheme thus transforms a bad cavity into an effective platform for strong-coupling physics, with potential applications in quantum computation and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [128] [Trainability and Mode Separation of Mixed IQP-QCBMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27883)
*Youngseok Lee,Hyunwoo Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出混合IQP-QCBM，将ancilla扩展电路推广为无ancilla IQP电路（分支）的加权混合。作者证明了多项式数量分支在数据无关和特定数据依赖初始化下可避免局部贫瘠高原，并提出簇初始化策略以实现模式分离，使各分支捕获目标分布的不同特征。实验表明该方法在多个基准上收敛最快且测试MMD²最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ancilla-free IQP-QCBM虽可避免贫瘠高原但表达能力有限，而ancilla-extended模型虽提升表达能力，其局部可训练性是否保留仍未知。作者旨在设计一种既保持局部可训练性又具备充分表达能力的IQP-QCBM架构。

Method: 提出混合IQP-QCBM，将ancilla-extended电路建模为多个ancilla-free IQP分支的加权混合。理论上证明了多项式数量分支在两种初始化下避免贫瘠高原。引入模式分离概念，指出各分支需生成互异分布才能超越最优单分支。提出簇初始化：将不同无监督数据簇分配给各分支，提供初始模式分离。在16-bit数据集上精确计算验证，并在四个基准（二值聚类、2D Ising模型、二值化MNIST、484自旋玻璃）上评估。

Result: 理论证明混合IQP-QCBM在多项式分支数下保持局部可训练性。簇初始化在所有基准上收敛最快、达到最低测试MMD²。实验观察到收敛时各分支专门化到可区分的数据特征（如团块模式、磁化扇区、数字形状），验证了模式分离的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合IQP-QCBM成功在保持局部可训练性的同时提升表达能力。簇初始化是实现模式分离、使各分支捕获不同数据特征的关键。该框架为经典可训练的量子生成模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Quantum circuit Born machines (QCBMs) based on instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits are promising quantum generative models for their classical trainability. It is known that their ancilla-free form avoids barren plateaus under certain initializations, but remains non-universal. Although adding ancilla qubits raises the expressivity, whether the ancilla-extended model retains local trainability remains unknown. We propose the mixed IQP-QCBM, which generalizes the ancilla-extended circuit as a weighted mixture of ancilla-free IQP circuits, called branches. For a polynomial number of branches, we prove local barren-plateau avoidance from data-agnostic and, under certain assumptions, data-dependent initializations. We further show that the mixed IQP-QCBM can surpass the best ancilla-free IQP circuit only if its branches generate a number of distinct distributions. In particular, we focus on a behavior we call \emph{mode separation}, in which each branch captures a particular feature of the target. Mode separation is hard to attain from an initialization whose branches generate the same distribution: the gradients that would separate them are suppressed while the distributions they generate remain close. This motivates \emph{cluster initialization}, which assigns a different unsupervised data cluster to each branch and provides an initial degree of mode separation. Exact calculations on two 16-bit datasets support the barren-plateau and gradient-suppression claims. On four benchmarks, binary clusters, a two-dimensional Ising model, binarized MNIST, and a 484-spin glass, cluster initialization converges fastest and reaches the lowest mean test $\mathrm{MMD}^2$. We observe that, when achieving the lowest test $\mathrm{MMD}^2$, the mixed IQP-QCBM contains branches specialized to distinguishable data features such as blob patterns, magnetization sectors, or digit shapes.

</details>


### [129] [Witness robustness: An operational quantifier of measurement resources via free state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27892)
*Jingsong Ao,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入量子测量资源理论中"见证鲁棒性"这一新量词，其容许噪声由自由态见证元组而非物理测量构成。它量化了测量在仅由自由态组成的系综上相比自由测量的最大区分优势。该量词一般不具有忠实性，但在特定条件下可恢复；在存在资源摧毁映射的理论中该量词恒为零。文中还给出了单量子比特魔幻态和两量子比特PPT纠缠理论中的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子测量资源理论中的鲁棒性度量（如标准鲁棒性和广义鲁棒性）在噪声模型上存在局限——它们以物理测量作为容许噪声，未能刻画当只能制备自由态时资源测量是否仍具优势。作者希望提出一个能更精细反映这一实际场景的资源量词。

Method: 作者定义见证鲁棒性为：在自由态见证的凸组合扰动下，使资源测量退化为自由测量所需的最小混合参数。通过几何与对偶方法建立其操作解释——等价于在纯自由态系综上相对自由测量的最大区分优势。进一步分析忠实性的充要条件，探讨资源摧毁映射存在时的行为，并证明凸性与单调性等基本性质。最后在具体资源理论中推导解析表达式。

Result: 1. 见证鲁棒性具有清晰的操作解释：量化了仅涉及自由态制备时资源测量的最大优势。2. 该量词一般不忠实，反映了资源测量在仅能制备自由态时未必有用；识别出忠实性恢复的条件。3. 在存在资源摧毁映射的理论中，见证鲁棒性恒为零。4. 证明了凸性和单调性。5. 在单量子比特魔幻态理论中给出投影测量的解析结果，以及在两量子比特PPT纠缠理论中给出二值纯态投影测量的解析结果。

Conclusion: 见证鲁棒性为量子测量资源理论提供了一个新的实用视角，其噪声模型基于自由态见证而非物理测量，更贴合"只有自由态可制备"的实际限制。它与标准鲁棒性和广义鲁棒性形成互补，帮助理解测量资源在不同操作约束下的效用。

Abstract: We introduce the witness robustness of quantum measurements, a resource quantifier whose admissible noise consists of tuples of free-state witnesses rather than physical measurements. We establish its operational interpretation: it quantifies the maximal advantage that a measurement can provide over free measurements in discriminating an ensemble composed entirely of free states. Unlike the standard and generalized robustnesses, the witness robustness is not faithful in general, reflecting the fact that a resourceful measurement need not be useful when only free states can be prepared. We identify conditions under which faithfulness is recovered and show that, in resource theories admitting a resource-destroying map, the witness robustness vanishes for every measurement. We also establish fundamental properties, including convexity and monotonicity. Finally, we derive analytical results for projective measurements in single-qubit magic and for binary pure-state projective measurements in the two-qubit PPT entanglement theory.

</details>


### [130] [Interconnection of Quantum Networks at Urban scale: Analysis of Temporal Stability of Entangled Photon Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27906)
*Laura d'Avossa,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti,Marcello Caleffi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于量子纠缠光源的时钟同步稳定性，在城域光纤部署中实现了两个纠缠光子源之间长达8小时的稳定时间关联，最大漂移约120 ps，验证了真实网络条件下可靠同步的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间同步是纠缠量子网络的基础需求，光子时间域不可区分性对Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉和纠缠交换至关重要。除精确时间对齐外，参考时钟的长期稳定性同样关键，微小波动即可导致网络性能下降。然而，在真实城域光纤基础设施（存在不可忽略的损耗与背景噪声）中验证时钟同步的长期稳定性，此前仍缺乏充分的实验研究。

Method: 采用工作在C波段电信波长的纠缠光子源，通过时间标记符合测量分析光子探测事件之间的时间关联性，提取同步峰及其长期稳定性。实验在城域现有光纤基础设施上部署两个纠缠光子源进行纠缠分发，考察真实损耗与噪声条件下的同步性能。

Result: 锁定后，光源在连续8小时采集周期内表现出稳定的时间关联，最大观测漂移约120 ps（主要源于长光纤链路）。关联峰宽度与探测器抖动一致，表明系统未引入显著的额外时间展宽。在城域部署中，尽管存在光纤网络引入的显著非理想因素，观测到的关联依然清晰可辨，且始终可由高斯统计良好近似。

Conclusion: 两个纠缠光子源不仅在实验室受控条件下、更在真实城域运行约束下实现了可靠同步，验证了基于现有光纤基础设施进行稳定纠缠分发的可行性，为推动实用化量子网络部署提供了关键的时钟同步实验支撑。

Abstract: Time synchronization is a fundamental requirement in entanglement-based quantum networks, where the indistinguishability of photons in the time domain is essential for enabling Hong-Ou-Mandel interference and entanglement swapping. In addition to precise temporal alignment, it is equally crucial to ensure the stability of the reference clock over time, as even small fluctuations can degrade the overall performance of the network. In this work, we investigate the stability of clock synchronization for entanglement distribution based on entangled-photon sources operating in the telecommunication C-band. Temporal correlations between photon detection events are analyzed using time-tagged coincidence measurements, enabling the extraction of synchronization peaks and their long-term stability. Experimental results demonstrate that, once locked, the sources exhibit stable temporal correlations over an 8-hour acquisition period, with a maximum observed drift of approximately 120 ps, primarily associated with long fiber links. The width of the correlation peak remains consistent with detector jitter, indicating negligible additional system-induced temporal broadening. A central result of this work is the experimental synchronization between two entanglement-photon sources in a realistic metropolitan deployment, where entanglement distribution is performed over existing telecommunication infrastructure characterized by non-negligible losses and background noise. In this scenario, despite the presence of significant imperfections introduced by the metropolitan-scale fiber network, the observed correlations remain clearly detectable and are consistently well-approximated by Gaussian statistics. This confirms that the two sources can be reliably synchronized not only in controlled laboratory conditions but also under real-world operating constraints.

</details>


### [131] [Iterative quantum algorithms for the minimum vertex cover problem based on continuous-time quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27915)
*Ruben Pariente Bassa,Finley A. Quinton,Franz G. Fuchs,Pascal Halffmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种保约束的量子-经典混合贪心框架，用于求解最小顶点覆盖（可对偶至最大独立集）。该方法利用投影Pauli-X算符在可行子空间内精确编码分层图上的连续时间量子行走，从全顶点配置出发传播振幅至更小覆盖，再基于边际概率或条件期望覆盖大小对顶点排序，指导递归贪心归约。在多个随机图族上，量子启发算法相比经典贪心基线取得更低的平均近似比，且能最优求解更多实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小顶点覆盖是经典NP-hard组合优化问题，现有量子方法多依赖罚项或变分训练，面临可行空间约束难以精确保持、训练开销大等挑战。作者旨在设计一种无需罚项和变分训练、天然保持约束、且电路深度与系统规模无关的量子增强贪心框架。

Method: 核心机制：利用投影Pauli-X项之和，该和在可行子空间内精确等同于可行覆盖分层图的邻接矩阵。该图连通，任意可行覆盖可通过单顶点翻转序列到达全顶点配置。从全顶点配置出发执行连续时间量子行走，振幅传播至包含更小覆盖的层。基于量子行走结果，采用两种排序策略指导递归贪心归约：一是边缘覆盖概率排序，二是固定候选顶点后的条件期望覆盖大小排序。最终在低深度Trotter化下近似实现，对有界度图每层电路深度与系统规模无关。

Result: 在多个随机图族上测试，使用独立校准集固定行走时间，量子启发算法相比对应经典贪心基线获得更低的平均近似比，且能最优求解更大比例的实例。其中条件能量策略在测试实例上表现最优，且在低深度Trotter化下保持接近精确连续时间极限的算法性能。

Conclusion: 该保约束混合框架为组合优化提供了一种结构感知的量子增强策略，无需罚项或变分训练，电路深度可控，展示了量子行走在经典贪心算法中的信息注入能力，为量子-经典协同求解NP-hard问题提供了新的实用路径。

Abstract: We introduce a constraint-preserving hybrid quantum-classical greedy framework for the minimum vertex cover problem, which extends directly to maximum independent set by bitwise complementation. The framework uses projected Pauli-X terms whose sum preserves the feasible subspace and acts within it exactly as the adjacency matrix of a layered graph of feasible covers. This graph is connected, so every feasible cover is linked to the configuration containing all vertices by a sequence of allowed single-vertex flips. Starting from this configuration, the corresponding continuous-time quantum walk propagates amplitude into layers containing progressively smaller covers. We rank vertices using either their marginal cover probabilities or the expected cover size obtained after fixing each candidate vertex in the cover, and use these rankings to guide recursive greedy reductions. Across several random-graph families, with walk times fixed using independent calibration ensembles, the quantum-informed algorithms achieve lower mean approximation ratios and solve a larger fraction of instances optimally than their corresponding classical greedy baselines. The conditioned-energy strategy performs best on the tested instances and retains algorithmic performance close to the exact continuous-time limit under low-depth Trotterisation. For bounded-degree graphs, each Trotter layer has circuit depth independent of system size, and the framework requires neither penalty terms nor variational training.

</details>


### [132] [Design of a Quantum Error Correction Decoder Exploiting Temporal Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27930)
*Leo Itoh,Takuya Kasamura,Hidetsugu Irie,Junichiro Kadomoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种利用时间并行性的三明治解码微架构，基于Union-Find算法实现，在码距d=21时平均延迟降低35%，同时保持约1.5%的逻辑错误阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算中，低延迟量子纠错(QEC)对于防止在综合征测量周期内错误快速累积至关重要。传统的批量Union-Find解码器延迟较高，需要更高效的解码方案。

Method: 提出一种新的微架构，利用时间并行性实现三明治解码(sandwich decoding)方法，基于Union-Find算法构建，并完成了ASIC实现。通过逻辑综合与仿真进行评估。

Result: 在码距d=21时，所提出的解码器相比传统批量Union-Find解码器平均延迟降低35%；在唯象噪声下，逻辑错误阈值保持在约1.5%，与批量方法相当。

Conclusion: 三明治解码微架构结合Union-Find算法能有效利用时间并行性显著降低解码延迟，同时不牺牲纠错能力，为低延迟容错量子计算提供了有前景的硬件方案。

Abstract: In the pursuit of fault-tolerant quantum computing, low-latency quantum error correction (QEC) is essential to prevent rapid error accumulation within the syndrome measurement cycle. In this work, we propose a microarchitecture that implements the sandwich decoding method using the Union-Find algorithm by exploiting temporal parallelism, and we present an ASIC implementation. Through logic synthesis and simulation, we show that the proposed decoder achieves an average latency reduction of 35% at code distance d = 21 compared with a conventional batch Union-Find decoder, while maintaining a comparable logical error threshold of approximately 1.5% under phenomenological noise.

</details>


### [133] [Complementary Matrix-Gated QKAN Fast-Weight Programmers for Quantum Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27945)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Tzung-Chi Huang,Prayag Tiwari,Chi-Sheng Chen,Chun-Hua Lin,Yu-Chao Hsu,Tai-Yue Li,Saif Al-Kuwari,Simon See,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子启发的KAN门控快速权重编程器（FWP）中标量门强制所有坐标共享同一记忆时间尺度的问题，提出自调制QKAN-FWP，引入互补矩阵门控（CMG），实现逐坐标的记忆控制，同时保留凸更新与仿射前缀扫描结构。在多个时序预测和量子动力学多步预测中，CMG显著优于标量门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子启发KAN门控快速权重编程器中，标量门对所有快速状态坐标施加统一的保留-写入平衡，无法实现逐坐标的差异化记忆时间尺度控制，限制了模型对长期依赖的灵活建模能力。

Method: 提出自调制QKAN-FWP，将广播式标量门替换为低秩生成的逐元素调制。核心创新为互补矩阵门控（CMG）：使用一个sigmoid矩阵门保留旧状态，其互补矩阵写入新提案，实现坐标级记忆控制，同时保持凸更新和仿射前缀扫描结构。比较了四种自调制规则与标量门控在四种FWP架构（组合经典与QKAN快/慢编程器）上的表现。

Result: 在7个单步预测基准和5种序列长度上，CMG对包含QKAN快速编程器的架构提升最一致。在CUDA-Q Dynamics模拟的Jaynes-Cummings和transmon-resonator动力学的多步预测中，CMG模型在4、8、16步预测视野上的MSE保持在0.001或更低，相比标量门控模型至少改善91.2%。

Conclusion: 逐坐标互补调制是QKAN-FWP稳定且有效的更新方式，CMG以单分支调制的开销实现了坐标级记忆控制，显著提升量子动力学等多步预测任务性能。

Abstract: Sequence models must decide what to write into memory and what to retain. In quantum and quantum-inspired sequence learning, nonlinear recurrent updates often require repeated circuit evaluations and sequential backpropagation through time, making long contexts costly. Gated fast-weight programmers (FWPs) based on quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (QKANs) alleviate this bottleneck by storing context in time-varying fast parameters. However, their scalar gate applies one retention-write balance to every fast-state coordinate, forcing all parameters to share a memory timescale. We introduce Self-Modulating QKAN-based FWPs, which replace this broadcast gate with low-rank-generated element-wise modulation of the new-proposal branch, a bounded old-state branch, or both. We further propose Complementary Matrix Gating (CMG), which uses one sigmoid matrix gate to retain the old state and its complement to write the new proposal. CMG provides coordinate-wise memory control while preserving the bounded convex update and affine prefix-scan structure of scalar gating, at the modulation-head cost of a single-branch rule. We compare four self-modulating rules with scalar gating across four FWP architectures combining classical and QKAN-based slow and fast programmers. Across seven single-step forecasting benchmarks and five sequence lengths, CMG gives the most consistent improvements for architectures whose fast programmer incorporates a QKAN-based module. In direct multi-step forecasting of Jaynes-Cummings and transmon-resonator dynamics simulated with CUDA-Q Dynamics, CMG models maintain mean-squared errors on the order of 0.001 or lower across forecasting horizons of 4, 8, and 16 steps, while improving on their scalar-gated counterparts by at least 91.2%. These results establish coordinate-wise complementary modulation as a stable and effective update for QKAN-based FWPs.

</details>


### [134] [A CPU+DCU Heterogeneous Parallel Framework for Post-Processing Reconstruction in Quantum Circuit Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27947)
*Qingqing Jiang,Weidong Liu,Yufu Liu,Ruiqing He,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CPU+DCU异构并行框架的量子电路切割后处理重构方法，通过高低字整数表示全局基态索引和三级协同存储机制，在百量子比特规模上实现了高达259倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ时代量子比特资源有限，量子电路切割虽能将大电路分解为小子电路，但将大量开销转移到了经典后处理阶段。随着电路规模、复杂度和切割数增加，重构成为主要的计算与存储瓶颈，现有方法要么构建稠密概率向量导致内存爆炸，要么仅返回高概率态丢失信息。

Method: 提出CPU+DCU异构并行重构框架，包括三个关键技术：(1) 从子电路测量结果直接重构原始输出分布中的非零概率态，而非构建完整概率向量；(2) 采用高低字整数表示方案处理超过64位的全局基态索引；(3) 设计三级协同存储机制，涵盖设备内存、主机内存和外存(out-of-core)。

Result: 在嵩山超级计算机上实验表明：线性簇态上相比优化后的串行基线加速达259倍，随机电路上相比同构CPU并行方法加速达4倍，同时保持高重构保真度，并能完成百量子比特规模的重构任务。

Conclusion: 面向HPC的异构重构方案能有效缓解经典后处理瓶颈并提升重构可扩展性，为NISQ时代大规模量子电路切割的实际应用提供了可行的后处理解决路径。

Abstract: In the NISQ era, limited qubit resources make it difficult to execute large quantum circuits directly on real hardware. Quantum circuit cutting mitigates this limitation by decomposing a large circuit into smaller subcircuits, but it shifts substantial overhead to classical post-processing. As circuit size, complexity, and cut count increase, reconstruction becomes a major computational and storage bottleneck. This paper presents a CPU+DCU heterogeneous parallel framework for circuit-cutting post-processing reconstruction. Instead of constructing a dense $2^n$-dimensional probability vector or returning only high-probability states, the framework reconstructs the nonzero-probability states in the original output distribution from subcircuit measurement results. It combines heterogeneous CPU+DCU execution with a high/low-word integer representation for global basis-state indices beyond 64 bits and a three-level cooperative storage mechanism spanning device memory, host memory, and out-of-core storage. Experiments on the Songshan supercomputer show that the framework maintains high reconstruction fidelity while achieving up to $259\times$ speedup over an optimized serial baseline on linear-cluster states and up to $4\times$ speedup over a homogeneous CPU-parallel method on random circuits. The framework can also complete reconstruction tasks at the hundred-qubit scale. These results demonstrate that HPC-oriented heterogeneous reconstruction can effectively alleviate the classical post-processing bottleneck and improve reconstruction scalability.

</details>


### [135] [Exponential Advantage of Multipartite Entanglement over Quantum Communication with Applications to Bounded-Storage Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27957)
*Ananya Chakraborty,Manik Banik,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了多体量子纠缠在通信中带来的指数优势：通过共享GHZ态，多方发送者只需对数级经典通信即可完成任务；而无预共享纠缠时，即使允许量子通信，也需多项式级通信量。此外，还给出了在密码学中两源随机性提取器上的指数分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体量子纠缠在通信任务中能否带来超越无纠缠量子通信的指数级优势，并将这种分离拓展到密码学场景。

Method: 基于二分Hidden Matching问题，设计了一个多方发送者与单一接收者的通信任务。分析在有共享GHZ态与无预共享纠缠（即使允许量子通信）两种设置下，完成该任务所需的最小通信复杂度，并给出上下界证明。进而应用于带种子的两源随机性提取器，比较纠缠与无纠缠量子边信息下的安全性。

Result: 共享GHZ态时，每个发送者只需O(log n)比特经典通信即可高概率完成任务；无预共享纠缠时，至少一个发送者需要多项式级通信，即使允许量子通信。应用于密码学：在有少量共享纠缠时，破解提取器只需指数级小的量子存储，而无纠缠则需多项式级存储。

Conclusion: 多体纠缠辅助的经典通信可以指数级优于无预共享纠缠的量子通信。这揭示了多体纠缠在通信复杂性和量子密码学中的独特威力，为纠缠作为资源在分布式计算和安全性中的核心地位提供了新证据。

Abstract: We establish an exponential communication advantage enabled by multipartite quantum entanglement. Building on the bipartite Hidden Matching problem, we introduce a communication task involving multiple spatially separated senders and a single receiver. We show that a shared Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state enables completion of this task using only logarithmically many bits of classical communication from each sender. In contrast, without preshared entanglement, any protocol achieving high success probability requires polynomial communication from at least one sender, even when \emph{quantum} communication is allowed. Thus, classical communication assisted by multipartite entanglement can be exponentially more powerful than quantum communication without preshared entanglement. As a cryptographic application, we construct a seeded two-source randomness extractor and establish an exponential separation between entangled and unentangled quantum side-information. Specifically, compromising the extractor with two unentangled quantum states storing information about the two sources, respectively, requires polynomial-size memory, whereas exponentially smaller quantum memory suffices in the presence of a small amount of shared entanglement.

</details>


### [136] [Towards Quantum Networks: Characterizing Raman Noise over Metropolitan-scale Fiber Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27970)
*Marcello Caleffi,Laura d'Avossa,Italo Ignacio Machuca Flores,Marco Grillo,Elena Montella,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实城域光纤网络中，O波段经典信号对C波段量子纠缠分发产生的拉曼散射噪声影响。通过在7公里城域光纤链路上的测量，发现实际部署光纤中存在局部频谱异常，需谨慎选择C波段工作频率以规避拉曼噪声，并给出了优化信道分配的实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动量子-经典信号共纤传输从实验室受控环境走向真实城域部署场景，揭示实际光纤网络中拉曼散射对量子纠缠分发的真实影响，为大规模量子网络部署提供依据。

Method: 在7公里城域光纤链路上，使用商用光源和窄带激光器，测量O波段经典信号在C波段产生的拉曼散射噪声；将实际部署光纤中的测量结果与实验室受控条件下的结果进行对比分析。

Result: 1）实验室与城域链路测量结果总体吻合良好，但实际部署光纤中观察到局部频谱异常；2）拉曼散射噪声是实际部署中量子信噪比的主导因素，超过背景噪声和探测器噪声；3）识别出受拉曼噪声影响较小的频谱区域，可作为量子信道分配的优选窗口。

Conclusion: 在C波段量子信号与O波段经典信号共纤传输时，必须根据实际光纤环境谨慎选择工作频率；研究为在现有光纤基础设施上部署稳健、可扩展的量子通信系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The coexistence of quantum and classical signals in optical fiber infrastructures represents a major challenge for large-scale quantum networks, as noise sources such as Raman scattering can significantly impact entanglement distribution, and the quantum protocols based on it. In this work, we analyze Raman scattering in the C-band, used for entanglement distribution, generated by a classical the O-band signal. The main contribution of this study lays in investigating these effects in a real metropolitan-fiber network, moving beyond controlled laboratory experiments to deployed telecommunication environments. Measurements are performed over a 7 km metropolitan fiber link using commercial sources and narrowband lasers. The experimental results shows good agreement between the measurements taken under laboratory conditions, although, within the metropolitan-scale loop, localized spectral anomalies are observed in the deployed fibers. Therefore, our results show that, whenever a quantum signal propagates in the C-band alongside an O-band classical channel within the same fiber, careful selection of the operating frequency is required, as Raman scattering and other real-world noise sources can significantly affect the quality and stability of the quantum transmission. In particular, we identify spectral regions that are less affected by Raman noise, thereby providing practical guidelines for optimal quantum channel allocation. We demonstrate that Raman-induced noise constitutes a dominant contribution to the quantum signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) in realistic deployments, beyond background and detector noise. Overall, our findings offer practical insights for deploying quantum communication systems over existing fiber networks, supporting the development of robust and scalable quantum infrastructures.

</details>


### [137] [Slow-light-enhanced Atomic Frequency Comb Quantum Memory in Stoichiometric EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28060)
*Zongfeng Li,Wanting Xiao,Mucheng Guo,Shuping Liu,Fudong Wang,Manjin Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在化学计量比EuCl₃·6D₂O晶体中实现的高效量子存储，利用其固有的高光学深度，在无需腔增强的条件下实现了经典光42.9%、弱相干脉冲34.4%的存储效率，以及90%的慢光存储效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土掺杂晶体在自由空间量子存储中的性能受限于低光学深度，传统解决方案需依赖复杂的光学腔。需要一种无需腔结构即可提供高光学密度的替代材料平台。

Method: 采用化学计量比EuCl₃·6D₂O晶体作为存储介质，利用其本征高光学密度；实验观测慢光效应（色散诱导回波延迟和精细度依赖的回波强度调制）；建立统一理论框架，阐释吸收与色散协同介导回波生成的机制。

Result: 实现了经典光42.9%、弱相干脉冲34.4%的存储效率，慢光存储效率达90%；观测到显著的慢光类效应，包括色散诱导的回波延迟和精细度依赖的回波强度调制。

Conclusion: EuCl₃·6D₂O被验证为一种鲁棒的固态量子存储平台，为可扩展固态量子存储器提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Rare-earth-doped crystals are promising candidates for quantum storage, yet their performance in free-space configurations is fundamentally restricted by low optical depth. Here, we demonstrate high-efficiency quantum storage in a stoichiometric EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O crystal, which intrinsically provides high optical density without the complexity of cavity implementation. We show that in this high-density regime, the system exhibits significant slow-light-like effects, including dispersion-induced echo delays and finesse-dependent echo intensity modulation. We develop a unified theoretical framework showing how absorption and dispersion work in concert to mediate echo generation. We achieve storage efficiencies of 42.9% for classical light and 34.4% for weak coherent pulses, alongside 90% efficiency for slow-light storage. These findings validate EuCl$_3 \cdot$ 6D$_2$O as a robust platform, establishing a viable pathway for scalable solid-state quantum memory.

</details>


### [138] [Deterministic QKD source robust against side-channel attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28063)
*Kiyoshi Tamaki,Marcos Curty,Akihiro Mizutani,Álvaro Navarrete*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种对侧信道攻击内在鲁棒的QKD光源方案，无需脉冲后选择或完美消光比器件，且不引入强度与编码比特/基之间的关联，从而实现更简洁的安全证明和显著更高的密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QKD系统的安全性因真实器件与理想模型之间的偏差而受损，源侧信道（尤其是特洛伊木马攻击）尤为严重：忽略它们危及安全性，而考虑它们则降低性能。现有被动式和无调制器方案需要脉冲后选择或完美消光比来抑制侧信道，存在局限性。

Method: 设计一种新型QKD光源，从物理机制上实现对侧信道攻击的内在鲁棒性。该方案避免了现有方案中的脉冲后选择、完美消光比要求，并消除了强度与编码比特/基之间的关联，从而可直接应用更简洁的安全证明。

Result: 该方案可获得显著更高的密钥率，且安全证明更为简洁直接。技术上具备当前可行性。

Conclusion: 该方案为实现实际安全的QKD提供了一条清晰可行的技术路径，有望弥合理论安全性与实际实现之间的差距。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) is secure in principle, but practical security can be undermined by discrepancies between real devices and the idealized models assumed in security proofs. Source side channels, including those exploited by Trojan-horse attacks, are particularly detrimental: neglecting them compromises implementation security, whereas accounting for them reduces performance. Here we propose a QKD source that is intrinsically robust against side-channel attacks. Unlike existing passive and modulator-free schemes, it requires neither post-selection of the emitted pulses nor devices with a perfect extinction ratio to suppress side channels, and it does not introduce correlations between the intensity and the encoded bit or basis. Consequently, simpler security proofs apply directly, yielding substantially higher key rates. Our proposal appears to be within reach of current technology and therefore provides a clear and practical path toward implementation-secure QKD.

</details>


### [139] [Approximate sampling from decoded quantum interferometry via Markov chain Monte Carlo methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28120)
*Elies Gil-Fuster,Matan Ninio,Lennart Bittel,Yishai Shimoni,Jens Eisert,Stefan Woerner,Almudena Carrera Vázquez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子干涉解码（DQI）优化性能与经典采样的对比。作者首先给出了DQI期望性能与二项式统计的解析联系，然后利用MCMC（特别是块吉布斯采样）从DQI诱导分布中采样，在max-XORSAT（超1000有效量子比特）和OPI（超150有效量子比特）问题上进行数值实验。结果表明MCMC能可靠复现DQI的近似比；在OPI问题上，MCMC运行时间约按1.1^n指数增长，对DQI声称的超多项式优势提出了更细致的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化问题是量子优势的重要候选领域，DQI被提出用于近似优化并与经典解码问题关联。但此前工作主要关注DQI的理论复杂度，对其实际经验性能与经典算法的对比知之甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究经典采样方法是否能模拟DQI的优化能力。

Method: 1. 解析分析：将DQI期望性能与二项式统计建立简化联系，并识别进一步研究DQI复杂度的具体障碍。2. 数值实验：利用DQI输出概率可高效计算的特点，采用MCMC方法（块吉布斯采样）从DQI诱导分布中采样。3. 基准测试：在两个优化问题（max-XORSAT和OPI）上系统评估MCMC运行时间的标度律。

Result: 1. MCMC算法能够在广泛的问题规模范围内可靠达到DQI期望的近似比。2. 在max-XORSAT问题上达到超1000有效量子比特，在OPI问题上达到超150有效量子比特。3. 在OPI中DQI声称具有超多项式优势的区域，MCMC经验运行时间约按1.1^n标度，呈指数增长但底数较小。

Conclusion: 研究结果并未直接驳斥现有的量子优势声明，但提供了新的经验证据表明经典采样算法能够紧密匹配DQI的优化性能，对DQI的实际优势提供了更细致、更平衡的视角。

Abstract: Optimization problems are among the leading candidates for industrially relevant quantum advantage. Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) has been proposed to tackle approximate optimization, establishing a connection to classical decoding problems. While previous work has primarily focused on the theoretical complexity of DQI, comparatively little is known about its empirical performance relative to classical algorithms. In this work, we shed further light on the complexity of DQI and investigate numerically whether classical sampling methods can emulate the optimization capabilities of DQI. We first present a simplified analytical characterization of DQI that connects its expected performance to binomial statistics, and we identify concrete obstacles in further studying the complexity of DQI. Exploiting the fact that DQI output probabilities are efficiently computable, we apply Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques, particularly block-Gibbs sampling, to sample from the induced distribution. We study the runtime scaling of these methods for two optimization problems called max-XORSAT, where we reach beyond $1000$ effective qubits; and OPI, where we reach beyond $150$ effective qubits. Our results show that MCMC algorithms can reliably attain the approximation ratios expected from DQI across a broad range of problem sizes. In OPI, in the regime where a super-polynomial advantage is claimed for DQI, we observe an empirical runtime for MCMC that scales approximately as $1.1^{n}$, indicating exponential growth with a comparatively small base. Our findings do not refute existing quantum advantage claims but provide new empirical evidence that classical sampling algorithms can closely match DQI's optimization performance, offering a more nuanced perspective on the practical advantage of DQI.

</details>


### [140] [Efficient atom rearrangements for quantum error correction primitives with a single AOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28149)
*Tom Hartweg,Asier Piñeiro Orioli,Hugo Perrin,Samuel Crew*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为中性质子量子计算机引入新的二维阵列几何变换原语（剪切、旋转、反射），利用单一动态交叉声光偏转器对在静态晶格中扫描实现。通过（负）二进制与几何分解，将AOD扫描次数从$O(d^2)$降至$O(\log d)$，大幅提升了逻辑操作吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机通过原子输运实现任意连接性，部分逻辑操作可简化为原子的几何重排。最小化这些移动的持续时间对高逻辑吞吐量至关重要。然而，逐原子重排方案需要$O(d^2)$次AOD扫描和$O(d^{7/3})$的时间开销，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出三种新的几何变换原语——剪切（shear）、旋转（rotate）和反射（reflect），利用一对动态交叉声光偏转器（AOD）在静态晶格上进行扫描操作。核心技巧包括：(1) 使用（负）二进制分解和几何分解来减少AOD扫描次数；(2) 引入Paeth分解实现$90^{\circ}$旋转，将旋转分解为一系列剪切操作。最终AOD扫描次数与阵列线尺寸呈对数关系。

Result: 在旋转表面码中实现横向Hadamard门时，对于距离为$d$的编码，所需的AOD扫描次数降至$3\lfloor\log_2(d-1)\rfloor + 4$次，恒定加加速度时间为$O(d^{1/3})$。相比之下，逐原子重排需要$O(d^2)$次扫描和$O(d^{7/3})$时间。

Conclusion: 利用新的几何变换原语和分解策略，可将二维原子阵列的全局几何操作从二次复杂度降至对数复杂度，显著提升中性原子量子计算机上逻辑操作的执行效率，为大规模容错量子计算提供了更优的原子重排方案。

Abstract: Neutral-atom quantum computers offer arbitrary connectivity enabled by atom transport. Some logical operations can then be simplified or reduced entirely to geometric rearrangements of the atoms. Minimizing the duration of these movements is therefore essential for high logical throughput. We introduce new primitives to shear, rotate and reflect 2D arrays of atoms in a static lattice using sweeps of a single dynamic crossed acousto-optic deflector (AOD) pair. Using (nega-)binary and geometric decompositions, we achieve an AOD stroke count scaling logarithmically in the linear size of the array. In one example, we use the Paeth decomposition to implement a $90^{\circ}$ rotation for a transversal Hadamard gate in a rotated surface code of distance $d$ in $3\lfloor\log_2(d-1)\rfloor + 4$ AOD strokes and $O(d^{1/3})$ constant-jerk time, against $O(d^2)$ strokes and $O(d^{7/3})$ time for atom-by-atom rearrangement.

</details>


### [141] [Entanglement Swapping with Integrated Narrowband Photon Sources for Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28184)
*Mingsong Wu,Leili Esmaeilifar,Rui Ning Wang,Tobias Kippenberg,Rob Thew,Tiff Brydges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了使用窄带集成光子源实现的纠缠交换实验，获得了0.99的HOM可见度和0.88的交换态可见度，并利用全光纤商用组件实现了跨1.6 THz的相位和频率稳定，解决了量子中继器实际部署中的关键技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一代量子中继器需要与光子源接口的原子量子存储器，但集成光子学难以产生量子存储器所需的窄带光子，且窄带光子在纠缠交换中面临系统级稳定化的技术挑战。本文旨在解决这些基础性和技术性难题。

Method: 采用先进的集成光子源产生与多种原子量子存储器平台带宽兼容的窄带光子对；使用两个独立泵浦激光器（频率差异显著）分别驱动每对光子源，模拟不同中继器节点间的纠缠交换；利用全光纤商用组件实现跨1.6 THz的相位和频率稳定。

Result: 获得背景扣除后的净HOM可见度为0.99±0.01，表明光子具有高不可区分性和纯度；净交换态可见度V=0.88±0.06，足以违反贝尔不等式，证明了最终纠缠的质量。

Conclusion: 该工作从窄带光子对的集成光子方案到独立量子中继器节点间的系统级稳定化，全面解决了场部署量子中继器中的关键挑战，为实现光纤基、场部署的量子中继器运行铺平了道路。

Abstract: Promising implementations of first generation quantum repeaters are predicted to require atomic-based quantum memory systems interfaced with photonic sources. Integrated photonics provides a promising solution for fibre-based, field-deployed operation of quantum repeaters, however many leading quantum memory platforms require narrow-bandwidth photons that are challenging to generate with integrated photonics. Narrowband photons also present significant technical challenges when implementing entanglement-swapping, particularly with regards to systems-level stabilisation. This work addresses some of these fundamental and technical challenges, by demonstrating entanglement-swapping using state-of-the-art integrated photon sources with bandwidths compatible with multiple atomic-based quantum memory platforms. We obtained a background-subtracted (net) HOM visibility of 0.99$\,\pm\,$0.01, showing high photon indistinguishability and purity, with a net swapped state visibility of $\mathcal{V}$=0.88$\,\pm\,$0.06 demonstrating that the final entanglement would be sufficient to violate a Bell inequality. The experiment used independent pump lasers for each photon pair source, with highly different frequencies to mimic entanglement swapping between different repeater nodes or platforms. Phase and frequency stabilisation spanning 1.6 THz was achieved using all-fibre, commercially-available components. These results address important challenges in implementing field-deployed quantum repeaters, from the integrated photonic solutions for narrowband photon pairs, to systems-level stabilisation between independent quantum repeater nodes.

</details>


### [142] [Purifications for Convex Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28202)
*Felix Campidell,Tim Netzer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限维真凸锥的几何框架下研究量子理论中的纯化原理，证明了不可分解锥及包含最大纠缠态的中间张量积锥的纯化存在性定理，特别地，不可分解齐次锥的每个内点都存在纯化。文中还给出了局部自同构意义下唯一性的判据，并讨论了边界上单纯面条件对纯化可能性的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯化原理在量子理论和广义概率论中具有重要意义。作者旨在摆脱具体理论框架，仅利用有限维真凸锥的纯几何结构来研究纯化的存在性与唯一性问题，从而为理解纯化提供更为普遍和基础的几何视角。

Method: 采用有限维真凸锥的几何方法：1) 证明不可分解锥及含有最大纠缠态的中间张量积锥中内点纯化的存在性定理；2) 利用局部自同构群刻画纯化的唯一性判据；3) 分析边界上锥面的单纯性（simplicial faces）条件对纯化可能性的约束。以正半定锥、Lorentz锥、k-正映射、PPT张量及多面体锥等为例进行验证。

Result: 1) 不可分解齐次锥的每个内点均存在纯化（如Lorentz锥满足该条件）；2) 给出了纯化在局部自同构下唯一性的判别准则；3) 在锥的每个真面均为单纯面的条件下，仅有纯点（pure points）可被纯化，且该结论在存在非单纯面时失效；4) 通过多个具体实例展示了存在性与非唯一性现象。

Conclusion: 纯化原理可以从量子理论的具体框架中抽离，提升为有限维真凸锥几何的内在性质。不可分解齐次锥的内点纯化存在性、局部自同构下的唯一性判据以及单纯面条件对边界纯化的限制，共同构成了纯化的几何理论基础，为正半定锥、Lorentz锥等多种锥类中的纯化现象提供了统一解释。

Abstract: Motivated by the importance of the purification principle in quantum theory and generalized probabilistic theories, we study purifications using only the geometry of a finite-dimensional proper convex cone. We prove an existence theorem for indecomposable cones and intermediate tensor cones containing the maximally entangled state; in particular, every interior point of an indecomposable homogeneous cone admits a purification. This applies to Lorentz cones, for example. We also give a criterion for uniqueness up to local automorphisms. On the boundary, we show that if every proper face of $C$ is simplicial, then only pure points can admit purifications, and we demonstrate that this conclusion fails in the presence of non-simplicial faces. Examples involving positive semidefinite cones, Lorentz cones, $k$-positive maps, PPT tensors, and polyhedral cones illustrate both existence and non-uniqueness phenomena.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Arago-Fresnel interference of displaced spin states of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28231)
*Yan Zhang,Xiang Liu,Hou Ian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了阿拉戈-菲涅尔干涉定律在量子光学框架下的表现，通过计算由相干态与位移单光子自旋态叠加而成的偏振光束的斯托克斯参数，揭示了光子螺旋度如何产生可区分的干涉条纹，并提出了一种纯光学方法来确定未知入射光子的自旋态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉戈-菲涅尔四条定律奠定了光的偏振概念的历史里程碑。本文旨在利用非经典态的现代描述，重新研究光子螺旋度在宏观阿拉戈-菲涅尔干涉中产生的效应，探索量子态叠加下的干涉行为。

Method: 将一束规则相干态（从真空位移）与一束位移单光子自旋态（从左旋或右旋光子态位移）组合成偏振光束，计算其斯托克斯参数。通过分析自旋取向及其相对于位移相干态偏振方向的非对称性，研究参数依赖关系对干涉条纹的影响。

Result: 光子自旋取向及其与位移相干态偏振方向之间的相对非对称性，产生了区分性的参数依赖关系，进而形成可分辨的干涉条纹。基于此，建立了一种纯光学的量子干涉测量方法，可用于确定未知入射光子的自旋态。

Conclusion: 该量子干涉测量方案不仅在理论上扩展了经典阿拉戈-菲涅尔定律的适用范围至非经典光态，还为光子自旋态的光学测定提供了无需电子学探测的纯光学新途径。

Abstract: The four laws by Arago and Fresnel distinguish the coplanarity of two light beams to determine their capacity of interference, laying the historic milestone for conceptualizing the polarization of light. Equipped with modern descriptions of non-classical states, we re-investigate the macroscopic Arago-Fresnel interference producible by photon helicities. To this end, we compute the Stokes parameter of a polarized beam combined from a regular coherent state (displaced from the vacuum) and a displaced single-photon spin state (displaced from either a left- or right-spin state of photon). The spin orientation, together with its relative asymmetry with respect to the polarizing orientation of the displacing coherent state, produces distinguishing parameter dependences and thus distict interference fringes. Conversely, this quantum interferometry establishes a purely optical method to determine the spin of an unknown incident photon.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal T Counts under Sparsity: from QROM to State Preparation and Block Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28260)
*Tongyang Li,Fengning Ou,Xinzhao Wang,Penghui Yao,Pei Yuan,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏量子只读存储器（QROM）的T门计数复杂度，证明了其上下界均为Θ(√(sm) + √(sn))，其中s为支持大小，m为消息长度。结果通过多层哈希方案和基于自适应Clifford+T电路计数论证得到，并推广至稀疏态制备和稀疏矩阵块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子算法需要将经典数据相干地加载到量子态中，QROM是实现这一目标的核心原语。然而，现有研究主要关注稠密QROM，对稀疏QROM（即只有s个地址存储非零数据）的T门复杂度尚缺乏系统分析。T门是实现容错量子计算中最昂贵的资源之一，因此精确刻画稀疏QROM的T门下界具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Method: 上界构造：采用多层哈希方案，通过层级化地址映射高效实现稀疏数据的量子访问。下界证明：将稀疏QROM归约为态制备问题，并利用自适应Clifford+T电路的计数论证方法推导下界。该方法允许中电路测量和经典控制操作，所得下界具有广泛适用性。

Result: 1. 证明了稀疏QROM的T门复杂度紧界为Θ(√(sm) + √(sn))，与支持大小s和消息长度m的平方根成正比。2. 将上述结果推广至s-稀疏态制备，获得T门紧界Θ(√(sn) + √(s log(1/ε)) + log(1/ε))。3. 对s-稀疏矩阵的块编码，得到T门紧界Θ(√(2^n s n) + √(2^n s log(s/ε_BE)) + log(s/ε_BE))。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统建立了稀疏QROM的T门复杂度理论，上下界在渐进意义下匹配，且下界即使在中电路测量和经典控制操作下仍然成立，体现了结果的鲁棒性。这些紧界为评估和优化基于稀疏QROM的量子算法提供了理论基准。

Abstract: Many quantum algorithms require coherent access to classical data, often modeled by quantum read-only memory (QROM). We initiate the study of the $T$ count of sparse QROM, in which only $s$ of the $2^n$ addresses store nonzero data. We prove asymptotically optimal $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sm} + \sqrt{sn})$ with square-root dependence on the support size $s$ and message length $m$. Our upper bounds use a multilevel hashing scheme, while our lower bounds reduce sparse QROM to state preparation and use counting arguments for adaptive Clifford+$T$ circuits. The lower bounds thus hold even when mid-circuit measurements and classically controlled operations are allowed. As applications, we obtain matching $T$-count bounds $Θ(\sqrt{sn} + \sqrt{s\log(1/\varepsilon)} +
  \log(1/\varepsilon))$ for $s$-sparse state preparation and $Θ( \sqrt{2^n sn}
  +
  \sqrt{2^n s\log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}})}
  +
  \log(s/\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}))$ for block encoding of $s$-sparse matrices, where $\varepsilon$ and $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{BE}}$ are the precision of state preparation and block encoding, respectively.

</details>


### [145] [No-Go Theorems for Quantum Transport Metrics from Fixed Cost Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28358)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Tianshi Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于耦合的量子最优传输中，对于任意维度 d≥3，不存在固定的成本算子使得最优传输成本或其平方根成为量子态空间上的度量。对于信道诱导耦合，非负性和自成本为零的条件迫使成本算子本身为零，无法实现点分离。这些否定性结果统一表明固定成本耦合框架无法在整个量子态空间上导出度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedland 等人曾猜想与 SWAP 投影子相关的平方根最优成本在所有维度上都是度量，且该性质对邻近的量子成本矩阵也成立。Miller 通过构造显式的 qutrit 反例反驳了这两个猜想。本文在此基础上进一步追问：是否存在任何固定成本算子能使最优传输成本（或其平方根）成为量子态空间上的度量？

Method: 作者采用标准耦合框架和信道诱导耦合框架两种设定。对于标准耦合，在 d≥3 时构造了对易态之间的反例，证明任何固定成本算子都无法使最优成本或其平方根满足度量公理（特别是指派性和三角不等式）。进一步证明即使对 SWAP 成本做稳定化处理，障碍依然存在。对于信道诱导耦合，利用全局非负性和自成本为零的约束，直接推导出成本算子必须为零，从而在 d≥2 时无法实现点分离。

Result: (1) 在 d≥3 时，不存在固定成本算子使最优成本或平方根最优成本成为度量，违反情况已出现在对易态之间；(2) SWAP 成本的稳定化无法消除此障碍；(3) 信道诱导耦合下，非负性和自成本为零迫使成本算子为零，d≥2 时无法区分不同态。

Conclusion: 固定成本耦合形式的量子最优传输无法在整个量子态空间上导出真正的度量结构。这些否定性结果划定了基于耦合的量子最优传输方法的根本局限，提示需要探索超越固定成本算子的新框架来在量子态空间上构建有意义的度量。

Abstract: Coupling-based quantum optimal transport generalizes classical optimal transport by representing transport plans as bipartite states with prescribed marginals and evaluating their cost as the expectation of a fixed Hermitian operator. Friedland et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 110402 (2022)] conjectured that the square root of the optimal cost associated with the SWAP projector is a metric in every dimension and that this property persists for nearby quantum cost matrices. Miller [arXiv:2607.07764] disproved both conjectures by constructing explicit diagonal qutrit counterexamples. Building on his analysis, we prove a uniform no-go theorem for standard couplings. In every dimension $d\geq3$, no fixed cost operator makes either the optimal cost or its square root a metric, with violations occurring already among commuting states. The obstruction persists under stabilization of the SWAP cost. For channel-induced couplings, global nonnegativity and vanishing self-cost force the cost operator to be zero, precluding point separation when $d\geq2$. Taken together, these no-go results show that fixed-cost coupling formulations do not lead to metrics on the full quantum state space.

</details>


### [146] [Noise-aware emulation and cross-device validation of neutral atom analog quantum processing units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28364)
*Constantin Dalyac,Sergi Julià-Farré,Lucas Leclerc,Vittorio Vitale,Boris Albrecht,Lucas Béguin,Kemal Bidzhiev,Petru Borta,Clémence Briosne-Frejaville,Daniel J. Campbell,Dorian Claveau,Makrem Chatti,Antoine Cornillot,Julius de Hond,Anita Devi,Thomas Eritzpokhoff,Gaétan Hercé,Fergus Hayes,Soufiane Kaghad,Arun Kumar Abhimanyu,Lucas Lassablière,Mauro Mendizabal,Anton Quelle,Julien Ripoll,Henrique Silvério,Joseph Vovrosh,Guillaume Villaret,Adrien Signoles,Alexandre Dauphin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种噪声感知的模拟量子处理器仿真框架，能够在Rydberg原子阵列平台上传播主要噪声机制，实现对设备行为的预测。通过在三个Pasqal量子处理器上对量子退火和淬火后动力学两种协议进行基准测试验证，测量可观测量均落在仿真器预测的不确定性包络内，该框架还能隔离各操作区域的主导物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于Rydberg原子阵列的模拟量子处理器日益普及，需要建立对其输出的置信度。现有方法缺乏将微观硬件缺陷与经验结果定量连接的预测模型，因而亟需一个能够全计算周期传播噪声的仿真框架，以验证设备行为并为算法设计和硬件改进提供指导。

Method: 提出噪声感知仿真框架，将主导噪声机制（包括激光强度噪声、原子位置涨落、退相干等）在整个计算周期中进行传播。在三个Pasqal量子处理器上，采用量子退火和淬火后动力学两种代表性协议进行基准测试，以经典可模拟区域的地面真值作为参照，对比预测不确定性包络与实测可观测量。

Result: 在全部三台设备上，实测可观测量均落在仿真器预测的不确定性包络范围内。框架不仅复现了实验数据，还能隔离出每个操作区间内起主导作用的物理机制。

Conclusion: 该噪声感知仿真框架为模拟量子处理器提供了可预测、可解释的验证工具，能够在经典可及范围内建立置信度，并为超出经典计算能力范围的计算体系验证奠定基础，同时为算法设计与硬件优化提供定量指导。

Abstract: Analog quantum processors based on Rydberg atom arrays are a powerful platform for many-body quantum simulation, combinatorial optimization, and graph machine learning. As these devices become increasingly accessible, establishing confidence in their outputs requires predictive models that quantitatively connect microscopic hardware imperfections to empirical results. Here, we present a noise-aware emulation framework that propagates the dominant noise mechanisms throughout the full computation cycle to predict device behavior. We validate the framework by benchmarking two representative protocols, quantum annealing and post-quench dynamics, on three Pasqal quantum processors where classical simulations still provide ground truth. Across all three devices, the measured observables fall within the uncertainty envelopes predicted by the emulator. Beyond reproducing the data, the framework isolates which physical mechanism dominates in each operating regime, provides quantitative guidance for algorithm design and hardware improvements, and establishes a foundation for verifying analog processors in regimes beyond classical reach.

</details>


### [147] [Emergence of a Macroscopic Cat State and Multi-Channel Entanglement in a Frustrated Cluster Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28373)
*Mohit Lal Bera,Andreu Anglés-Castillo,Alberto Acevedo Meléndez,Rafael Gómez-Lurbe,Luca Ion,Rodrigo M. Sanz,Somayeh Mehrabankar,Tanmoy Pandit,Carmen G. Almudéver,Armando Pérez,Miguel Angel Garcia-March*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一维阻挫自旋链模型，融合了团簇-伊辛和各向异性次近邻伊辛相互作用，揭示了铁磁相与非公度相之间的一阶量子相变，并探讨了宏观猫态在量子计量学中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于历史视角，将团簇-伊辛模型与各向异性次近邻伊辛模型结合，系统研究一维阻挫自旋链中竞争相互作用诱导的量子相与相变，填补对该混合模型基态性质认识的空白。

Method: 构建一维混合自旋链模型，结合解析分析与数值计算，研究两相的基态性质：通过施密特系数分析纠缠结构，考察能隙在热力学极限下的行为，并利用序参量和关联函数区分量子相。

Result: 发现两个量子相：铁磁相存在宏观猫态，能隙在热力学极限下关闭，有两个主导施密特系数；非公度相为有能隙相，能隙在热力学极限下保持有限，有四个主导施密特系数对应四条纠缠通道。两相之间由一阶量子相变分隔，非公度相因竞争相互作用未能形成拓扑相，但保留了大的非公度量子关联，本质上不同于简单顺磁体。

Conclusion: 该混合模型展现出丰富的量子现象：铁磁相的宏观猫态可用于量子计量学；非公度相是一种新型有能隙相，具有四条显著纠缠通道。模型具有良好的实验可行性。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional frustrated spin chain, which combines cluster-Ising and anisotropic next-nearest neighbor Ising models. We first offer a historical perspective that justifies the studied model. Then we study in detail the two quantum phases and prove that they are separated by a first order quantum phase transition. On one side, the ground state corresponds to a ferromagnetic phase, shows the presence of macroscopic cat states, and a small gap that closes in the thermodynamic limit. On the other phase, competing interactions avoid the establishment of a topological phase, though it conserves large incommensurate quantum correlations. We prove it is fundamentally distinct from a simple paramagnet, and we name it an incommensurate phase. This is a gapped phase, which gap does not close in the thermodynamic limit. While in the ferromagnetic phase there are two dominant Schmidt coefficients, in the incommensurate phase there are four. This corresponds to four distinct bipartite entanglement channels contributing substantially to the ground state. Finally, we discuss the utility of the macroscopic cat states for quantum metrology applications and the experimental feasibility of the system.

</details>


### [148] [Counterdiabatic Driving under Variational Frame Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28383)
*Boxi Li,Franco Nori,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分修饰框架下的通用反绝热驱动映射方法，通过在修饰表象中引入自由辅助生成元，使校正控制始终限制在实验室可用的物理操作内，从而克服传统反绝热驱动难以物理实现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反绝热(CD)驱动虽能加速绝热过程，但其推导出的辅助控制场往往无法映射到实际物理系统中可用的操控手段（如固定的耦合项或有限的调控自由度），这严重制约了CD驱动的实验实现。现有方法或依赖瞬时本征态构造，或局限于绝热表象，缺乏一个系统性的非微扰框架来保证校正项的物理可实现性。

Method: 作者在变分修饰表象（variational dressed frame）中重新表述CD驱动：引入一个不受约束的辅助生成元来重塑有效绝热问题，同时强制校正项始终限定在实验室原生控制（native laboratory controls）内。由此导出一个实验室表象下的对易子方程，无需构造瞬时本征态、绝热演化算符或修饰表象幺正算符即可求解。修饰表象的额外自由度揭示了传统绝热表象中不可达的新解。

Result: 通过三个实例验证了该机制：(1)单正交驱动下多量子比特链中旁观者误差的抑制；(2)固定纠缠相互作用下绝热Bell态制备的解析加速；(3)简并三脚架流形中快速绝热holonomic门实现，且校正脉冲限制在原生耦合内。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建物理可实现的CD协议提供了一个系统性的非微扰框架，突破了传统绝热表象的限制，在量子控制领域具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving accelerates adiabatic protocols by prescribing auxiliary control fields, but often fails to map them to physically available operations. We derive a general formalism for such a mapping. We formulate CD driving in a variational dressed frame, where an unconstrained auxiliary generator reshapes the effective adiabatic problem while simultaneously forcing the applied correction to stay restricted to the native laboratory controls. This yields a laboratory-frame commutator equation that can be solved without constructing instantaneous eigenstates or the adiabatic and dressed-frame unitaries. The additional dressed-frame freedom reveals solutions that are inaccessible in the conventional adiabatic frame. We illustrate this mechanism in three settings: suppression of spectator errors in a multi-qubit chain driven by a single quadrature, an analytical acceleration of adiabatic Bell-state preparation with a fixed entangling interaction, and implementation of a fast adiabatic holonomic gate in a degenerate tripod manifold with correction pulses confined to the native couplings. Our results provide a systematic nonperturbative framework for constructing implementable counterdiabatic protocols beyond the conventional adiabatic frame.

</details>


### [149] [Nearly optimal quantum circuits for Boolean oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28402)
*Junhong Nie,Wei Zi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对三类布尔函数的量子预言机电路，给出了电路规模、深度与辅助量子比特数之间的近似最优权衡方案，所有边界在对应辅助量子比特范围内均达到对数因子内的渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔函数的量子预言机是连接经典算法与量子算法的核心桥梁，但此前针对此类预言机的量子电路优化研究尚属空白。需要填补这一空白，为将经典过程嵌入量子电路（如QROM实现和量子算法设计）提供理论基础。

Method: 作者针对三类布尔函数分别构造量子预言机电路：一般全布尔函数（输出规模b）、有效支撑规模为d的部分布尔函数，以及真输入规模为d的稀疏布尔函数。对每一类函数，在给定辅助量子比特数m的不同范围内，分析并给出电路规模和深度的上界，并证明这些上界在相应区域内达到对数因子下的渐近最优。

Result: 得到了三类函数的完整权衡关系：(1)一般全布尔函数，m∈[1, Θ(2ⁿ/n)]，规模O(b·2ⁿ/log(n+m))，深度O(b·2ⁿ/(n+m))；(2)部分布尔函数，m∈[Θ(log d), Θ(d)]，规模O(n log d+bd)，深度O(n log n log d/(n+m)+log n+d(log d+b log m)/m)；(3)稀疏布尔函数，m∈[Θ(log n+log d), Θ(nd/log d)]，规模O(n² log d+nd/log(log d+m/n))，深度O(n² log n log d/(n+m)+log n+nd/m)。

Conclusion: 首次系统给出了三类布尔函数量子预言机的电路规模-深度-辅助比特数的近似最优权衡。这些结果有望在QROM实现和量子算法设计中得到应用，尤其适用于需要将经典子程序嵌入量子电路的场景。

Abstract: Quantum oracle of Boolean functions is one of the central bridges between classical and quantum algorithms, but the study focusing at quantum circuit optimization of such oracle is yet closed. In this paper, we propose nearly optimal tradeoffs among circuit size, circuit depth and ancilla count, for quantum oracles of three kinds of Boolean functions:
  general total Boolean functions with output size $b$: with $1\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{2^n}{n}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{\log(n+m)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{b2^n}{n+m}\right)$;
  partial Boolean functions of effective support size $d$ and output size $b$: with $Θ\left(\log d\right)\le m\le Θ\left(d\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n\log d+bd\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{d(\log d+b\log m)}{m}\right)$;
  sparse Boolean functions of true input size $d$: with $Θ\left(\log n+\log d\right)\le m\leΘ\left(\frac{nd}{\log d}\right)$ ancilla, size $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\log d+\frac{nd}{\log(\log d+m/n)}\right)$, depth $\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n^2\log n\log d}{n+m}+\log n+\frac{nd}{m}\right)$.
  All the size and depth bounds are asymptotically optimal up to logarithmic factors in the corresponding ancilla count regions. We hope these results find applications in scenarios where classical procedures are needed to be embedded into quantum circuits, such as QROM implementation and quantum algorithm design.

</details>


### [150] [Improved Convergence of Carleman-Embedded Quantum Algorithm for the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28426)
*Matthew Christensen,Tom Goffrey,Animesh Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Carleman嵌入量子算法的收敛区间扩展至Vlasov-Poisson方程，利用Fourier-Hermite展开和碰撞频率，通过解析与数值下界建立了收敛性，并揭示了碰撞频率强度需随速度分辨率增加的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carleman嵌入方法用于将非线性Vlasov-Poisson系统线性化以便量子计算机求解，但其收敛性条件尚未充分建立。本文旨在扩展收敛区间，使该方法在物理上合理的碰撞频率下可行，从而推动量子算法在动理学等离子体模拟中的实际应用。

Method: 采用位移相空间分布函数的Fourier-Hermite展开，结合解析推导与数值下界，分析Carleman嵌入后的Vlasov-Poisson系统的收敛性；并针对一大类基函数，研究碰撞频率强度与速度分辨率之间的标度关系。

Result: (1) 在物理合理的碰撞频率下，通过解析与数值下界验证了收敛性；(2) 对于一大类基函数，Carleman嵌入Vlasov-Poisson系统的收敛要求碰撞频率强度随速度分辨率提高而增加；(3) 量子算法的复杂度强烈依赖于输出类型是时间平均量还是时间分辨量。

Conclusion: Carleman嵌入量子算法求解Vlasov-Poisson方程的收敛性可在合理碰撞频率下实现，但收敛条件对速度分辨率存在约束；量子算法的实际可行性取决于所需的时态分辨率，这对未来量子等离子体模拟的设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: We extend the regime of convergence of Carleman-embedded quantum algorithms that solve the Vlasov-Poisson equations from kinetic plasma physics. We establish convergence, using both analytical and numerical lower bounds, for physically reasonable collision frequencies using a Fourier-Hermite expansion of the shifted phase-space distribution function. We also show that for a large class of basis functions, the convergence of the Carleman-embedded Vlasov-Poisson system requires increasing collision frequency strength with velocity resolution. The complexity of the quantum algorithm depends strongly on whether we seek time-averaged or -resolved outputs.

</details>


### [151] [Classical Tensor Network and Quantum Fourier Transform Approaches for Large-Scale Carr-Madan Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28435)
*Sascha Hauck,Ivica Turkalj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用张量网络重构Carr-Madan期权定价框架，采用超级快速傅里叶变换（SFFT）——即量子傅里叶变换（QFT）的压缩张量列表示，在不显式构建指数级大向量或傅里叶算子的情况下实现期权定价。该方法在保持精度的同时显著降低内存需求，并实现次指数计算扩展，同时建立了经典傅里叶定价、张量网络算法与量子计算之间的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典傅里叶方法在期权定价中应用广泛，但随着离散化精度提高，传统FFT的内存需求呈指数增长，成为大规模定价问题的计算瓶颈。需要一种既能保持精度又能避免指数级内存扩展的替代方案。

Method: 将Carr-Madan期权定价框架用张量网络重新表述，采用超级快速傅里叶变换（SFFT），即量子傅里叶变换（QFT）的压缩张量列（Tensor Train）表示。直接将SFFT应用于张量化的期权定价中，避免显式构造指数级大的向量或傅里叶算子。同时在量子模拟器和量子硬件上实现基于QFT的期权定价，进行直接对比。

Result: 对欧式看涨期权的数值实验表明，SFFT方法在保持定价精度的同时，大幅降低了内存需求，并实现了相比传统FFT的次指数计算扩展。量子模拟与硬件执行结果表明，经典张量网络与QFT量子方法均避免了传统傅里叶实现的指数扩展，为大规模期权定价提供了互补视角。

Conclusion: 研究成果建立了连接经典傅里叶定价、张量网络算法和量子计算方法的统一框架，证明了张量化傅里叶方法可为高维金融计算提供可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Fourier-based methods are among the most widely used techniques for pricing European options when the characteristic function of the underlying asset process is available. Their applicability to increasingly fine discretizations, however, is limited by the rapidly growing memory requirements of classical Fourier transforms, which become a computational bottleneck for large-scale pricing problems.
  In this work, we overcome this limitation by reformulating the Carr-Madan pricing framework using tensor networks. Specifically, we employ the Superfast Fourier Transform (SFFT), a compressed Tensor Train representation of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), and apply it directly to tensorized option pricing without ever explicitly constructing exponentially large vectors or Fourier operators. This formulation also enables a direct comparison between the classical tensor network algorithm and its quantum counterpart through QFT-based option pricing on quantum simulators and quantum hardware. Numerical experiments for European call options demonstrate that the proposed SFFT method maintains pricing accuracy while substantially reducing memory requirements and achieving subexponential computational scaling compared with conventional FFT-based pricing. The accompanying quantum simulations and hardware executions enable a direct comparison between the classical tensor network formulation and its QFT-based quantum counterpart, showing that both approaches avoid the exponential scaling of conventional Fourier implementations and provide complementary perspectives on large-scale option pricing.
  Together, these results establish a unified framework connecting classical Fourier pricing, tensor network algorithms, and quantum computing approaches, demonstrating how tensorized Fourier methods can provide scalable alternatives for high-dimensional financial computations.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum Steering and Nonlocal Correlations Between Non-Interacting Delocalized Electrons Under Rashba Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28450)
*Zakaria Bouafia,Mostafa Mansour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了二维电子气中Rashba自旋轨道耦合强度与电子间距对量子导引、Bell非定域性和不确定性诱导非定域性的影响，发现量子关联随RSOI强度呈非单调恢复，在最优耦合强度处达到峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rashba自旋轨道相互作用如何调控二维电子气中的量子关联，特别是寻找能够对抗电子间距增大导致量子关联衰减的控制参数，以Bi/Ag(111)体系为具体研究对象。

Method: 以Bi/Ag(111)体系（强RSOI，α₀ = 3.05×10⁻¹¹ eV·m）为平台，理论计算并分析Bell非定域性、不确定性诱导非定域性以及量子导引三种量子关联度量随RSOI强度（α_R）和电子间距的变化行为。
