Result: 增大α_R起初会抑制量子关联，但三种度量随后均呈现非单调恢复，并在α_R = 4.32×10⁻¹¹ eV·m附近达到峰值，该行为在所考虑的电子间距范围内一致成立。

Conclusion: RSOI是稳定二维电子气中量子特性的关键调控参数；通过调节电子间距并精细调控RSOI强度，可有效控制Bi/Ag(111)体系中量子资源的抑制与恢复。

Abstract: We investigate quantum steering and nonlocal correlations between two electrons in a two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) as functions of Rashba spin-orbit interaction (RSOI) strength and inter-electron separation. We focus particularly on the Bi/Ag(111) system characterized by its strong RSOI ($α_0 = 3.05\times10^{-11}$ eV~m), and we explore the influence of tuning intensity of RSOI and inter-electron distance on the dynamics of Bell nonlocality, uncertainty-induced nonlocality and steering. We find that, although increasing $α_R$ initially suppresses quantum correlations, all three metrics exhibit a non-monotonic recovery as functions of $α_R$, peaking near an optimal coupling strength $α_R = 4.32\times10^{-11}$~eV~m across the range of inter-electron separations considered. This finding establishes RSOI as a critical control parameter for stabilizing quantum properties in two-dimensional electron gases against the decay of quantum correlations with inter-electron separation, and shows that the suppression and recovery of quantum resources within the Bi/Ag(111) system can be controlled by adjusting the inter-electron distance and carefully tuning the RSOI strength.

</details>


### [153] [Mitigating quantum decoherence via global optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28479)
*Ashkan Abedi,Roberto Menta,Julien Despres,Marco Polini,Francesco Caravelli,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过张量网络方法，证明全局最优控制可通过时间整形大幅抑制全局驱动超导量子架构中的退相干影响，将门序列压缩一个数量级并恢复高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全局驱动的超导量子计算架构中，退相干不仅影响逻辑量子比特，还波及维持有序相的周围物理量子比特，因此传统的孤立子系统保护策略失效，需要新的全局性退相干抑制方法。

Method: 以准二维梯形几何结构为原型，采用张量网络方法量化振幅阻尼和退相位信道对量子信息流及单/双量子比特门保真度的退化效应，进而通过全局驱动的时间整形压缩门序列时间。

Result: 全局驱动的时间整形将门序列时长压缩约一个数量级，成功恢复了高门保真度，有效克服了退相干带来的性能退化。

Conclusion: 在全局驱动架构中，退相干的影响无法通过子系统隔离来抑制，但可以通过全局驱动的脉冲时间整形来克服，这表明全局最优控制是保障此类架构可扩展性的关键使能技术。

Abstract: We show that global optimal control can drastically suppress the impact of decoherence in globally driven superconducting quantum computing architectures, taking as a prototype a recently proposed quasi-two-dimensional ladder geometry. Using a tensor-network-based approach, we quantify how amplitude-damping and dephasing channels degrade the flow of quantum information along the ladder and the fidelity of one- and two-qubit gate operations. We then demonstrate that shaping the global drive compresses the gate sequences by an order of magnitude in time, restoring high gate fidelities. We stress that this mitigation is far from trivial: in a globally driven processor, dissipation acts on every physical qubit---including those outside the logical register that sustain the surrounding ordered phases---so its impact cannot be suppressed by protecting an isolated subsystem, and is instead overcome purely through the temporal shaping of the global drive.

</details>


### [154] [Optical linewidth narrowing for device-coupled single T centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28485)
*Adam Johnston,Yu-En Wong,Shengbin Yan,Ulises Felix-Rendon,Songtao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用带隙以上光激发，通过激光扫描显微镜将光生自由载流子注入器件耦合的单T中心，实现了高达70%的光学线宽压窄，并建立速率方程模型解释了电荷稳定化动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单T中心因光谱扩散导致光学线宽远大于其腔增强辐射线宽（大两个数量级），严重阻碍其在量子网络中的应用，亟需有效手段控制并压窄线宽。

Method: 利用激光扫描显微镜对器件耦合的单T中心施加上带隙光学激发，通过光生自由载流子填充邻近电荷陷阱；采用脉冲式上带隙激发分析电荷稳定化动力学，并建立速率方程模型定量描述线宽压窄和中心频率偏移现象。

Result: 实现了单T中心光学线宽最高70%的压窄；确认线宽压窄归因于光生自由载流子填充电荷陷阱导致的电荷环境稳定化；速率方程模型成功解释了观察到的线宽压窄和谱线中心偏移。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条有效控制并减小单T中心光学线宽的路径，清除了推进单T中心自旋平台在量子信息和量子网络应用中一个主要障碍。

Abstract: Single T centers in silicon have emerged as promising optically active spins for quantum networking applications. One of the major obstacles to advancing the system is their broad optical linewidth due to spectral diffusion, which is two orders of magnitude larger than their cavity-enhanced radiative linewidth. We tackle this issue by utilizing above-band optical excitation delivered via a laser scanning microscope to device-coupled single T centers, achieving up to 70% optical linewidth reduction. We attribute the linewidth narrowing effect to the filling of nearby charge traps by photo-generated free carriers. We analyze charge stabilization dynamics by exploiting pulsed above-band excitation and develop a rate equation model to describe the dynamics and to explain the observed linewidth narrowing and center shift. This work provides an effective pathway to control and reduce the optical linewidth for single T centers, clearing one of the major roadblocks to advance the single T center spin platform for quantum information and networking applications.

</details>


### [155] [Generating broadband optical squeezing via Cascaded Micro-Ring Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28493)
*Chung-Hsien Wang,Tian Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于级联微环谐振器阵列的宽带压缩真空态生成方案，通过多腔耦合克服单腔方案中压缩深度与带宽的权衡，实现平坦宽谱压缩，且对制造误差具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带压缩光作为马尔可夫库可指数增强光-物质相互作用，但传统单腔源面临压缩深度与光谱带宽之间的固有权衡，限制了其在量子技术中的应用。

Method: 提出将参量微环谐振器级联耦合到公共总线波导的可扩展架构，通过理论分析输出光谱，给出产生平坦宽谱压缩的具体条件，并考察腔内有泵浦衰减、谐振频率不均匀及器件失效等实际因素下的性能。

Result: 该架构对制造缺陷（包括非均匀谐振频率和元件故障）具有鲁棒性；平坦压缩谱向马尔可夫极限的收敛速度远快于单腔洛伦兹谱型。仅需N=5个耦合谐振器、内禀损耗比κ_I/κ=0.1，即可将实现同等压缩所需带宽降至单腔的四分之一。

Conclusion: 该方案通过将压缩过程分布到多个中等泵浦的谐振器中，放宽了单腔宽带源对低Q值和高增益的严苛要求，为在成熟集成光子平台上工程化压缩库提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Broadband squeezed light functioning as a Markovian reservoir can exponentially enhance light-matter interactions, benefiting quantum technologies. However, conventional single-cavity sources face a trade-off between squeezing depth and spectral bandwidth. We propose a scalable scheme for generating broadband squeezed vacuum using a cascade of parametric microring resonators coupled to a common bus waveguide. By analyzing the output, we identify the specific conditions that yield a broad, flat-topped squeezing spectrum, even under realistic intracavity pump attenuation. We demonstrate that this architecture is robust against fabrication imperfections, including inhomogeneous resonator frequencies and component failures. We show that the flat-topped spectrum converges to the Markovian limit significantly faster than a single-cavity Lorentzian profile. An array of as few as $N=5$ coupled resonators with an intrinsic loss ratio of $κ_I/κ= 0.1$ reduces the required bandwidth to a quarter of that needed by a single cavity to achieve same squeezing. This rapid convergence relaxes the low-$Q$ and high-gain constraints of single broadband cavities, distributing the squeezing process across moderately pumped resonators to provide a practical route for engineering squeezed reservoirs on mature integrated photonic platforms.

</details>


### [156] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Simulation of Wave Equations on a Trapped-Ion Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28499)
*Abhishek Shringi,Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Ahmed Shokry,Xiantao Li,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱量子处理器上，开发并测试了基于傅里叶变换的结构保持量子电路，用于模拟一维和二维声波方程及变质量狄拉克动力学，系统规模最大达1024个格点（一维）和32×32（二维），编码态空间维度达4096。通过直接估计子域动能，实验结果与经典动力学吻合良好（平均绝对误差在5.9×10⁻³至2.4×10⁻²之间），为当前量子硬件上模拟结构化波问题提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波方程为近期量子模拟偏微分方程提供了天然测试平台，但现有硬件演示在空间维度、方程类型、系统规模和物理可观测量输出方面存在显著局限。本文旨在突破这些限制，验证在真实量子硬件上对高维、大规模波动问题进行保结构模拟并提取有物理意义的观测量的可行性。

Method: 研究者开发了基于傅里叶变换的结构保持（structure-preserving）量子电路，针对声波方程和变质量狄拉克方程进行设计。实验在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱处理器上执行，涵盖一维（最多1024个格点）和二维（32×32格点）网格，编码态空间维度最高达4096。不重建完整波场，而是通过测量样本直接估计子域动能。编译后的门计数随网格量子比特数近似二次增长，声波电路规模与演化时间基本无关。

Result: 在所有测试的声学和狄拉克动力学问题中，H2-2的实验结果与经典动能动力学轨迹一致，平均绝对误差介于5.9×10⁻³至2.4×10⁻²之间。在固定保留带宽下，编译门计数随网格量子比特数近似二次增长；声波电路规模几乎不依赖于演化时间，而狄拉克电路成本随乘积公式步数增加。

Conclusion: 研究提供了硬件层面的证据，表明在当前离子阱量子处理器上，对于具有数千个编码自由度的结构化波问题，准确的观测量动力学仍然是可以分辨的。这为近期量子设备在偏微分方程模拟领域的实用化迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Wave equations provide a natural testbed for near-term quantum simulation of partial differential equations, but hardware demonstrations have remained limited in spatial dimension, equation class, system size, and physically meaningful output. We develop and benchmark structure-preserving, Fourier-based quantum circuits for the one- and two dimensional acoustic wave equations and Dirac dynamics with variable mass on the Quantinuum H2-2 trapped-ion processor. The experiments include one-dimensional grids with up to \(1024\) points and \(32\times32\) two-dimensional grids, corresponding to an encoded state-space dimension of up to \(4096\). Rather than reconstructing the full fields, we estimate subdomain kinetic energies directly from measurement samples. Across all tested acoustic and Dirac dynamics problems, the H2-2 results track the classical kinetic-energy dynamics with mean absolute errors between \(5.9\times10^{-3}\) and \(2.4\times10^{-2}\). At fixed retained bandwidth, the compiled gate counts grow approximately quadratically with the number of grid qubits; the acoustic circuit sizes are essentially independent of evolution time, whereas the cost also grows with the number of product-formula steps. These results provide hardware-level evidence that accurate observable dynamics can remain resolvable for structured wave problems with thousands of encoded degrees of freedom on a present-day trapped-ion processor.

</details>


### [157] [Spacetime Layout and Logical Compilation of Color Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28504)
*Qinjing Yu,Ke Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为color code构建了一个完整的逻辑编译框架，基于其拓扑结构建立时空块图表示，并与ZX图建立对应关系，实现了从逻辑计算到color code时空布局的自动化编译，覆盖多种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要在系统层面协调逻辑原语，但color code（色码）此前缺乏一个统一的逻辑编译框架来自动化地将逻辑计算映射到物理布局上。这限制了color code从孤立原语走向全栈量子计算的实用化进程。

Method: 基于color code的anyon凝聚和畴壁结构，引入时空块图表示来刻画逻辑补丁和逻辑操作，并推导块组装规则。建立与ZX图的对应关系以赋予块图表示逻辑语义，并利用ZX图的等价变换保持计算不变性。进一步提出码推导的编译策略：用边缘装饰的ZX图（edge-decorated ZX diagrams）在块组装约束下定制逻辑表示，并通过融合区域感知路由（fusion-region-aware routing）在几何嵌入中利用语义等价性。最终实现全流程自动化编译。

Result: 实现了完整的自动化逻辑编译流程，并在广泛的算法上成功进行了编译验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将color code架构从单个原语推进到逻辑计算的自动化合成，标志着迈向全栈color code量子计算的重要一步。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires system-level coordination of logical primitives. Here, we establish a logical compilation framework for the color code, grounded in its topological structure and supporting universal logical operations. Based on its anyon-condensation and domain-wall structure, we introduce a spacetime block-diagram representation capturing logical patches and operations and derive the rules governing block assembly. A correspondence with ZX diagrams further identifies the logical semantics of this representation and enables transformations that preserve the represented computation. Moreover, we develop a code-derived compilation strategy that converts ZX representations of logical computations into valid color-code spacetime layouts. In this strategy, edge-decorated ZX diagrams tailor the logical representation to the color code under the block-assembly constraints, and fusion-region-aware routing exploits semantic equivalence during geometric embedding. We automate the complete logical compilation process and demonstrate successful compilation across a broad range of algorithms. Our work advances color-code architecture from individual primitives to the automated synthesis of logical computations, marking a significant step toward its full-stack quantum computing.

</details>


### [158] [Spectral gaps of ironed two-qubit gadgets matching the iSWAP gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28521)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类特定双量子比特gadget（KAK参数满足a=5/9）在n≥5的完全图上的二阶矩谱隙与iSWAP gadget相同，核心步骤是一个表示论局部化定理，并由此解决了Kong、Li和Liu的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子计算中不同双量子比特gadget的谱性质，特别是理解ironed two-qubit gadget在完全图上的二阶矩谱隙行为，并验证Kong、Li和Liu提出的猜想——即满足a=5/9的gadget与iSWAP gadget具有相同的二阶矩谱隙。

Method: 核心方法是一个表示论局部化定理：证明相关的𝔖ₙ-不变算子的最大严格负特征值总是出现在最高自旋的SU(2)直和分量中。此外，利用局部正半定分解将除两个最高自旋扇区之外的所有自旋扇区分离开来，从而将问题约化到最高自旋部分进行分析。

Result: 证明了每个KAK参数满足a=5/9的ironed双量子比特gadget，在n≥5的完全图Kₙ上，其二阶矩谱隙与iSWAP gadget完全相同。这一结果正式解决了Kong、Li和Liu提出的相关猜想。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在完全图相互作用图景下，满足特定参数条件的ironed双量子比特gadget与iSWAP gadget在二阶矩谱隙上表现出普适性。表示论局部化定理为分析此类量子多体系统的谱性质提供了强有力的工具，也为进一步研究其他gadget类型和拓扑结构下的谱隙行为奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove that every ironed two-qubit gadget whose KAK-derived parameter satisfies $a=5/9$ has, on the complete graph $K_n$ with $n\geqslant 5$, the same second-moment spectral gap as the iSWAP gadget. The central step is a representation-theoretic localisation theorem: the largest strictly negative eigenvalue of the associated $\mathfrak S_n$-invariant operator always occurs in the highest-spin $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ summand. A local positive-semidefinite decomposition separates every spin sector except the two highest. This settles a conjecture of Kong, Li, and Liu.

</details>


### [159] [When quantum thermal states look classical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28536)
*Harald Putterman,Alexander Zlokapa,Jordan Cotler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子Gibbs态在有限温度下保留若干经典特征（无纠缠、无魔法、解析性、关联衰减、算法可处理性），但这些特征在不同逆温尺度上依次失效，形成经典到量子的层级跃迁。结果适用于每个格点有界强度的长程Pauli相互作用，并在常数温度下给出纠缠死亡时刻，解决了开放问题，同时提供了优于已知量子Gibbs采样器的经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下量子Gibbs态表现出诸多经典特征，但这些特征随温度降低而失效的具体尺度尚不清晰。作者旨在精确刻画这些经典到量子跃迁的层级结构，并解决纠缠死亡温度、关联衰减猜想等开放问题。

Method: 针对每个格点有界强度的长程Pauli相互作用模型，利用严格的数学分析方法，分别对纠缠消亡、魔法消失、配分函数解析性、关联衰减及算法可处理性等经典特征建立新的紧界，证明它们在不同逆温阈值依次崩溃。

Result: （1）纠缠死亡发生在常数温度，解决了Rouze-Franca-Alhambra (STOC'25)的开放问题；（2）给出多项式时间经典算法，可制备纠缠死亡跃迁前的Gibbs态，该温度渐近低于已知量子Gibbs采样器快速混合的温度及Bakshi等人(FOCS'24)的可分离温度，并将后者改进至常数级紧界；（3）在更低温度下，Gibbs态仍处于热力学无限温相，可在纠缠与魔法并存时用多项式时间经典算法估计热期望值，并解决了Harrow-Mehraban-Soleimanifar (STOC'20)的关联衰减猜想。

Conclusion: 高温量子Gibbs态的经典特征并非同时消失，而是按纠缠、魔法、算法可处理性等层级依次崩溃，形成清晰的经典到量子跃迁谱系。这一结果为理解多体量子系统在有限温度下的经典-量子边界提供了精确的数学图景。

Abstract: At high temperature, quantum Gibbs states retain several classical features of the maximally mixed state: the absence of entanglement, the absence of magic, analyticity of the partition function, correlation decay, and algorithmic tractability. We prove new and sharp bounds showing that these features persist down to finite temperatures independent of system size, but fail at distinct inverse-temperature scales, forming a hierarchy of classical-to-quantum transitions. Our results hold for long-range Pauli interactions with bounded strength at every site. Despite such all-to-all interactions, we show that the death of entanglement occurs at constant temperature, resolving an open question of Rouze, Franca and Alhambra (STOC'25). We give a polynomial-time classical algorithm that prepares Gibbs states up to the death of entanglement transition. Notably, this is asymptotically colder than temperatures at which quantum Gibbs samplers are known to mix quickly, as well as the original separability temperature of Bakshi et al. (FOCS'24), which we improve to be tight up to constants. At asymptotically even colder temperatures, we show that the Gibbs state remains in the thermodynamic infinite-temperature phase. This leads to polynomial-time classical algorithms for estimating thermal expectations despite both entanglement and magic, and the resolution of a correlation decay conjecture of Harrow, Mehraban and Soleimanifar (STOC'20).

</details>


### [160] [A Provable Oracle-Free Quantum Algorithm for Nonlinear Dynamics on Hybrid Oscillator-Qubit Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28541)
*Kausthubh Chandramouli,Yan Li,Yuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子比特-量子模式（hybrid qubit-qumode）算法，用于求解漂移项为多项式（最高次数L）的非线性常微分方程。核心思路是将离散化生成元通过warped-phase变换转化为参数化Schrödinger方程族，并将Fourier模式参数编码到单个连续变量量子模式上。关键结构发现是生成元的Hermitian部分具有可二分Pauli分解，能将非零Pauli串分为O(log N)个相互对易的族，每族可因子化为对角矩阵与固定秩-2键算符的张量积，从而消除族内Trotter误差。电路代价为每个Trotter步O(d^{L+1} n^{L+2})门，且无需稀疏oracle或块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性常微分方程在科学计算中广泛存在，经典算法面临维度灾难。量子算法虽然有望加速，但现有方法常依赖稀疏访问oracle或块编码，实现复杂且资源消耗大。作者希望利用连续变量量子模式的独特优势，为多项式型非线性ODE构建结构更清晰、门实现更直接的量子算法，并消除离散化模式寄存器带来的Trotter误差。

Method: 1. 遵循Tennie和Magri的Fokker-Planck路线，将状态密度演化并在小噪声极限下以密度峰值恢复确定性轨迹。2. 应用Jin-Liu-Yu的warped-phase变换，将离散化生成元转为参数化Schrödinger方程族。3. 将Fourier模式参数编码到单个连续变量qumode上。4. 核心贡献：证明生成元Hermitian部分H₁和H₂具有二分Pauli分解结构——非零Pauli串分为O(log N)个对易族，每族因子化为L次对角矩阵与秩-2键算符的张量积。5. 利用该因子化将每族指数表示为O(n^L)个单omial控制的动量位移的精确积。6. 证明数值横标λ_max(H₁)的界，确定warped-phase变换的恢复域和后选择代价。

Result: 1. 电路代价：d维网格每轴N=2ⁿ点，每Trotter步需O(d^{L+1} n^{L+2})门。2. 无需稀疏访问oracle和块编码，所有门由漂移多项式系数闭式确定。3. 族内无Trotter误差（因子化为精确积）。4. 给出数值横标上界，确定warped-phase恢复域和后选择代价。5. 在两个非线性基准上经典仿真验证了结构定理、偏移恢复，以及连续变量耦合相比离散化模式寄存器在精度-资源权衡上的优势。

Conclusion: 该混合qubit-qumode算法为多项式非线性ODE提供了一种结构优雅、门实现直接的量子求解方案。Pauli分解与因子化结构消除了族内Trotter误差，连续变量qumode的引入比离散模式寄存器具有更好的资源效率。该方法为连续变量量子计算在科学计算中的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid qubit--qumode algorithm for nonlinear ordinary differential equations of the form $\dot{\mathbf{x}}=\mathbf{f}(\mathbf{x})$ with drift of polynomial degree~$L$. Following the Fokker--Planck route of Tennie and Magri, the algorithm propagates the state density and returns the deterministic trajectory as the peak of that density in the small-noise limit. The discretised generator is carried into a parametrised family of Schrödinger equations by the warped-phase transformation of Jin, Liu, and Yu, and the Fourier-mode parameter of that family is placed on a single continuous-variable qumode. Our central structural result is that the Hermitian parts $H_{1}$ and $H_{2}$ of the discretised generator admit a bipartite Pauli decomposition that sorts the non-zero Pauli strings into $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ mutually commuting families and factorises each family into a diagonal of degree at most $L$ tensored with a fixed rank-two bond operator. The factorisation renders each family exponential an exact product of $\mathcal{O}(n^{L})$ monomial-controlled momentum displacements, with no intra-family Trotter error. On a $d$-dimensional grid of $N=2^{n}$ points per axis the circuit costs $\mathcal{O}(d^{L+1}n^{L+2})$ gates per Trotter step. No sparse-access oracle and no block encoding is invoked: every gate is fixed in closed form by the polynomial coefficients of the drift. We also prove a bound on the numerical abscissa $λ_{\max}(H_{1})$ that fixes the recovery domain of the warped-phase transform and the post-selection cost. A classical simulation on two nonlinear benchmarks confirms the structural theorems, the shifted recovery, and the accuracy-per-resource advantage of the continuous-variable coupling over a discretised mode register.

</details>


### [161] [Statistically secure uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28561)
*Archishna Bhattacharyya,Anne Broadbent,Eric Culf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于已知单比特无克隆加密方案使用Clifford酉算子的特性,本文将其扩展至任意长度消息,在保持统计安全性的同时实现多项式时间的加解密,证明了任意消息的一次性无克隆加密具备统计安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单比特无条件无克隆加密已实现高效加解密,但能否在任意长度消息上扩展并保持统计安全性仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 利用已知安全单比特方案的编码基是Clifford酉集子集这一事实,将该方案升级为支持任意长度消息的无条件无克隆加密方案,编码时间与消息长度和安全参数呈多项式关系。

Result: 成功构造了任意长度消息的无条件无克隆加密方案,编码时间为消息长度和安全参数的多项式级别,证明了任意消息的一次性无克隆加密可获得统计安全性。

Conclusion: 单比特无克隆加密方案可借助Clifford群性质自然推广至任意长度消息,且保持统计安全性和计算效率,解决了该领域的核心开放问题。

Abstract: Unconditional uncloneable encryption of a single bit with efficient encryption and decryption is now possible. However, whether the extension to messages of arbitrary length achieves statistical security remains to be known. Using the fact that the encoding bases for the single-bit scheme known to be secure consist of a subset of the Clifford unitaries, we show that this scheme can be upgraded to achieve unconditional uncloneable encryption for messages of arbitrary length, with encoding time polynomial in the message length and security parameter. This establishes that one-time uncloneable encryption of arbitrary messages enjoys statistical security.

</details>


### [162] [Benchmarking Quantum Simulations of the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick Model Using Large Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28570)
*Maggie Bao,Rushil Dandamudi,Jerimiah Wright,Joan Étude Arrow,Henry Zou,Vardaan Sahgal,Brian J. McDermott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用DMRG算法在超级计算机上计算LMG模型基态能量（最大1400粒子），并将其作为基准，与IBM Eagle量子计算机上运行的VQE和SQD算法进行对比。结果显示，VQE仅在6粒子内误差低于1%，而SQD将这一范围扩展到17粒子，表明子空间方法在NISQ时代更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算日趋成熟，亟需以竞争性的经典方法（如张量网络）为基准，评估量子计算在真实世界问题上的表现。现有NISQ算法缺乏大规模、高精度的经典基准数据集进行系统性比较。

Method: 作者在NERSC Perlmutter超级计算机上运行DMRG算法，计算Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick（LMG）模型的基态能量，生成包含多达1400粒子系统的精确基准数据集；同时在IBM Eagle量子计算机上实现VQE和SQD算法，将二者的计算结果与DMRG基准进行误差对比。

Result: DMRG提供了文献中最大规模的LMG基态能量数据集（最高1400粒子）。VQE仅在6粒子时达到1%以内的误差，粒子数更多时误差均超出该阈值；SQD则将1%误差范围扩展到17粒子，表现更优。

Conclusion: 在含噪中等尺度量子（NISQ）时代，基于子空间的方法（如SQD）比VQE等方法在精度、电路深度和噪声鲁棒性之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: As quantum computing matures, it is critical to benchmark its real-world problem solving performance against competitive classical methods, such as tensor networks. In this work, we leverage the Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) algorithm to compute ground state energies of the Lipkin Meshkov Glick (LMG) model as a comparative benchmark against popular noisy intermediate-scale (NISQ) algorithms like the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD) method. By running DMRG on the NERSC Perlmutter supercomputer, we provide one of the largest LMG ground state energy datasets in literature, containing accurate ground state energies for systems up to 1400 particles. We compare these results with VQE and SQD implementations on an IBM Eagle quantum computer for comparison. VQE achieved results within 1 percent error for 6 particles, while exceeding that threshold for all other values while SQD extended that range to 17 particles, suggesting that in a noisy intermediate scale quantum era, subspace-based approaches may strike the best balance between accuracy, circuit depth, and noise resilience.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost: A Metric for Evaluation of Quantum Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28572)
*Siddarth Shinde,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子保真度-成本比（QFC）指标，结合KL散度、shot数和货币成本，对14个云量子计算访问入口进行跨提供商测量研究，发现基于成本的排名与纯保真度排名存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云量子计算的普及使硬件比较从纯粹物理基准转变为实际采购决策，但各提供商采用异构计费模式，导致成本感知的量子计算机比较仍未被充分探索且难以实施。

Method: 作者对AWS、IBM、IQM、OQC四大云平台共14个QPU访问入口（对应12个不同物理QPU）进行了跨提供商量子电路执行保真度测量研究，并提出QFC指标，将理想输出分布的KL散度、shot计数和货币成本综合为一个可量化比较指标。

Result: 实证结果表明，成本感知排名与纯保真度排名存在差异；用户考虑价格后的后端选择会不同于仅基于保真度的选择；该排名在指标重新加权下具有稳定性；设备计费模式（而非硬件本身）决定了其得分随shot数变化的缩放行为。

Conclusion: 在量子计算机选型中引入成本维度会显著改变评估结果，QFC为云量子计算用户提供了一种实用的价格感知比较框架，但QFC值会随新机器上线或价格变动而动态变化。

Abstract: Cloud-accessible quantum computing has made hardware comparison not only a physics benchmark but also a practical purchasing decision. Cost-aware comparison of quantum computers remains underexplored and is difficult to do under the heterogeneous billing models offered by various cloud-based quantum computing providers. This paper makes two main contributions to enable price-aware comparison of quantum computers. First, this work presents a cross-provider measurement study of quantum circuit execution fidelity spanning 14 cloud QPU access-path entries (12 distinct physical QPUs) across four cloud access paths: Amazon Web Services (AWS) cloud, IBM Quantum Runtime (IBM) cloud, IQM Resonance (IQM) cloud, and Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC) cloud. Second, this work proposes and analyzes a cost-aware score, Quantum Fidelity-per-Cost (QFC), which combines Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence from an ideal output distribution, shot count, and monetary cost into one possible metric under a documented billing model. The main empirical observation from this work is that cost-aware ranking can differ from purely fidelity-based evaluation of quantum computers, and that users may select different quantum computing backends when they consider price in their selection, as opposed to selection based on fidelity alone. This work shows that the ranking is stable under reweighting of the metric, and that a device's billing model, not its hardware, governs how its score scales with shot count. Reported QFC values change as new machines come online or as providers revise their prices.

</details>


### [164] [Explicit Matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ with CNOT and Row Complexity $4n-\mathrm{o}(n)$ and Local Logic Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28598)
*Sherry Gong,Andrew Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 上可逆矩阵，证明其 CNOT 门复杂度与行复杂度至少为 $4n - o(n)$，并将局部逻辑门生成的置换群同构于 $\mathbb{Z}_2^n$ 上的全体可逆仿射变换，从而将该群的量子复杂度问题归结为矩阵行化简复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子电路中 CNOT 门（以及更一般的局部线性逻辑门）的复杂度下界，旨在为可逆计算与量子计算中的基本操作建立精确的复杂度理论，特别是理解将可逆矩阵化简为单位矩阵所需的最少基本行操作次数。

Method: 作者显式构造了一族 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 上的 $n \times n$ 可逆矩阵，并证明其 CNOT 复杂度与行复杂度至少为 $4n - o(n)$。进而，定义 $G_n$ 为由局部逻辑门（作用于二元串对的任意可逆线性变换）生成的置换群，证明 $G_n$ 自然同构于 $\mathbb{Z}_2^n$ 上全体可逆仿射变换构成的群，从而将 $G_n$ 中置换的量子复杂度估计问题转化为 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 上可逆矩阵的行化简复杂度问题。

Result: （1）显式矩阵族的 CNOT 复杂度与行复杂度下界均为 $4n - o(n)$，且该下界在将 CNOT 替换为任意局部可逆线性变换的更强模型下依然成立；（2）$G_n \cong \text{Aff}(\mathbb{Z}_2^n)$（可逆仿射变换群）；（3）对应显式矩阵的置换量子复杂度至少为 $4n - o(n)$。

Conclusion: 该工作为可逆逻辑电路和量子电路中的基本门复杂度建立了新的下界，并通过群同构将置换的量子复杂度与矩阵行化简复杂度联系起来，为后续研究提供了有力框架。

Abstract: In this article, we present an explicit family of invertible $n\times n$ matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$ whose CNOT and row complexity is at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$; equivalently, reducing these matrices to the identity requires at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$ elementary row operations. Moreover, the same complexity lower bound holds in the stronger computational model where the CNOT gates are replaced by arbitrary local linear logic gates, namely arbitrary invertible linear transformations acting on pairs of coordinates.
  Let $G_n$ denote the permutation group generated by local logic gates acting on the set of binary strings of length $n$. We prove that $G_n$ is naturally isomorphic to the group of all invertible affine transformations of the vector space $\mathbb Z_2^n$, thus reducing the problem of estimating the quantum complexity of permutations in $G_n$ to the row reduction complexity of invertible matrices over $\mathbb Z_2$. As an application, we show that the permutations associated with our explicit matrices have quantum complexity at least $4n-\text{o}(n)$.

</details>


### [165] [SymFT: Universal Fault-Tolerant Quantum Circuit Simulation via Symbolic Clifford--Pauli Frames and Stabilizer Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28600)
*Wang Fang,Huazhe Lou,Riling Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SymFT 是一款针对以 Clifford 子电路为主、含 Pauli 旋转和非 Clifford 操作的容错量子电路的高通量采样模拟器，结合符号化 Clifford-Pauli 帧分解和自适应稳定子坐标规划，在多种近 Clifford 电路上实现了领先的采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子协议中大部分子电路为 stabilizer 操作，但实现通用量子计算所需的非 Clifford 操作使得精确采样开销极大。现有模拟器在处理此类 Clifford 主导但含有 Pauli 旋转、随机 Pauli 噪声、中间测量及测量反馈的电路时仍存在瓶颈。

Method: SymFT 融合两项核心技术：(1) 符号化 Clifford-Pauli 帧分解——将分支概率采样归约为 Pauli 旋转和测量投影，噪声与反馈以符号标志表示，残留的 Clifford/Pauli 帧因幺正性无需逐次应用；(2) 自适应稳定子坐标规划——利用共享的 stabilizer-destabilizer tableau 定义基，仅在动态大小的稠密活跃态向量中存储非 stabilizer 自由度，通过一次性基变换解析并生成直接多坐标采样指令，避免逐 shot 的 tableau 更新和稠密向量的局域化 Clifford 变换。

Result: 在纯 Clifford 和近 Clifford 电路测试中，SymFT 达到业界领先采样性能：单 CPU 核上比 Stim 快 2.51–2.56 倍（surface-code 电路），比 Clifft 快 1.86–3.51 倍（magic-state  cultivation 与蒸馏电路），在 cultivation 电路上的采样吞吐量超过此前 SOFT 模拟器两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SymFT 通过将符号化帧分解与自适应坐标规划相结合，显著提升了 Clifford 主导量子电路的经典模拟采样效率，为容错量子计算的验证与仿真提供了强大的工具。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant protocols often consist largely of stabilizer subcircuits, yet the non-Clifford operations required for universality make exact sampling costly. We present SymFT, a high-throughput simulator for Clifford-dominated circuits with Pauli rotations, stochastic Pauli noise, mid-circuit Pauli measurements, and measurement-record-controlled Pauli feedback. It combines two ideas. First, symbolic Clifford--Pauli frame factorization reduces branch-probability sampling to Pauli rotations and measurement projectors, with noise and feedback represented by symbolic signs. Since the residual Clifford and Pauli frames are unitary, they do not affect branch probabilities and need not be applied in every shot. Second, adaptive stabilizer-coordinate planning uses a shared stabilizer--destabilizer tableau to define the basis and stores only the active non-stabilizer degrees of freedom in a dynamically sized dense active-state vector. It resolves basis changes once and emits direct multi-coordinate sampling instructions, thereby avoiding per-shot tableau updates and localization-induced Clifford transformations of the dense vector. Across the tested pure-Clifford and near-Clifford circuits, SymFT achieves state-of-the-art sampling performance. On a single CPU core, it is $2.51\text{--}2.56\times$ faster than Stim for surface-code circuits and $1.86\text{--}3.51\times$ faster than Clifft for magic-state cultivation and distillation circuits. For the tested cultivation circuits, its sampling throughput also exceeds that of our previous simulator, SOFT, by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [166] [Pauli Encodings & Unclonable Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28602)
*Pierre Botteron,Sébastien Designolle,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Pauli编码的单克隆安全性，对含K个Pauli串的编码证明了纠缠一夫一妻制获胜概率的通用下界1/2+1/(2√K)，揭示了两类自然方法的安全局限性，并对全反对易情形给出了约0.5556的渐近上界，同时在某些情形下证明了强不可克隆-不可区分安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子加密能否防止敌手在获取密钥后同时从两个系统恢复明文——即不可克隆比特问题。作者旨在系统性地研究Pauli编码这一简单单比特加密方案类的安全性，理解其优势与局限。

Method: (1) 引入Pauli编码框架，将密文定义为Pauli串的归一化本征空间投影算子；(2) 对含K个Pauli串的编码推导纠缠一夫一妻制获胜概率的通用下界及结构性更紧界；(3) 证明两类限制：纯X/Z串编码不安全、仅基于成对猜测边缘概率无法建立不可克隆-不可区分安全性（3/4障碍）；(4) 对全反对易情形利用对称性求解半定规划松弛的第三层，获渐近上界；(5) 对有界局域维度敌手及若干高效Pauli族证明强不可克隆-不可区分安全性，并用第一层NPA计算提供数值证据。

Result: (1) 通用下界：1/2+1/(2√K)；(2) X/Z串编码不安全；(3) 成对猜测边缘方法有3/4通用障碍；(4) 全反对易协议获胜概率渐近上界约0.5556；(5) 有界局域维度敌手下可证强不可克隆-不可区分安全性，若干高效族可证强不可区分安全性。

Conclusion: Pauli编码为不可克隆加密提供了统一的分析框架。结果表明，虽然某些自然方法存在根本性限制，但通过适当构造（尤其是全反对易情形）仍能实现有意义的安全性。半定规划松弛的求解与NPA数值证据为进一步研究强不可克隆-不可区分安全性提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The unclonable bit question asks whether quantum encryption can prevent an adversary from producing two systems that both reveal the plaintext once the key is disclosed. We introduce and study Pauli Encodings, a simple class of one-bit encryption schemes whose ciphertexts are normalized eigenspace projectors of Pauli strings. For every Pauli Encoding with K Pauli strings, we prove a universal lower bound $1/2+1/(2\sqrt{K})$ on the optimal monogamy-of-entanglement winning probability, together with sharper bounds for several structured families. We then establish two limitations of natural approaches to unclonable security. First, if the Pauli strings are restricted to strings of X and Z of length n, the encoding is not secure. Second, we identify a universal 3/4 obstruction showing that arguments based only on pairwise guessing marginals cannot establish unclonable-indistinguishable security. When the Pauli strings all pairwise anticommute, the protocol becomes the one studied in [Quantum 10, 2157 (2026)]. We exploit the symmetry of this protocol to solve the third level of the natural semidefinite programming relaxation obtaining an asymptotic upper bound of approximately 0.5556 on the winning probability. Finally, we prove strong unclonable-indistinguishable security against bounded-local-dimension adversaries and strong indistinguishability security for several efficient Pauli families. First-level NPA computations provide additional numerical evidence towards the strong unclonable-indistinguishable security.

</details>


### [167] [Logical computation with canonical lifted product codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28605)
*Han Zheng,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有循环对称性的规范提升积（LP）码，通过码与逻辑指令集的协同设计，解锁了完整的逻辑操作集，包括常深度自同构门、折叠横向Clifford门、基于常量数量种子手术gadget的模块化图码手术、高度并行的逻辑Pauli乘积测量及并行魔法态注入，大幅推进了超高码率量子架构上的容错计算前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率qLDPC码能以低物理量子比特开销编码大量逻辑量子比特，但在此类密集编码上实现高效容错计算仍是重大挑战。通用技术（如码手术和门隐形传态）难以在结构未被充分利用的复杂高码率码上实现模块化、低开销和完全可认证。

Method: 作者对具有循环对称性的规范提升积（LP）码族进行码与逻辑指令集的协同设计：揭示其具备规范逻辑基（canonical logical basis），共轭逻辑算符按循环轨道组织成行列结构；基于此结构构建常深度自同构门与折叠横向Clifford门、利用常量数量的可复用种子手术gadget进行模块化图码手术、设计紧凑的规范提取器实现任意高权重逻辑测量，以及并行魔法态注入。

Result: 例如，[[1122,148,≤20]] LP码仅需2个种子手术gadget，[[4350,1224,≤20]] LP码仅需4个；任意高权重逻辑测量可通过小于数据码块一半大小的全提取器实现。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计码结构与逻辑指令集，充分利用LP码的循环对称性，可实现超高码率量子码上的高效、模块化、低开销容错量子计算，推动了该领域的边界。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes encode many logical qubits with low physical-qubit overhead, but realizing efficient fault-tolerant computation on such dense encodings remains a major challenge. Generic, code-agnostic techniques such as code surgery and gate teleportation apply broadly, but are difficult to make modular, low-overhead, and fully certifiable on complex high-rate codes whose structure is left unexploited. Here we overcome these obstacles by co-designing the code together with its logical instruction set for a broad family of \emph{canonical} lifted-product (LP) codes with cyclic symmetry. We show that these codes admit a \emph{canonical logical basis}, in which conjugate logical operators are organized into rows and columns of cyclic orbits inherited directly from the underlying classical codes, analogous to the structure that makes hypergraph-product codes so tractable. This canonical basis unlocks a complete logical instruction set, including constant-depth automorphism and fold-transversal Clifford gates, modular graph code surgeries built from a constant number of reusable seed surgery gadgets or a compact canonical extractor, highly parallel logical Pauli-product measurements, and parallel magic-state injection. For example, a $[[1122,148,\leq\!20]]$ (resp. $[[4350,1224,\leq\!20]]$) LP code requires only two (resp. four) seed surgery gadgets, while arbitrary high-weight logical measurements can be implemented using a full extractor smaller than half of the data code block. These results advance the frontier of fault-tolerant quantum computation on ultra-high-rate quantum architectures.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Arbitrary Lindbladians from Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28610)
*Zhili Chen,Zhan Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效算法，从系统的时间演化中学习未知的马尔可夫开放量子系统生成器（Lindbladian），在极小假设下，以近乎最优的实验次数和总演化时间估计所有哈密顿量和耗散系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的Lindbladian包含指数多个可能的Pauli项系数，现有学习方法在效率或假设条件上存在局限。作者旨在设计一种在最少假设下、实验资源和运行时间近乎最优的Lindbladian学习算法。

Method: 算法分为两个非自适应、无辅助比特、无控制的阶段：（1）支持学习阶段：通过制备积Pauli本征态并进行单量子比特Pauli测量，以$\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$次实验输出包含所有幅度≥η的系数的候选支持集；（2）系数学习阶段：通过制备随机稳定子态并在随机Clifford基下测量，以$\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^2)$次实验将候选支持集中的所有系数估计至误差ε。两阶段组合可在多项式时间内识别并估计任意Lindbladian的所有系数。

Result: 对于动力学强度至多为Λ的Lindbladian，算法以$\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$次实验和$\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$总演化时间估计每个系数至误差ε，并具有多项式经典运行时间。实验次数和总演化时间的标度与下界仅差对数因子，因此算法近乎最优。

Conclusion: 该算法在最少假设下实现了任意Lindbladian的高效、近乎最优的学习，其非自适应、无辅助和无控制的特性使其特别适合近期量子设备的实验实现。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning an unknown Markovian open-system generator from access to its physical time evolution. This generator, called a Lindbladian, contains Hamiltonian and dissipative coefficients indexed by an exponentially large family of possible Pauli terms. We propose an efficient algorithm that learns arbitrary Lindbladians from time evolution under minimal assumptions. For a Lindbladian of dynamical strength at most $Λ$, the algorithm estimates every coefficient to error $ε$ using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$ experiments and $\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$ total evolution time, together with polynomial classical running time. The algorithm consists of two nonadaptive, ancilla-free, and control-free stages: 1. The support-learning stage outputs a candidate support of size $\mathrm{poly}(Λ/η)$ that contains every Hamiltonian and dissipative coordinate of magnitude at least $η$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$ experiments with preparations of product Pauli eigenstates and single-qubit Pauli measurements. 2.The coefficient-learning stage estimates all coefficients in any candidate support of size $M$ to error $ε$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$ experiments with preparations of random stabilizer states and measurements in random Clifford bases. Composing the two stages identifies and estimates every coefficient of an arbitrary Lindbladian in polynomial time. The experiment-count and total-evolution-time scalings match the lower bounds up to logarithmic factors, so the algorithm is nearly optimal for learning arbitrary Lindbladians.

</details>


### [169] [On Optimal Measurement-State Preparation via Geometric Transport of the Squeezing Ellipse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28620)
*Vsevolod Salakhutdinov,Nikolay Kalinin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，将初始未对准的压缩态通过SU(2)对称系统中的受控轨迹，转化为测量最优态。压缩椭圆的取向作为一个额外的几何自由度，其旋转由路径所包围的立体角决定，并与几何相位建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学中使用压缩态时，制备最优测量态是一个关键需求。然而，初始压缩态往往存在对准偏差，需要一种系统的方法将其转化为测量最优态。

Method: 作者在SU(2)对称系统中建立了一个几何框架。在该框架中，压缩椭圆的取向作为额外的几何自由度，随着平均态在单位球面上沿受控轨迹演化。椭圆旋转由路径几何决定，特别是由轨迹所包围的立体角决定，从而与几何相位建立了直接联系。

Result: 该框架揭示了压缩椭圆旋转与路径几何之间的内在关系：椭圆旋转量等于轨迹所包围的立体角。这一结果适用于多种物理平台，文中以偏振压缩光为例，提出了一种利用连续变化双折射元件的可能实现方案。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为SU(2)对称系统中压缩态的优化对准提供了统一描述，将椭圆旋转与几何相位联系起来，并对偏振压缩光等具体物理系统具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Preparation of optimal measurement states is a key requirement in quantum metrology utilizing squeezed states. We discuss a geometric framework that transforms an initially misaligned squeezed input into a measurement-optimal state in SU(2)-symmetric systems. Within this framework, the orientation of the squeezing ellipse constitutes an additional geometric degree of freedom and evolves as the mean state follows a controlled trajectory on the unit sphere. The resulting rotation of the ellipse is determined by the geometry of the path and, for the relevant class of transformations, depends on the solid angle enclosed by the trajectory, establishing a connection with the geometric phase. The discussed framework is applicable to different physical platforms. As a particular example, we consider polarization-squeezed light and outline a possible implementation using a continuously varying birefringent element.

</details>

### [170] [Three-Photon Bayesian Imaging of Ortho-Positronium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27741)
*L. Raczynski,W. Krzemien,A. Coussat,M. Bala,B. C. Hiesmayr,K. Klimaszewski,M. Obara,R. Y. Shopa*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIO算法，一种基于贝叶斯最大后验推断的三光子事件级图像重建方法，首次将QED描述的Ps衰变物理先验融入概率框架，兼容标准核素（如¹⁸F），定位误差1.62 cm，较纯时间三边测量提升约2倍、较纯能量重建提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正电子发射断层成像（PET）中约40%的湮灭事件经历了正电子素（Ps）形成，其中ortho-Ps可衰变为三个光子，携带周围微环境的额外诊断信息（如三光子/双光子产额比）。然而现有正电子素寿命成像依赖瞬发光子，局限于特定核素。因此需要一种仅利用三光子、兼容标准核素（如¹⁸F）的三光子符合事件重建方法。

Method: 提出TRIO（Three-photon event-by-event Image Reconstruction with QED prior）算法，将三光子事件重建建模为贝叶斯最大后验（MAP）推断问题。该方法在一个统一概率框架中融合了：（1）基于时间的三角定位（trilateration）；（2）基于能量的重建；（3）首次引入源于QED对Ps衰变描述的物理信息先验。采用Siemens Biograph Quadra扫描仪建模进行蒙特卡罗模拟评估。

Result: 蒙特卡罗模拟显示：TRIO算法的平均定位误差为1.62 cm，比纯时间三角定位（3.05 cm）提升约2倍，比纯能量重建（约18 cm）提升约一个数量级。该贝叶斯方法兼容现有可记录三光子湮灭符合事件的飞行时间PET扫描仪。

Conclusion: TRIO算法首次将QED物理先验融入三光子事件重建，在不依赖瞬发光子的前提下实现了显著优于传统方法的定位精度，且与标准PET核素和现有TOF-PET硬件兼容，为基于三光子通道的PET微环境成像提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: PET provides functional images relying on two-photon coincidences from positron-electron annihilation. In human tissue, about 40\% of annihilations are preceded by Ps formation, of which o-Ps component partially decays into three photons, with the remainder annihilating via pick-off or spin-exchange into two photons. This three-photon channel carries additional information about the surrounding micro-environment, including the three-to-two-photon yield ratio as a potential diagnostic marker. We propose the TRIO algorithm, a novel three-photon event-by-event image reconstruction algorithm formulated as a Bayesian maximum a posteriori inference problem. TRIO unifies time-based trilateration, energy-based reconstruction and, for the first time, a physics-informed prior derived from the QED description of Ps decay within a single probabilistic framework.
  In contrast to positronium lifetime imaging, which requires a prompt photon and is therefore restricted to specific radionuclides, TRIO relies solely on the three photons and is fully compatible with standard radionuclides such as 18F.
  Monte Carlo simulation modelled after the Siemens Biograph Quadra scanner demonstrates a mean position error of 1.62~cm, improving by approximately a factor of two over the time-based trilateration (3.05 cm) and by about an order of magnitude over energy-based reconstruction alone (18 cm). More importantly, the proposed Bayesian approach is compatible with existing TOF-PET scanners that can register three-photon annihilation coincidences.

</details>

### [171] [Confinement and String Breaking in the Compact Abelian Higgs Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27550)
*Blake Senseman,Zane Ozzello,Yannick Meurice,Stephen Mrenna*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可在当前量子模拟设备上实现的简化模型——在qutrit格点上模拟的Compact Abelian Higgs模型的自旋-1截断，通过DMRG方法研究1+1维标量电动力学低能区的弦激发谱，并引入局域化学势来测量弦张力和介子质量等物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子色动力学(QCD)的实时模拟目前技术上仍不可行，但研究QCD唯象学要素的简化模型很多。作者旨在提出一个足够简单、能在当前模拟方法中实现禁闭与弦断裂现象的模型，填补现有晶格规范理论模拟在弦稳定性表征方面的空白。

Method: 采用Compact Abelian Higgs模型的自旋-1截断，在qutrit格点上进行模拟。在1+1维标量电动力学的低能区，积掉重模后得到一个有效自旋链哈密顿量，其中高斯定律被隐式满足。使用DMRG方法在约100个格点上研究弦状激发的能谱，并引入局域化学势模拟外电荷，通过调节化学势测量弦张力和有效介子质量。

Result: 成功在约100个格点规模的系统中展示了禁闭与弦断裂现象；通过变化的局域化学势，实现了对弦张力和有效介子质量的参数依赖测量；获得了此前晶格禁闭模型研究中未被评估的弦稳定性表征。

Conclusion: 该简化模型为当前量子模拟平台研究QCD-like现象（禁闭、弦断裂）提供了可行途径；化学势的引入为弦稳定性表征提供了新工具，补充了现有研究的不足。

Abstract: While real-time simulation of Quantum Chromodynamics remains technologically out of reach, simplified models for studying elements of QCD phenomenology abound. This work presents a simple model, a spin-1 truncation of the Compact Abelian Higgs Model simulated on qutrit sites, in which confinement and string breaking is accessible to current simulation methods. In the low-energy regime of 1+1D scalar electrodynamics, the heavy modes are integrated out, producing a spin chain effective Hamiltonian in which Gauss' law is implicitly satisfied. We study the spectrum of string-like excitations using DMRG methods on the order of 100 sites. We demonstrate that an added, local chemical potential, playing a role analogous to external charges, permits parameter-dependent measurements of physical features of interest like the string tension and effective meson mass. Varying the chemical potential also permits a characterization of string stability not assessed in prior studies of confining lattice models.

</details>


### [172] [Exact chiral symmetry with quantum signal processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28524)
*Henry Lamm,Alessandro Roggero,Hersh Singh,Luca Spagnoli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对重叠费米子哈密顿量的量子信号处理（QSP）算法，该算法可在可控误差 $ε_e$ 内保持 Ginsparg-Wilson 关系，使得具有精确手征对称性的狄拉克费米子量子模拟成本仅比 Wilson-Dirac 哈密顿量多一个对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在格点量子色动力学（LQCD）中，实现具有精确手征对称性的费米子模拟是一个基本挑战。重叠费米子满足 Ginsparg-Wilson 关系、可保持格点上的手征对称性，但计算成本高昂。本文旨在利用量子计算优势，为重叠费米子哈密顿量设计高效的量子模拟算法，降低量子比特和电路复杂度开销。

Method: 作者采用量子信号处理（QSP）框架，通过构造一个能有效实现重叠算符的量子电路。其核心思想是利用 QSP 将符号函数 $ε(h_W)$ 作用于 Wilson-Dirac 核 $h_W$，从而构造重叠哈密顿量。该方法在模拟重叠算符时，QSP 实际上构造了一个"额外维度"——这与畴壁费米子中重叠费米子作为边界态出现的深层物理图像一致。

Result: 该 QSP 算法的电路复杂度仅比 Wilson-Dirac 哈密顿量模拟多一个对数因子 $\log(1/ε_e)$。与畴壁费米子相比，电路复杂度有轻微增加，但量子比特成本显著降低。此外，QSP 构造额外维度的方式在数学上反映了畴壁费米子与重叠费米子之间的体-边界对应关系。

Conclusion: 量子信号处理为重叠费米子的高效量子模拟提供了一条可行路径，使得保持精确手征对称性的量子 LQCD 模拟成本大幅降低。算法的标度行为揭示了重叠费米子与畴壁费米子之间深层物理联系的量子对应，为格点场论的量子模拟开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We give a quantum signal processing (QSP) algorithm for the overlap fermion Hamiltonian which preserves the Ginsparg-Wilson relation up to a controllable error $ε_e$. Quantum simulations of Dirac fermions with exact chiral symmetry are thus nearly free: applying the overlap Hamiltonian costs only a factor logarithmic in $ε_e$ more than the Wilson-Dirac Hamiltonian. Comparing to domain-wall fermions, a mild overhead is found in circuit complexity while reducing qubit costs. We show how QSP effectively constructs an extra dimension when simulating the overlap operator, illustrating that the scaling of quantum algorithms reflects the deeper physics of overlap fermions arising at the boundary of domain-wall fermions.

</details>

### [173] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了在标准d-单纯形上，相对于负熵的L-光滑凸函数类中，任何一阶方法的最优收敛速率下界为Ω(L/T)，当d=Ω(T²)时成立，表明镜像下降在该类中是对数因子意义下最优的。结果进一步扩展至量子设定（von Neumann熵相对光滑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究(Dragomir et al., 2022)表明相对光滑条件下加速可能不可行，但其构造的prox函数是病态的且与困难实例共同构造。本文旨在证明：即便对于结构特别良好的具体prox函数（负熵），加速同样不可能实现，从而揭示相对光滑性下非加速现象并非仅由病态构造所致。

Method: 采用下界构造法，在d-单纯形上构造满足L-相对光滑（相对于负熵）的凸函数类，结合对抗性oracle分析框架，证明任何一阶确定性方法在该类上迭代T步后的最优性间隙至少为Ω(L/T)。条件d=Ω(T²)确保单纯形维度足够大以嵌入困难实例。进一步通过矩阵提升技巧，将构造推广至spectrahedron上的von Neumann熵相对光滑情形。

Result: 建立了Ω(L/T)的收敛速率下界，与镜像下降的上界O(L log d/T)相匹配（在对数因子意义下），表明镜像下降在该函数类中近乎最优。同时证明即便对于负熵这一自然且结构良好的prox函数，加速也无法实现，与ℓ₁范数光滑下可用加速方法形成鲜明对比。量子设定下得到相同下界。

Conclusion: 相对光滑性框架下，加速方法的缺失不是prox函数病态构造的产物，而是相对光滑概念本身的内在局限——即便是负熵这样结构优良的prox函数也会导致非加速现象。该结果为相对光滑条件下的一阶方法设计确立了基本的理论边界。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>

### [174] [Third-order nonlinear transport in a percolative two-dimensional superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27641)
*Wenjun Liu,Chenghe Wang,Xiubin Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Tao Zhang,Xiao-Xiao Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双层范德华超导体1T'-MoTe₂的渗流转变区观测到显著的三阶非线性输运现象，三次谐波电压与激励电流呈立方关系，可用含时Ginzburg-Landau理论的超导涨落机制来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华材料中因维度降低、量子涨落增强及复杂电子-声子相互作用，常出现渗流超导电性，正常电子与Cooper对共存。然而，这一体系中高阶非线性输运现象尚缺乏系统研究，需探索灵敏探针来表征渗流体系中的超导转变。

Method: 以三层1T'-MoTe₂超导体为研究对象，在渗流转变区测量三次谐波纵向电压（V_∥^{3ω}）随激励电流的变化关系，并通过含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中超导涨落模型对非线性响应进行半定量分析。

Result: 三次谐波纵向电压在阈值以下与激励电流呈现清晰的立方依赖关系，其幅值和非线性系数均与超导态强烈关联；含时Ginzburg-Landau理论中的超导涨落机制可半定量地捕捉该非线性输运行为。

Conclusion: 三阶非线性输运可作为渗流体系中超导转变的灵敏探针，为强关联系统中高阶输运现象的探索奠定了基础。

Abstract: Percolative superconductivity frequently arises in two-dimensional van der Waals materials due to reduced dimensionality, enhanced quantum fluctuations, and complex electron-phonon interactions, providing a unique platform where normal electrons coexist with Cooper pairs. We report the observation of substantial third-order nonlinear transport in a trilayer $1T^\prime$-MoTe$_2$ superconductor within its percolative transition regime. The third-harmonic longitudinal voltage ($V_{\|}^{3ω}$) exhibits a clear cubic dependence on excitation current below a threshold, with both its magnitude and nonlinear coefficient strongly correlated with the superconducting state. This nonlinear response is semiquantitatively captured by the superconducting fluctuation within the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory, where nonlinear transport arises due to fluctuating Cooper pairs. Our results demonstrate that third-order nonlinear transport serves as a sensitive probe of superconducting transitions in percolative systems and establish a foundation for exploring higher-order transport phenomena in strongly correlated systems.

</details>


### [175] [Anomalous metal and superconducting phases in rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28425)
*Anna Okounkova,Abigail Sohm,Tobias Faehndrich,Manish Kumar,Derek Waleffe,Jiaqiang Yan,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Joshua Folk,Matthew Yankowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在菱方石墨烯/WSe₂异质结中，研究者系统地研究了超导转变中电阻未能降至零的异常现象，发现栅极空间中存在零电阻超导态与有限电阻饱和态共存的现象，后者重现了"反常金属态"的关键特征，为理解这一长期未解之谜提供了新的可调平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于石墨烯的二维超导系统中，许多栅极调控相虽表现出清晰的超导迹象，但电阻在降温时并不消失而是饱和在有限值。这一现象与薄膜超导体中观察到的"反常金属态"高度相似，该状态尽管已被发现数十年，但仍缺乏公认的理论解释。研究者旨在利用超洁净的菱方石墨烯平台对该行为进行系统研究。

Method: 研究者在WSe₂衬底上的菱方石墨烯器件中，系统扫描栅极空间，在零磁场下识别零电阻超导区与有限电阻饱和区，并测量这两种状态对温度、垂直磁场、面内磁场以及偏置电流的响应，对比二者的临界行为与相边界演化。

Result: 在零磁场下，零电阻超导态与有限电阻饱和态在栅极空间中表现为孤立的口袋区域，但二者在温度、垂直磁场和电流超过临界值时均呈现出向正常态的突变转变，现象高度相似。施加小面内磁场可使这些口袋扩展并融合，在毫开尔文基温下形成零电阻态与有限电阻态之间的锐利边界。有限电阻态复现了反常金属态的关键现象学特征。

Conclusion: 该体系的超高可调性与可重复性对基于无序或非均匀性等外在机制的解释构成了强约束，表明反常金属态可能具有内在的物理起源。菱方石墨烯为最终理解反常金属态提供了一个理想的实验平台。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductivity is now well established in graphene-based systems, with many such realizations showing evidence for unconventional pairing. Yet in several of the gate-tuned phases that otherwise exhibit clear signatures of superconductivity, the resistance does not vanish as temperature is lowered, instead saturating at a finite value. Here we report a systematic study of this behavior in rhombohedral graphene on a WSe$_2$ substrate, finding regions of gate space with zero-resistance superconductivity alongside others with finite saturation resistance. At zero magnetic field, these regions appear as isolated pockets in gate space that otherwise exhibit strikingly similar phenomenology, including abrupt transitions to the normal state as temperature, perpendicular magnetic field, and current are raised above critical values. A small in-plane field expands and merges these pockets without qualitatively altering their behavior, producing a sharp boundary at millikelvin base temperature between states of zero or finite resistance. The finite-resistance state reproduces key phenomenology associated with the anomalous metal, a state that has been observed in thin-film superconductors for decades but lacks an accepted theoretical explanation. The tunability and reproducibility of ultra-clean rhombohedral graphene place strong constraints on extrinsic explanations and provide a new platform for understanding this behavior.

</details>

### [176] [Clifford-Appell formulation of a Dirac-type Kronig-Penney model in condensed matter physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27920)
*L. Aceto,P. A. Grassi,H. Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(N+1)维Clifford分析框架下研究广义Kronig-Penney模型的稳态Dirac方程，通过引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子和双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基，将自由传播问题转化为解耦的广义Helmholtz方程，并给出显式级数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Kronig-Penney模型中的自由Dirac传播问题建立一个统一的Clifford分析框架，将一阶耦合系统转化为可解耦的方程，并寻求显式解析解。

Method: 首先在相互作用位点之外将自由Dirac系统用广义Cauchy-Riemann算子重新表述为两个Clifford值场的一阶耦合系统；然后利用特征超复变量将其约化为一对解耦的广义Helmholtz方程；最后引入适应于广义Cauchy-Riemann算子的双变量Clifford-Appell多项式基，将微分问题转化为展开系数的代数递推，得到解的显式级数表示。

Result: 成功将自由Dirac传播问题转化为解耦的广义Helmholtz方程，并通过Clifford-Appell多项式展开得到解的显式级数表示，建立了广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统之间的显式联系。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为自由传播问题提供了统一的Clifford解析表述，并在超复函数论中揭示了广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统之间的深层关联。

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

</details>


### [177] [Redundant moments of non-melonic random tensor models: simplifying the tensor bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28518)
*Carlos I. Perez-Sanchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意张量模型中大N极限下"melon族"矩的冗余性，即任何melon算符的期望值仅依赖于其阶数，从而减少了张量bootstrap中半正定矩阵的独立条目数量，且该结果适用范围不限于melon张量模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量bootstrap方法依赖于由半正定矩阵构成的核心结构（类似于格点规范理论中的Toeplitz矩阵和随机矩阵bootstrap中的Hankel矩阵）。减少这些矩阵中独立条目的数量对于简化计算、提高bootstrap效率至关重要。作者旨在通过证明melon族矩的冗余性来系统性地压缩这些矩阵的规模。

Method: 作者采用大N极限下的解析方法，证明任意melon算符C的期望值仅通过其"阶数"（degree）依赖于C本身，而与C的具体结构无关。进一步，该结论被推广至melon矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程，证明其同样具备这种仅依赖于阶数的简化性质。

Result: 主要结果有两个：(1) 大N下任意melon算符的期望值仅依赖于其阶数；(2) melon矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程同样具备该性质。由此，张量bootstrap中作为核心的半正定矩阵的独立条目数量得以显著减少。该结果的适用范围超出了melon张量模型本身。

Conclusion: 该工作为张量bootstrap方法提供了一种系统性的简化手段，通过证明melon族矩的冗余性有效压缩了半正定矩阵的维度。这一结论的强度在于其普遍性——不受限于特定类型的melon张量模型，具有广泛的适用前景。

Abstract: We prove the redundancy of a family (the so-called melonic family) of large-$N$ moments in arbitrary tensor models. This reduces the number of independent entries in the positive semidefinite matrices that build the core of the tensor bootstrap (for tensor models there are already several generalizations of the positive semidefinite Toeplitz and Hankel matrices of lattice gauge theory and random matrix bootstrap, respectively). More concretely, we prove that any melonic operator $C$ at large-$N$ has an expectation value that depends on $C$ exclusively through its degree. A similar statement is shown here to hold for the Schwinger-Dyson equations of melonic moments. The strength of our result is also its scope, which is not limited to melonic tensor models.

</details>

### [178] [The cycle C9 does not admit uniform mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28345)
*Alison Gray,Pransu Patel,Isaiah Young*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何和Gröbner基技术证明九阶圈图$C_9$在任何时刻均不允准均匀混合，从而否定了所有循环9-根的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究圈图上连续时间量子游走（CTQW）的均匀混合问题。均匀混合是量子游走中的重要性质，关系到量子态在图上能否均匀分布。此前哪些圈图可达到均匀混合是开放问题，本文旨在对$C_9$给出否定回答。

Method: 采用代数几何方法，将均匀混合条件转化为多项式方程组，进而利用Gröbner基技术系统性地排除所有"循环9-根"（即与$C_9$均匀混合相关的代数条件）的存在性，从而严格证明不可混合性。

Result: 证明圈图$C_9$在任何时刻均不允准均匀混合（does not admit uniform mixing at any time）。

Conclusion: 通过代数几何和Gröbner基的计算方法，完成了对$C_9$不可均匀混合的严格证明，填补了圈图CTQW均匀混合分类中的一个重要空白。

Abstract: We study continuous-time quantum walks (CTQWs) on cycles. In particular, we prove that the cycle $C_9$ does not admit uniform mixing at any time via algebraic geometry and Gröbner basis techniques to rule out all cyclic 9-roots.

</details>

### [179] [Signatures of kinetic gravity braiding in cosmological probes of the gravitational field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28207)
*Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz,Farbod Hassani,Julian Adamek,Emilio Bellini,Martin Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用相对论N体模拟研究了动力学引力编织（KGB）模型在相对论宇宙学探针中的观测特征，发现标量场与度规的导数耦合显著修正了引力势的振幅和演化，在ISW-RS效应和弱引力透镜中产生可达百分之几十的尺度依赖性偏差，非线性效应在小尺度上不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGB模型作为修正引力理论的重要候选者，其标量场与度规的导数耦合会在宇宙学尺度上产生可观测的引力效应，但以往研究多局限于线性微扰理论，缺乏对非线性演化和相对论性光锥效应的全面分析。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统研究KGB模型在多种相对论宇宙学探针中的完整观测特征。

Method: 使用相对论N体程序KGB-evolution生成光锥输出，计算弱引力透镜收敛、Shapiro时间延迟、积分Sachs-Wolfe和Rees-Sciama（ISW-RS）效应以及引力红移等可观测量，构建全天图与角功率谱，并与k-essence模型及线性微扰理论预测进行系统比较。

Result: 导数耦合同时修正引力势的振幅和时间演化，产生从百分之几到百分之几十的尺度依赖性偏差。ISW-RS信号响应最大：在ISW主导区，Weyl势衰减变慢压制了KGB信号；而在高多极矩处非线性演化反转这一趋势，相对k-essence产生百分之几十的差异。弱引力透镜在小尺度（ℓ~10²-10³）相对k-essence增强约10-12%。线性理论能准确描述大尺度行为，小尺度必须包含非线性修正。

Conclusion: KGB模型的导数耦合在多种相对论宇宙学探针中留下显著且互补的观测印记，ISW-RS效应和弱引力透镜是最有希望的探针。可靠的KGB理论预测必须包含非线性效应，这为利用未来高精度巡天数据检验修正引力理论提供了重要的理论基准。

Abstract: We study the observational signatures of kinetic gravity braiding (KGB) models in relativistic cosmological probes constructed along the past light cone. Using the relativistic $N$-body code KGB-evolution, we generate light-cone outputs and compute several observables that directly probe the gravitational field, including weak gravitational lensing convergence, Shapiro time delay, the integrated Sachs-Wolfe and Rees-Sciama (ISW-RS) effects, and gravitational redshift. Full-sky maps and angular power spectra of these quantities are constructed and compared with $k$-essence models and predictions from linear perturbation theory. We find that the derivative coupling between the scalar field and the metric modifies both the amplitude and the time evolution of the gravitational potentials, producing scale-dependent deviations ranging from a few percent to tens of percent. In particular, the ISW-RS signal exhibits the largest fractional response, as the slower decay of the Weyl potential suppresses the KGB signal in the ISW-dominated regime, whereas nonlinear evolution reverses this trend at higher multipoles, producing differences of tens of percent relative to $k$-essence. Weak gravitational lensing also provides a strong complementary probe and, for the model considered here, exhibits clear deviations from the $k$-essence prediction at small scales with enhancements up to $\sim 10$-$12\%$ at multipoles $\ell \sim 10^2$-$10^3$. Our results show that linear perturbation theory accurately describes the large-scale behaviour, while nonlinear effects become important at smaller scales, particularly for the ISW-RS signal and, more moderately, for the convergence, and must therefore be included for reliable theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [180] [Nonlinear Dynamics near the Threshold of Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27343)
*Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用数值相对论模拟，研究了标量场在自引力坍缩过程中，微扰理论（含NNLO效应）的适用范围。研究发现，当过程峰值光度达到约 10⁻² 普朗克光度时，系统开始偏离微扰预测，并在黑洞形成前表现出非线性效应，如驱动频率红移与幂律谱，后者与近似离散自相似性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰理论是建模和解释引力动力学（如双黑洞并合）的核心工具，但对其适用范围缺乏精确理解。标量场自引力坍缩为研究微扰与非微扰区域之间的过渡提供了干净的实验平台。

Method: 采用数值相对论模拟，对标量场在自引力作用下的坍缩过程进行求解。通过改变场的初始振幅，实现从微扰（近平坦时空散射）到非微扰（有限时间内形成黑洞）区域的平滑过渡。微扰展开包含至次次领头阶（NNLO）效应。

Result: 当峰值光度达到 L_peak ~ 10⁻² L_Planck 时，模拟结果开始偏离微扰理论预测。非线性效应表现为驱动频率的红移以及功率谱呈幂律形式，后者与近似离散自相似性相符。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解更现实强引力现象（如非球对称坍缩、高速黑洞并合）中微扰展开的极限迈出了重要一步，揭示了微扰理论在接近黑洞形成阈值时失效的具体特征与定量判据。

Abstract: Perturbation theory is an essential tool to model and interpret gravitational dynamics, for example, binary black hole mergers. Therefore it is also crucial to precisely understand its regimes of validity. The collapse of a scalar field under its own self-gravity provides a clean laboratory to study these questions. By varying the field's initial amplitude we can transition smoothly between a perturbative regime, where the field scatters in an approximately flat spacetime; and a nonperturbative regime, where a black hole forms in finite time. In this work, we use numerical relativity simulations of this set-up to investigate the accuracy of a perturbative expansion around flat spacetime, including next-to-next-to-leading order effects. Our simulations show deviations from these perturbative predictions before black hole formation, once the maximum luminosity of the process is sufficiently large, $L_{peak} \sim 10^{-2} L_{Planck}$. We characterize these nonlinear effects including a redshift of the driving frequency and a power-law spectral amplitude, which we show is consistent with approximate discrete self-similarity. These results provide a step forward towards understanding the limits of perturbative expansions in more realistic strong-gravity phenomena such as non-spherical collapse and high-velocity black hole mergers.

</details>


### [181] [Inflation in unimodular loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27344)
*Steffen Gielen,Rita B. Neves*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单模圈量子宇宙学框架下研究暴胀，采用具有清晰几何意义的单模时间（而非通常的自由无质量标量场）来定义时间演化，推导了半经典有效方程的解析解与数值解，并展示了α-attractor势能可实现与观测兼容的反弹模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准圈量子宇宙学（LQC）通常使用一个自由无质量标量场作为"时钟"来定义时间演化，但这种选择依赖于特定物质内容，缺乏独立的几何意义。单模引力框架则提供了不依赖物质场的、具有清晰几何意义的时间坐标，因此作者试图将暴胀研究纳入单模LQC框架，以探索更自然的量子宇宙学时间演化。

Method: 作者首先回顾了单模圈量子宇宙学的理论结构，并讨论了其与完整单模圈量子引力的可能联系。然后，聚焦于半经典有效方程，在简单情形（如常数势）下推导解析解，强调单模时间坐标的使用。对于唯象上感兴趣的情形（如二次势和Starobinsky暴胀），采用数值求解，并比较不同初始条件选择的影响。特别地，引入α-attractor势来构建由动能或势能主导的反弹模型。

Result: （1）在常数势等简单情形下获得了有效方程的解析解；（2）对二次势和Starobinsky暴胀模型进行了数值求解；（3）证明了选择α-attractor势可以实现在反弹时由动能主导或势能主导的不同模型，这些模型与观测兼容，且可能保留量子引力区间的可观测印记。

Conclusion: 单模圈量子宇宙学为暴胀宇宙学提供了一个具有清晰几何时间定义的自然框架。α-attractor势在该框架下尤其有吸引力，能够产生与观测兼容的反弹模型，并可能在宇宙微波背景或其他观测量中留下量子引力效应的痕迹。

Abstract: We study inflation in the setting of unimodular loop quantum cosmology, where time evolution is defined in unimodular time rather than with respect to a free, massless scalar field as is standard in loop quantum cosmology. The unimodular setting leads to a natural Schrödinger time evolution in a time coordinate with clear geometric meaning, defined independently of any particular matter content; an inflaton can be included but is not needed as a clock. We review the unimodular version of loop quantum cosmology and comment on possible connections to full (unimodular) loop quantum gravity. Then, focusing on semiclassical effective equations, we derive analytical solutions in simple cases such as a constant potential, emphasising the use of a unimodular time coordinate. We also discuss numerical solutions for phenomenologically interesting cases such as a quadratic potential and Starobinsky inflation, comparing different possible choices of initial conditions. In particular, we show that choosing an $α$-attractor potential allows for models of a bounce either dominated by kinetic or potential energy, which are compatible with observations while potentially including observable imprints of the quantum-gravity regime.

</details>


### [182] [Gauge vs (hidden) physical symmetries of FLRW cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27351)
*Andrea Calcinari,Adrià Delhom,Federico Greco,Daniele Oriti,Néstor Rivero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在平坦FLRW时空与n个自由无质量标量场的模型中，共形Killing矢量生成守恒的Dirac可观测量，其Poisson代数为最大共形代数so(n+1,2)。Eisenhart-Duval提升显示规范对称性代数依赖于规范选择，在调和规范中扩大为Schrödinger代数（非物理对称性），而退参数化将提升荷映射回共形代数的子代数。该框架可分离规范对称性与物理对称性，且适用于有势情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义协变理论中坐标时间演化是规范变换，规范固定可使某些对称性显现，但这些对称性未必是物理动力学的真实对称性；退参数化消除规范对称性但可能隐藏依赖于物理时钟的物理对称性。作者旨在澄清规范对称性与（隐藏的）物理对称性之间的关系，并将单场结果推广到任意n个标量场。

Method: (1) 利用极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成Dirac可观测量守恒荷，计算其Poisson代数结构；(2) 在一族规范中进行Eisenhart-Duval提升，分析规范固定后显现的对称性代数对规范选择的依赖性；(3) 通过退参数化将提升荷映射回规范不变的Dirac可观测量，比较不同规范下恢复的代数。

Result: 极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成的守恒荷为Dirac可观测量，其Poisson代数为最大共形代数conf(n,1)≃so(n+1,2)。Eisenhart-Duval提升中，规范对称性代数依赖规范选择：在调和规范（规范固定后极小超空间度规变平坦）中扩大为Schrödinger代数，但该代数并非物理对称性。退参数化将提升荷映射到Dirac可观测量，这些可观测量始终实现共形代数的一个子代数，仅在调和规范中恢复完整共形代数。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在极小超空间模型中分离规范对称性与物理对称性的系统框架，恢复了约化相空间描述在结构上无法看到的守恒荷，且方法可推广至存在标量势的情形。

Abstract: In generally covariant theories evolution in coordinate time is a gauge transformation, so that a symmetry made manifest in a gauge-fixed description need not be a symmetry of the physical dynamics. Deparametrisation, in turn, removes gauge symmetries but may hide physical symmetries, in particular those dependent on the chosen physical clock. We study the relation between gauge and (hidden) physical symmetries in flat FLRW geometry coupled to an arbitrary number $n$ of free massless scalar fields. We show that conformal Killing vectors of the minisuperspace metric generate conserved charges which are Dirac observables--hence gauge-invariant--and whose Poisson algebra is the maximal conformal algebra $\mathfrak{conf}(n,1)\simeq\mathfrak{so}(n+1,2)$, extending previous single-field results to arbitrary $n$. We then revisit the Eisenhart-Duval lift in a family of gauges and show that the manifest symmetry algebra is gauge dependent, enlarging to the Schrödinger algebra (which is thus not a physical symmetry) in the distinguished harmonic gauge where the gauge-fixed minisuperspace metric becomes flat. Further, deparametrisation maps the lifted charges to gauge-invariant Dirac observables, which always realise a subalgebra of the conformal algebra and reproduce it in full in the harmonic gauge. These results establish a framework for separating gauge from physical symmetries in minisuperspace models, recovering charges to which reduced phase-space descriptions are structurally blind, and remaining applicable in the presence of potentials.

</details>


### [183] [Structural morphology and the gravitational arrow of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27526)
*Julian Barbour,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究牛顿N体问题中零能量、零线动量和零角动量扇区内的Janus点解。作者考察了两个N=5000的平面中心构型，发现尽管它们的无量纲多样性V（一种全局尺度不变的聚类对比度度量）仅相差1.686%，但局部形态学指标——六邻域各向异性A₆——的均值却相差156.8%。这表明全局量V本身并不唯一地确定局部几何组织，需要引入局部关系可观测量作为补充描述层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Barbour-Koslowski-Mercati引力箭头中，全局标量V所编码的结构信息是否足以唯一确定系统的局部形态。文章旨在通过中心构型作为受控探针，定量揭示全局多样性与局部几何组织之间的关系，为连接静态形状空间景观与Janus点历史中的形态提供桥梁。

Method: 使用两个平面N=5000中心构型的数值粒子坐标数据；以六邻域各向异性A₆（取值0到1，0表示各向同性局部环境，1表示有效一维环境）量化局部形态；比较两构型的V值差异、A₆均值差异、A₆>0.5的粒子比例；对邻域大小k=4到12进行稳健性检验，并实施无量纲近对稳健性测试。

Result: 两中心构型的V值仅相差1.686%，但A₆均值分别为0.1331和0.3419，相差156.8%；高V构型中有18.1%的粒子满足A₆>0.5，而低V构型中为零；各向异性排序在所有测试的邻域大小（4≤k≤12）下持续成立；主要对比在无量纲近对稳健性测试中得以保持。

Conclusion: 全局多样性V这一标量虽能刻画BKM引力箭头的长期行为，但仅凭它无法唯一确定系统的局部形态。局部关系可观测量构成了一个互补的描述层次，必须与全局量结合才能完整理解Janus点历史中形态与形状空间景观之间的定量联系。

Abstract: The Newtonian $N$-body problem in the zero-energy, zero-linear-momentum, and zero-angular-momentum sector provides a time-reversal-invariant setting in which generic complete solutions possess a Janus point and exhibit a gravitational arrow on both branches away from it. The dimensionless variety $V$, a global scale-invariant measure of clustering contrast, is not pointwise monotonic but fluctuates while growing between rising bounds away from the Janus region. Central configurations are critical shapes of the same scale-invariant landscape and therefore provide controlled probes of the structural information encoded by $V$. We investigate this question for two planar $N=5000$ central configurations using the numerical particle-coordinate data. Local morphology is quantified by the six-neighbour anisotropy $A_6$, which ranges from $0$ for an isotropic local environment to $1$ for an effectively one-dimensional one. Although the varieties of the two configurations differ by only $1.686\%$, their mean anisotropies differ by $156.8\%$, from $0.1331$ to $0.3419$. Moreover, $18.1\%$ of the particles in the higher-variety configuration satisfy $A_6>0.5$, whereas none do so in the lower-variety configuration. The anisotropy ordering persists for all tested neighbourhood sizes $4\le k\le12$, and the principal contrast survives a dimensionless close-pair robustness test. For this pair of critical shapes, nearby values of the global variety therefore coexist with markedly different local geometrical organization. Thus the scalar quantity whose long-term behavior characterizes the BKM gravitational arrow does not, by itself, uniquely specify morphology. This identifies local relational observables as a complementary level of description and provides a quantitative bridge between the static shape-space landscape and morphology along genuine Janus-point histories.

</details>


### [184] [Tidal Love numbers of multi-state Boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27587)
*Xin-Lei Zhao,Jun-Ru Chen,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由基态和第一激发态复标量场构成的多态玻色星（MSBS）的潮汐Love数。在同步与非同步频率条件下，背景解分为单支和双支类型。研究发现电Love数在特定参数阈值下会从正值突变为负值，而磁Love数始终为负且绝对值更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补多态玻色星潮汐Love数研究的空白，探索复合标量场构型下星体对外部潮汐场的响应特性，为引力波探测中区分不同致密天体模型提供理论依据。

Method: 构建由基态和第一激发态复标量场组成的多态玻色星模型，在同步与非同步频率条件下求解背景解，分类为单支和双支类型；计算四极（ℓ=2）电场型和磁场型潮汐Love数，并分析不同参数（如约化质量参数μ̃₁和约化频率参数ω̃₀）对结果的影响。

Result: 电Love数在参数μ̃₁ > 0.891或ω̃₀ > 0.777时出现正转负的突变，低于阈值则保持正值；磁Love数始终为负，且绝对值始终小于电Love数。

Conclusion: 多态玻色星的电潮汐Love数存在符号翻转现象，这一特征可用于通过引力波观测区分多态玻色星与其他致密天体。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the tidal Love numbers of multi-state boson stars (MSBSs) composed of ground state and first excited state complex scalar fields. Under synchronized and nonsynchronized frequency conditions, the background solutions of MSBSs are classified into single-branch and double-branch types. The field functions, ADM mass, and binding energy of different solutions are discussed. We then calculate the quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) electric and magnetic tidal Love numbers for branches containing stable solutions. Our results show that the electric tidal Love numbers are initially positive and then suddenly transition to negative values. This phenomenon occurs when the parameters satisfy $\tildeμ_1 > 0.891$ or $\tildeω_0 > 0.777$; for smaller values of these parameters, the electric Love numbers remain positive. The magnetic tidal Love numbers are always negative, with absolute values smaller than those of the electric tidal Love numbers.

</details>


### [185] [Degenerate Limits of Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27801)
*Bekir Baytaş,Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了标量-张量引力理论在哈密顿联络动力学表述中的退化极限。通过对空间三元组进行定域标度变换，实现了几何量和关系的退化，并推导了退化标量-张量理论的约束条件，讨论了其物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量-张量引力理论在退化几何极限下的行为，以理解该理论在奇点附近或低维有效理论中的结构与物理内涵，尤其是退化空间三元组如何影响引力理论的约束结构和动力学。

Method: 采用哈密顿联络动力学（Ashtekar 变量）表述标量-张量引力；通过对空间三元组施加定域标度变换，系统地导出退化极限下所有几何量（如联络、曲率、标量场耦合项）的退化形式；在此基础上推导退化理论的一类约束和第二类约束，并分析约束代数。

Result: 成功导出了退化标量-张量引力理论的完整约束集合，包括高斯约束、向量约束和标量约束的退化对应形式；揭示了退化极限下约束代数可能的修正，以及标量场非最小耦合在退化几何中的表现。

Conclusion: 退化标量-张量理论的哈密顿联络动力学框架具有自洽的约束结构，该退化极限可能为理解引力奇点附近的物理（如宇宙学奇点、黑洞内部）及与低维有效理论的联系提供新的视角。

Abstract: We study the degenerate limits of the scalar-tensor theory of gravity in its Hamiltonian connection dynamics formulation. The degenerate limit of the spatial triad is effectuated by a local scaling transformation of the triad, which leads to the degenerate limits of all other geometric quantities and relations in the connection dynamics. We derive the constraints for the degenerate scalar-tensor theory of gravity and discuss their physical implications.

</details>


### [186] [Quartic Scalar Clouds on Fixed Kerr Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28044)
*Hendrik Mennenga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在固定Kerr和Schwarzschild背景下，研究具有无界标量势的复标量场的稳态非线性云。在Kerr背景上发现满足同步条件ω=mΩ_H的有限振幅Q云，它们占据(Ω_H, M_K)平面的二维区域，并在振幅趋于零时退化为线性云。四次势区域可延续至Ω_H=0，此时解变为Schwarzschild背景上的静态非线性标量云。还计算了轴子启发的余弦势的存在域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复标量场在固定黑洞背景上的非线性云结构，旨在探索超越线性层面的标量云解及其与黑洞旋转的同步机制，并理解不同标量势（无界四次势、有界余弦势）对解的存在性的影响。

Method: 在固定Kerr和Schwarzschild时空背景下，考虑复标量场与无界标量势（四次势）及轴子启发的余弦势耦合。通过施加同步条件ω=mΩ_H（标量场频率与黑洞视界角速度的整数倍同步），数值求解非线性场方程，寻找有限振幅的Q云解，并追踪其在参数空间(Ω_H, M_K)中的存在域。

Result: 在Kerr背景上发现了有限振幅Q云，占据(Ω_H, M_K)平面的二维存在域；当标量场振幅趋于零时，Q云退化为已知的线性云。四次势情形下，存在域可延续至Ω_H=0，此时同步频率和Noether荷均消失，解变为Schwarzschild背景上的静态非线性标量云。还给出了轴子型余弦势的Q云存在域。

Conclusion: 非线性Q云是线性标量云的自然推广，其存在域在参数空间中具有二维延拓性。无界势允许解延续至静态Schwarzschild情形，而有界余弦势的存在域则呈现不同结构，揭示了标量势形式对黑洞标量云物理的重要影响。

Abstract: We study stationary nonlinear clouds of a complex scalar field on fixed Kerr and Schwarzschild backgrounds, with an unbounded scalar potential. On Kerr backgrounds we find finite-amplitude Q-clouds satisfying the synchronisation condition $ω=mΩ_H$. They occupy two-dimensional regions of the ($Ω_H,M_K$) plane and reduce to the corresponding linear clouds as the scalar field amplitude tends to zero. The quartic domain can continue to $Ω_H=0$, where both the synchronised frequency and the Noether charge vanish and the solutions become static nonlinear scalar clouds on a fixed Schwarzschild background. We also compute the existence domain for a normalised, axion-inspired cosine potential.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>
