### [187] [Spin-incoherent Mott-Thouless pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27321)
*Ajesh Kumar,Urban F. P. Seifert,Erez Berg,Achim Rosch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一类Mott-Thouless泵，可在高熵、自旋非相干的Mott态中实现受指数保护的量子化输运，同时指出另一类缺乏此保护的泵中自旋激发会迅速破坏量子化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Thouless泵要求系统始终保持基态且热力学熵为零，但实际多体系统中熵不可避免。作者旨在探索是否存在能在高熵Mott绝缘态中依然维持量子化输运的泵浦机制，并受近期实验启发，分析不同Mott-Thouless泵的鲁棒性差异。

Method: 解析推导：证明在特定Mott-Thouless泵中，泵浦电荷存在于一个有能隙的扇区，该扇区与热的自旋自由度动力学解耦，从而获得指数级保护。对比分析另一类泵中自旋激发通过有效耦合产生电荷激发从而破坏量子化输运的机制。数值验证：采用有限系统的数值精确实时模拟支撑解析结果。

Result: 发现一类Mott-Thouless泵尽管具有广延熵，仍展现受指数保护的量子化电荷输运，因为电荷扇区与自旋扇区动力学解耦。另一类泵缺乏此保护，自旋激发可有效生成电荷激发，导致量子化输运迅速崩溃。

Conclusion: 高熵Mott态中可以实现鲁棒的量子化输运，关键在于电荷与自旋自由度之间的动力学解耦；这一发现拓展了Thouless泵的适用范围，并对相关实验实现具有指导意义。

Abstract: A Thouless pump describes a system in which a quantized amount of charge is transported by one lattice spacing per cycle when the parameters of a Hamiltonian are varied slowly and periodically. In the standard case, this quantization requires the system to remain in its ground state with vanishing thermodynamic entropy throughout the pumping process. Here, we introduce a class of Mott-Thouless pumps, which operate in highly entropic, spin-incoherent Mott states. We show analytically that these states exhibit exponentially protected quantized transport despite their extensive entropy, because the pumped charge resides in a gapped sector that remains dynamically decoupled from the hot spin degrees of freedom. By contrast, motivated by a recent experimental realization, we identify other classes of Mott-Thouless pumps that lack this protection. In these systems, spin excitations can efficiently generate charge excitations, leading to a rapid breakdown of quantized transport. Our analytical results are supported by numerically exact real-time simulations of finite systems.

</details>


### [188] [Altermagnetism from a Cu-Fe Lieb Lattice in FeSe/Cuprate Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27331)
*Ying Li,Augustin Davignon,Peng Rao,Runhan Li,Maia G. Vergniory,Roser Valentí,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在FeSe/铜氧化物异质结中实现交变磁性（altermagnetism），利用45°扭转形成CuFe₂ Lieb晶格，通过Fe磁序和配体介导的Cu-Fe杂化诱导d波自旋劈裂，并结合DFT计算验证了两种可行机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高温超导铜氧化物体系中实现交变磁性，可为零净磁化下的自旋劈裂电子能带研究及其与非常规超导电性的相互作用提供直接途径。

Method: 构建FeSe/铜氧化物异质结平台，将Cu层与Fe层45°扭转形成有效CuFe₂ Lieb晶格；建立最小紧束缚模型描述Fe磁序与配体介导的Cu-Fe杂化诱导的d波自旋劈裂机制；利用DFT计算验证FeSe/Bi₂Sr₂CuO₆异质结中两种机制（体机制与近邻转移机制）的可行性。

Result: （1）提出了两种实现交变磁性的机制：一是CuFe₂ Lieb晶格中配体杂化诱导的d波自旋劈裂；二是衬底诱导FeSe中两Se位不等价，使交变磁性起源于Fe层并通过近邻效应转移到铜氧化物层。（2）DFT计算证实了两种机制的可行性，并揭示了增强自旋劈裂的方法。

Conclusion: 超导铜氧化物/过渡金属硫族化物异质结是工程化交变磁性并研究其与非常规超导电性耦合的有前景的平台。

Abstract: Realizing altermagnetism in high-$T_c$ cuprate-based systems would provide a direct route for studying spin-split electronic bands in the absence of net magnetization and investigate their interplay with unconventional superconductivity. Here, we propose that FeSe/cuprate heterostructures offer such a platform, where a 45$^\circ$ twist of Cu and Fe layers creates an effective CuFe$_2$ Lieb lattice in which Fe magnetic order and Cu-Fe hybridization through the ligands induces altermagnetic $d$-wave spin splitting. A minimal tight-binding model shows that this mechanism is generic. Furthermore, a substrate-induced inequivalence of the two Se sites in FeSe provides a second route in which altermagnetism originates in the Fe layer and is transferred to the cuprate layer by proximity. Density functional theory calculations for FeSe/Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CuO$_6$ heterostructures confirm the viability of both mechanisms and reveal ways to enhance the spin splitting. These results establish superconducting cuprate/transition metal chalcogenide heterostructures as a promising setting for engineering altermagnetism and studying its coupling to unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [189] [Flat-band formation and chiral superconductivity in driven topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27355)
*Suman Jyoti De,Leo Goutte,Kartiek Agarwal,T. Pereg-Barnea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示利用圆偏振光对三维拓扑绝缘体表面态进行弗洛凯工程调控，可实现近扁平或墨西哥帽型电子能带，并在纯排斥库仑相互作用下支持拓扑超导，Tc可达约7 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在三维拓扑绝缘体表面通过弗洛凯驱动实现平坦能带，从而在纯排斥相互作用机制下诱导拓扑超导的可能性，类比于菱形石墨烯中位移场调控超导的近期进展。

Method: 利用圆偏振光对三维拓扑绝缘体表面狄拉克锥进行弗洛凯工程：驱动不仅打开能隙，还可减弱甚至反转狄拉克点之外的表面态色散内禀曲率；结合适当放置的屏蔽金属栅极，抑制维格纳结晶化。

Result: 在合理电场强度下可达平带极限；通过调节驱动场强度获得的能带在能量和空间轮廓上与菱形石墨烯位移场调控结果相似；在电子密度10¹¹–10¹²/cm²范围内可实现Tc~7 K，并避免维格纳结晶。

Conclusion: 弗洛凯驱动的三维拓扑绝缘体表面是实现纯排斥相互作用机制下拓扑超导的可行平台，为无需声子媒介的超导电性提供了新的实验可实现路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that circularly polarized light can be used to Floquet-engineer nearly flat or Mexican-hat like electronic bands on the surface of three-dimensional topological insulators (3D TIs), which under suitable conditions, can support topological superconductivity via purely repulsive Coulomb interactions. The driving acts not merely by gapping out the Dirac cone on the surface of the 3D TI, but can be used to diminish, and even flip in sign, the intrinsic curvature of the surface state dispersion away from the Dirac point. Using parameters for canonical 3D TIs, we find that the flat band limit is attained for reasonable electric fields and the bands realized by changing the strength of the driving field have a similar energetic and spatial profile to those obtained in rhombohedral graphene under varying displacement field, where the case for superconductivity with purely repulsive interactions has recently been made. We find that, with the aid of appropriately placed screening metallic gate, one can obtain $T_c \sim 7 $K in this setup while avoiding Wigner crystallization for low electron densities in the range of $10^{11}-10^{12}/\text{cm}^2$.

</details>


### [190] [Excited state optimization for strongly correlated quantum defects using ensemble variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27377)
*Kevin G. Kleiner,Lucas K. Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文利用系综变分蒙特卡洛方法，系统研究了金刚石和氮化铝中多种点缺陷的激发态性质，发现波函数各参数（轨道、行列式展开系数、Jastrow因子）的全优化对准确描述强关联缺陷至关重要，不同缺陷的主导参数各不相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联点缺陷体系（如金刚石中的NV、SiV中心和AlN中的Fe、Cr杂质）的激发态能量准确计算极具挑战性。传统DFT方法依赖近似泛函，而现有量子蒙特卡洛方法往往未对波函数所有参数进行全面优化。本文旨在探究全参数优化对激发态能量预测的影响。

Method: 采用系综变分蒙特卡洛（ensemble VMC）方法，系统比较不同层次波函数优化方案：①对比杂化泛函PBE0与半局域PBE轨道对目标泛函的影响；②直接优化目标泛函（包括行列式展开参数、轨道和Jastrow关联因子）；③逐缺陷分析各参数类型对激发能的贡献差异。

Result: PBE0杂化泛函轨道相比PBE半局域轨道显著降低目标泛函，激发能变化最高达0.5 eV；直接优化目标泛函可进一步改善，激发能变化最高达0.2 eV。最关键参数因缺陷而异，优化所有参数对获得准确激发态至关重要。

Conclusion: 对于强关联点缺陷的精确激发态计算，仅依赖DFT轨道是不够的，必须对波函数中行列式展开、轨道和Jastrow因子等全部参数进行系统优化，不同缺陷体系的主导优化参数不同，不存在统一的简化方案。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced ensemble variational Monte Carlo (VMC), we study optimized wave functions for strongly correlated point defects, including nitrogen-vacancy and silicon-vacancy centers in diamond and substitutional iron and chromium impurities in aluminum nitride. We study the effects of fully optimized determinant expansion parameters, orbitals, and Jastrow correlation factors on these systems. We find that orbitals from the hybrid functional PBE0 are much better (have lower objective functional) than semilocal PBE, which results in changes in the excitation energies up to 0.5 eV. Further improvements can be made by directly optimizing the objective functional, resulting in changes in excitation energies up to 0.2 eV. The most important parameter varies from defect to defect, reinforcing the necessity of optimizing all parameters to obtain accurate excited states in strongly correlated defects.

</details>


### [191] [Universality of Energy-Space Entanglement in Quantum Impurity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27394)
*Geng-Dong Zhou,Zhi-Da Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子杂质模型中能量空间二分下的纠缠熵具有普适行为：在费米液体固定点上，低能纠缠熵收敛到与模型参数无关的常数（ln2的整数倍加修正），该行为可由一维拓扑能带理论分类；在非费米液体临界点则呈现不稳定平台，并与过屏蔽双通道Kondo模型属于同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠熵研究多基于实空间二分，作者旨在探索能量空间（等价于浴的动量空间）二分是否能在量子杂质模型中展现出普适行为，并利用拓扑能带理论对费米液体固定点进行分类。

Method: 受poor man's scaling启发，对浴进行对数离散化并将其分为高能和低能扇区；在费米液体固定点（Anderson模型、完全屏蔽与欠屏蔽Kondo模型）上计算低能纠缠熵的流向；借助低能手征对称性，将能量空间中标度不变的固定点波函数映射到一维链的有效平移不变性，进而用一维拓扑能带理论进行分类；以双轨道Anderson模型中轨道间反铁磁耦合驱动的局域单态-Kondo单态相变作为验证实例。

Result: 费米液体固定点的低能纠缠熵流向与模型参数无关的常数，为ln2的整数倍加仅依赖于对数离散化参数Λ的修正；每个ln2贡献源自低能手征对称性下的拓扑边缘模；局域单态相纠缠熵近乎为零，Kondo单态相纠缠熵大于ln2（每自旋每轨道）；在非费米液体临界点，纠缠熵出现不稳定平台，其Λ依赖与过屏蔽双通道Kondo模型一致。

Conclusion: 能量空间纠缠熵为量子杂质模型的固定点分类提供了新视角：费米液体固定点可按一维拓扑能带理论分类，低能纠缠熵中的ln2整数倍对应拓扑边缘模；非费米液体临界点的纠缠熵行为支持与过屏蔽双通道Kondo模型属于同一普适类的观点。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is commonly studied using real-space bipartitions. We show that, in quantum impurity models, an energy-space bipartition, equivalent to the momentum-space bipartition of the bath, can display universal behavior. Motivated by poor man's scaling, we logarithmically discretize the bath and partition it into high- and low-energy sectors. For models with Fermi-liquid fixed points, including the Anderson model and fully screened or underscreened Kondo models, the low-energy EE flows to constants independent of model parameters. These constants are integer multiples of $\ln 2$ plus corrections that depend only on the logarithmic discretization parameter $Λ$. We show that scale invariance of the fixed-point wavefunction in energy space maps to effective translation invariance along a one-dimensional chain, allowing the fixed points to be classified by one-dimensional topological band theory. With low-energy chiral symmetry, each $\ln 2$ contribution originates from a topological edge mode. We also study transitions between distinct Fermi-liquid fixed points using the local-singlet--Kondo-singlet transition in a two-orbital Anderson model driven by an inter-orbital antiferromagnetic coupling. The local-singlet phase has an effectively decoupled impurity and nearly vanishing EE, whereas the Kondo-singlet phase has finite EE larger than $\ln 2$ per spin and orbital. When chiral symmetry holds at low energies, this distinction corresponds to a topological transition of the effective bath chain. At the non-Fermi-liquid critical point, the EE develops an unstable plateau. Its $Λ$ dependence resembles that of the overscreened two-channel Kondo model, supporting universality within the same non-Fermi-liquid universality class.

</details>


### [192] [Iterative minimization in reduced density matrix functional theory for periodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27679)
*Kai Luo,Jingang Han,Peize Lin,Daye Zheng,Mohan Chen,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了约化密度矩阵泛函理论（RDMFT）在周期性固体中的平面波实现，采用谱投影梯度（SPG）和一阶显式隐式（EBI）映射优化自然轨道与占据数，验证了方法的可靠性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMFT作为超越Kohn-Sham DFT处理强关联体系的有力候选方法，其在周期性固体中的实用计算长期未能实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，为扩展体系构建基组无关的RDMFT框架并实现高效的平面波计算方案。

Method: 作者以基组无关形式表述扩展体系的RDMFT，在平面波框架下采用迭代最小化。非局域交换关联泛函通过自适应压缩交换（ACE）技术评估。自然占据数在N-可表示性约束下用SPG方法或EBI映射优化；自然轨道通过复Stiefel流形上的黎曼优化更新。基准测试涵盖Hartree-Fock、Müller和Power泛函。测试体系包括H₂、硅、钠、LiH及N₂分子。

Result: SPG方法能精确重现杂化泛函参考值，而EBI在占据数趋近0或1时停滞。使用Müller和Power泛函时，SPG比EBI获得更低能量且收敛更稳定。在分数电荷LiH、解离H₂和N₂、硅的状态方程等应用中，算法表现出可靠性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的RDMFT平面波实现（尤其结合SPG优化）为周期性固体提供了一种可靠且稳健的计算方案，显著推进了RDMFT在扩展体系中的实用化进程。

Abstract: Reduced density matrix functional theory (RDMFT) offers a route beyond Kohn-Sham density functional theory for strongly correlated systems, yet practical calculations for periodic solids are still out of reach. We formulate RDMFT for extended systems in a basis-independent way and present a planewave implementation using iterative minimization for periodic solids, evaluating nonlocal exchange-correlation functionals through the existing adaptive compressed exchange machinery. Natural occupations are optimized under N-representability constraints with a spectral projected gradient (SPG) method or an first-order explicit-by-implicit (EBI) map, while natural orbitals are updated by Riemannian optimization on the complex Stiefel manifolds. Benchmarks on typical systems of \ce{H2}, silicon, and sodium with the Hartree-Fock functional show that SPG reproduces converged hybrid references, whereas EBI can stall when occupations approach $0$ or $1$. With the power and Müller functionals, SPG yields lower energies and more stable convergence than EBI. Applications to fractionally charged \ce{LiH}, dissociating \ce{H2} and \ce{N2} molecules, and equation of state of silicon show that the algorithm presented in this implementation is reliable and robust.

</details>


### [193] [Trion Excitations in Twisted Bilayer Graphene: A Quantum Monte Carlo Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27745)
*Shibo Shan,Cheng Huang,Patrick Ledwith,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动量空间量子蒙特卡洛方法，在转角双层石墨烯的有限温正常态中揭示了一种名为"狄拉克三重子"（Dirac trion）的新型三粒子激发——由两个电子和一个空穴组成的无质量束缚态，其有效质量极轻，且性质可通过转角与层间跃迁调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转角双层石墨烯等强关联电子体系中，电荷载流子的本质（单粒子还是多粒子复合激发）是核心问题。已有研究多聚焦于基态，但对于有限温度下正常态中的多体激发性质知之甚少，亟需用无偏的量子多体方法揭示是否存在超越朗道能级图像的新型集体激发。

Method: 采用连续场动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（Continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）方法，在投影的关联平带模型中，对转角双层石墨烯进行有限温度下的无偏量子多体计算。

Result: 基态为对称破缺绝缘体，具有约20 meV能隙的类电子激发；但当温度升至约3 meV（远低于相互作用能标）时，系统进入强涨落的对称正常态。该正常态中存在无能隙的狄拉克三重子激发——由两个电子和一个空穴构成的三粒子束缚态，在零动量无能隙点与高能电子严格正交。这些三重子虽由重组分构成，却呈现出任意轻的有效质量，其光谱可通过改变转角和层间跃迁强度灵活调控。

Conclusion: 该研究在平带强关联体系中首次揭示狄拉克三重子的存在，为探索拓扑关联能带中超越朗道能级的多体激发开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Determining the nature of charge carriers is a fundamental goal in the study of strongly correlated electron systems. Here, we employ the continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo method to reveal exotic "Dirac trion" excitations in the finite-temperature normal state of twisted bilayer graphene. While the ground state is a symmetry-breaking insulator with gapped ($\sim$ 20 meV) electron-like excitations, we show that a small temperature ($\sim$ 3 meV), well below the interaction scale, drives the system into a strongly fluctuating symmetric normal state. We demonstrate that this normal state hosts gapless excitations consisting of three-particle bound states, two electrons and one hole, that are exactly orthogonal to the higher-energy electrons at the zero-momentum gapless point. These Dirac trions have the remarkable property of being arbitrarily light despite being composed of heavy constituents, and their spectra can be easily tuned by varying the twist angle and interlayer hopping strength. Our unbiased quantum many-body computation sheds light on the Dirac trions in a projected correlated flat-band setting and opens the door for further investigation of many-body excitations in strongly correlated topological bands beyond Landau levels.

</details>


### [194] [Finite-size effects and interaction-driven crossovers in quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model: Exact diagonalization, DMRG and machine-learning analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27916)
*Md Fahad Equbal,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在有限宽度圆柱晶格上，利用精确对角化、DMRG和无监督机器学习方法研究四分之一填充吸引Hubbard模型，发现了一个从弱关联费米子到紧束缚单重态配对主导区的连续交叉。PCA和UMAP两种机器学习方法独立识别出与传统观测量一致的交叉区域，且有限尺寸标度分析表明这些特征随系统尺寸增大保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四分之一填充吸引Hubbard模型中从弱关联费米子态到强配对态的连续交叉行为，旨在：(1) 揭示动力学驱动费米子巡游性与相互作用驱动在位配对形成之间的竞争机制；(2) 利用无监督机器学习方法提供一种不依赖序参量的、无偏的多体关联表征手段。

Method: 在有限宽度圆柱晶格上，综合运用精确对角化(ED)、密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)以及两种无监督机器学习技术——主成分分析(PCA)和均匀流形近似与投影(UMAP)。通过基态能量学、局域观测量、关联函数分析交叉行为；通过空穴结合能计算提供配对形成的直接能量证据；将PCA和UMAP应用于实空间关联矩阵，进行无偏的相关景观表征；并通过配对结构因子和主导PCA方差比进行有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 发现了一个由相互作用驱动的连续交叉：从弱关联费米子到紧束缚单重态配对主导区，与热力学极限下的BCS-BEC渡越行为一致。双空穴结合能在整个吸引区保持为负，而三空穴结合能仅在足够强的吸引下才出现且表现出显著的有限尺寸依赖性。PCA揭示了关联方差的系统重分布，UMAP识别出弱配对与强配对区之间的清晰分离，两者独立确定了同一交叉区域。有限尺寸标度分析证实这些特征随系统尺寸增大保持稳健。

Conclusion: 四分之一填充吸引Hubbard模型存在一个连续的BCS-BEC交叉，该交叉源于巡游性与配对形成的竞争。无监督机器学习方法（PCA和UMAP）能够在不依赖序参量的情况下有效表征这一交叉，且其识别结果与传统观测量高度一致，为强关联系统中多体关联重组提供了一种无偏的诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate the quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model on finite-width cylindrical lattices using exact diagonalization (ED), density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and unsupervised machine-learning-based techniques. Analysis of the ground-state energetics, local observables and correlation functions reveals a continuous interaction-driven crossover from weakly correlated fermions to a regime dominated by tightly bound singlet pairs. This crossover originates from the competition between kinetic-energy-driven fermionic itinerancy and interaction-driven onsite pair formation and exhibits behavior consistent with the BCS--BEC crossover in the thermodynamic limit. Hole-binding-energy calculations provide direct energetic evidence for pair formation: the two-hole binding energy remains negative throughout the attractive regime whereas three-hole binding emerges only at sufficiently strong attraction and exhibits pronounced finite-size dependence. To obtain an unbiased characterization of the correlation landscape, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) and uniform manifold approximation and projection (UMAP) to the real-space correlation matrices. PCA reveals a systematic redistribution of correlation variance whereas UMAP identifies a clear separation between weak- and strong-pairing regimes. Both machine-learning-based approaches independently identify the same crossover region inferred from conventional observables while providing an order-parameter-independent characterization of the underlying reorganization of many-body correlations. Finite-size scaling analyses of the pairing structure factor and the leading PCA variance ratio demonstrate that these signatures remain robust with increasing system size.

</details>


### [195] [Orbital-Selective Mott Transition and Correlation-Amplified Charge Ordering in the Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28029)
*Xiuhua Chen,Yilin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过DFT+DMFT计算，揭示了交变磁体CsCr₂S₂O中Verwey型金属-绝缘体转变的微观机制：S位配体畸变触发微小电荷不对称，随后被动态电子关联显著放大，导致Cr位点间d_yz轨道的轨道选择性Mott转变，最终驱动MIT；而Te替代S因电子关联较弱无法诱发MIT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CsCr₂S₂O中Verwey型MIT的驱动力尚不明确——实验观测到原子畸变仅发生在配体位而非Cr位，却导致Cr位点间显著的电荷不平衡，其物理根源未被揭示。

Method: 采用DFT+DMFT（密度泛函理论+动力学平均场理论）计算，分析电子结构、轨道占据、电荷分布及自旋极化，并对比S与Te配体替代的效应。

Result: S位畸变通过Cr-d_yz与S-p轨道杂化触发初始微小电荷不对称；动态电子关联将此不对称性显著放大，导致不同Cr位点间d_yz轨道在电荷占据和电子关联强度上出现巨大差异，进而引发局域自旋极化显著分化，最终驱动轨道选择性Mott转变和MIT。Te替代S因电子关联较弱，无法实现该关联放大效应，MIT不发生。

Conclusion: 多体效应可剧烈放大配体不稳定性，重塑交变磁体的电子结构；配体工程对实现稳健的金属性交变磁性至关重要。

Abstract: Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O undergoes a Verwey-type metal-to-insulator transition (MIT) driven by lattice distortion and a stripe charge order on the Cr sublattice, reminiscent of the physics in Fe$_3$O$_4$. However, atomic distortions occur exclusively at the ligand sites rather than the Cr sites. What drives such a pronounced charge imbalance between Cr sites thus remains a mystery. Utilizing DFT+DMFT calculations, we identify an orbital-selective Mott transition, which leaves the correlated metallic $d_{yz}$ orbital governing the low-energy physics. We demonstrate that S-site distortions trigger an initial, tiny charge asymmetry between Cr sites via Cr-$d_{yz}$ and S-$p$ orbital hybridization. Crucially, this asymmetry is significantly amplified by dynamical electronic correlations, resulting in a large discrepancy in both the charge and electron correlations of the Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbital between distinct Cr sites. This further induces a substantial differentiation in local spin polarizations in the altermagnetic state, ultimately driving the MIT. In contrast, we predict that replacing S with Te weakens this correlation-amplification effect and fails to induce an MIT due to weaker electron correlations. Our findings demonstrate that many-body effects can drastically amplify ligand instabilities to reshape the electronic structure of altermagnets, highlighting that ligand engineering is of paramount importance for realizing robust metallic altermagnetism.

</details>


### [196] [Thermal spin transport in easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnets controlled by magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28085)
*Yuliia I. Gusieva,Kostiantyn V. Yershov,Jeroen van den Brink,Volodymyr P. Kravchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了易面型d波交变磁体（如NiF₂）中，由交变磁性产生的动量依赖的d波对称磁振子磁矩，并证明该磁矩导致自旋劈裂效应——即温度梯度驱动下产生纯自旋流，且该热自旋电导率可由垂直易面的外磁场有效调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明易轴型d波交变磁体中存在热磁振子劈裂效应，但易面型系统中磁振子分支不携带恒定磁矩，该效应是否存在尚不明确。作者旨在探索易面型金红石结构d波交变磁体中是否存在类似的自旋输运现象。

Method: 作者以金红石型NiF₂为代表，理论分析易面型d波交变磁体中的磁振子能谱与磁矩结构，推导交变磁生成的动量依赖磁振子磁矩（具有d波对称性），进而计算温度梯度驱动下的磁振子自旋流及热自旋电导率，并考察外磁场对输运性质的调控。

Result: 揭示了易面型d波交变磁体中存在交变磁生成的磁振子磁矩，该磁矩具有动量依赖性和d波对称性；证实了自旋劈裂效应的存在，即温度梯度可驱动纯磁振子自旋流；证明垂直易平面的外磁场可有效调控热自旋电导率。

Conclusion: 易面型d波交变磁体中同样存在由交变磁性导致的非常规自旋输运现象，外磁场提供了有效的调控手段，拓展了交变磁体在自旋热电子学中的应用前景。

Abstract: Altermagnets constitute a novel class of collinear spin-compensated materials in which magnon branches are spin-split even in the non-relativistic limit. The latter is the result of a more complex symmetry operation (compared to conventional antiferromagnets) that connects the two sublattices. Altermagnetic splitting strongly affects the magnon transport properties, leading, in particular, to the thermal magnon splitter effect in easy-axial $d$-wave altermagnets. Whether this effect also appears in easy-planar systems is not obvious, since the magnon branches do not carry a constant magnetic moment in this case. Here, we consider the easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnet of a rutile-type, e.g., NiF$_2$, and demonstrate the emergence of the altermagnetically generated magnon magnetic moment, which is momentum-dependent and possesses $d$-wave symmetry. This then leads to the spin-splitter effect, i.e., the emergence of a magnon-driven spin current (the flow of magnetic moment) in response to the applied temperature gradient. We also demonstrate that the corresponding thermal spin conductivity can be effectively tuned by an external magnetic field perpendicular to the easy plane.

</details>


### [197] [Coupled Spin-Density-Wave and Bond-Order Driven Metal-Insulator Transition in Altermagnetic CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28329)
*Chenchao Xu,Wansheng Bai,Guo-Xiang Zhi,Yi Liu,Xiaoqun Wang,Jianhui Dai,Chao Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在预存C型反铁磁序破坏时间反演对称性的条件下，CsCr₂S₂O中轨道选择性的键序与次级自旋密度波耦合，协同驱动了金属-绝缘体相变，解释了实验观测到的结构畸变、电荷歧化及能隙打开等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释CsCr₂S₂O中金属-绝缘体相变的微观机制，特别是实验上同时观测到的结构畸变、电荷歧化和局域磁矩调制等现象的起源，以及预存反铁磁序在其中的作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合轨道分辨的电子结构分析，揭示Cr-d_{yz} 和 Cr-d_{xz} 轨道的分化行为；进一步通过在位相互作用分析次级自旋密度波不稳定性及其与键序的耦合。

Result: 发现Cr-d_{yz} 轨道形成局域磁矩并建立交变磁序（altermagnetism），而Cr-d_{xz} 轨道保持金属性并与S-p_z 杂化，主导低能物理。在位相互作用增强了巡游d_{xz} 电子的次级SDW不稳定性，该不稳定性与Cr-d_{xz}-S-p_z 键序耦合，形成的耦合sSDW-BO同时产生了结构畸变、电荷歧化、局域Cr磁矩调制和能隙打开。

Conclusion: 确立了一种轨道选择机制：预存交变磁性与电子关联协同作用，通过次级SDW与键序的耦合驱动结构型金属-绝缘体相变。

Abstract: A metal-insulator transition (MIT) driven by bond order (BO) coupled with a secondary spin-density wave (SDW) is identified in CsCr$_2$S$_2$O. Such coupling is enabled as a result of the broken time-reversal symmetry due to the pre-existing C-type antiferromagnetic (C-AFM) order. First-principles calculations reveal an orbital-selective physics that Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbitals form local moments and establish the altermagnetic order, while the Cr-$d_{xz}$ orbitals remain metallic and hybridize with S-$p_z$. Thus the low-energy physics is governed by the Cr-$d_{xz}$ and S-$p_z$ orbitals. On-site interactions then enhance a secondary SDW ($s$SDW) instability of the itinerant $d_{xz}$ electrons, which couples to the Cr-$d_{xz}$-S-$p_z$ bonding order. The resulting coupled $s$SDW-BO simultaneously produces experimentally observed structural distortion, charge disproportionation, local Cr-moment modulation, and gap opening. Our results establish an orbital-selective mechanism upon which pre-existing altermagnetism and electronic correlations cooperate to drive a structural MIT.

</details>


### [198] [Field-Selected Topological Buffering in a Disordered Skyrmion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28458)
*Wenyu Su,Nvsen Ma,Chen Cheng,Hong-Gang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟研究了具有随机Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的三角晶格手性磁体中的斯格明子晶体，揭示了磁场如何在两种无序化路径之间进行选择：高场下平移序、键取向序和拓扑电荷依次在不同无序强度下丧失，形成一个"拓扑缓冲区"；低场下键取向无序化与拓扑重构几乎同时发生，缓冲区收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火无序可以破坏晶体序但未必立即破坏拓扑结构，然而斯格明子晶体中这两个过程之间的关系尚不清楚。本研究旨在揭示无序诱导的晶体序丧失与拓扑电荷丧失之间的分离机制及其调控手段。

Method: 对具有随机DM相互作用的三角晶格手性磁体进行大规模数值模拟，结合有限尺寸标度、空间关联函数、缺陷统计和自旋自关联函数等方法，分析不同磁场强度下系统的无序化行为。

Result: 高场下发现三个特征无序尺度：平移关联先丧失（弱无序），六方键取向序在更大无序强度下存活，拓扑电荷在更大尺度仍几乎锁定，形成包含类Bragg玻璃斯格明子态和斯格明子玻璃态的"拓扑缓冲区"。低场下键取向无序化与拓扑重构几乎重合，斯格明子玻璃窗口消失。

Conclusion: 磁场可作为分离晶体无序化与拓扑电荷丧失的调控旋钮；"拓扑缓冲"是一种使拓扑结构在结构无序介质中保持鲁棒性的机制。

Abstract: Quenched disorder can disrupt crystalline order without immediately destroying the topology of its constituent textures, but the relation between these processes in skyrmion crystals remains unclear. Using large-scale simulations of a triangular-lattice chiral magnet with random DM interactions, we show that the magnetic field selects between two disordering routes. At high fields, global translational coherence is lost at a weak-disorder scale, while sixfold bond-orientational order survives to a larger disorder strength and the total topological charge remains nearly locked up to a substantially larger scale. The resulting interval defines a topological buffer containing a Bragg-glass- like skyrmion regime followed by a skyrmion-glass regime. Finite-size scaling, spatial correlations, defect statistics, and spin autocorrelations support their distinct structural and glassy character. At lower fields, bond-orientational disordering nearly coincides with topological reconstruction, eliminating the skyrmion-glass window and contracting the buffer. These results identify the magnetic field as a control knob for separating crystalline disordering from topological-charge loss and establish topological buffering as a mechanism by which topological textures can remain robust in structurally disordered media.

</details>


### [199] [From Exact Diagonalization to DMRG: A Complete Numerical Study of the Transverse-Field Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28471)
*Chandra Sekhar Prayaga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对一维横场伊辛模型(TFIM)进行了系统的数值研究，通过精确对角化(小尺寸 L=8,14,20)和密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG，最大 L=100)追踪其基态纠缠结构。采用统一的方法论(相同场网格、双键维度 χ=100/200 收敛诊断)，明确展示了精确对角化与DMRG的精确一致性区域。研究中发现并解决了一个键维度收敛伪影，该伪影在早期数据集中曾造成虚假的不连续性，作为方法论的警示案例。利用修正后的数据集，通过两种互补途径——中间链熵的对数有限尺寸标度和全尺寸Calabrese-Cardy公式拟合——提取出有效中心荷 c_eff ≈ 0.51-0.52，与二维Ising模型的精确值 c=1/2 一致。本文既是物理结果，也是谨慎有限尺寸数值方法的工作范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在：1) 通过统一、自洽的数值框架，系统研究一维TFIM的基态纠缠结构，填补此前研究中方法不一致导致的系统性误差；2) 明确精确对角化与DMRG之间的精确对应关系，而非仅近似一致；3) 识别并纠正早期数据集中因键维度收敛不足导致的虚假不连续性，为从业者提供警示案例；4) 通过有限尺寸标度提取中心荷，验证量子-经典对应(Suzuki-Trotter)及Widom-Kadanoff标度假说在量子问题中的适用性。

Method: 1) 精确对角化：对 L=8,14,20 的小尺寸系统进行精确对角化，获得基态纠缠熵的基准数据。2) DMRG：对 L 最大至 100 的系统进行 DMRG 计算。3) 统一方法框架：所有七个系统尺寸使用相同的横场网格、相同的收敛诊断标准——每个点独立运行键维度 χ=100 和 χ=200 的计算，通过比较两者结果判断收敛性。4) 中心荷提取：采用两条互补路径——(a) 中间链纠缠熵的对数有限尺寸标度，(b) 对每个系统尺寸的每个键同时应用完整的 Calabrese-Cardy 公式，系统排除短程格点修正后获得 c_eff。5) 理论关联：回顾 Suzuki-Trotter 量子-经典对应，讨论 Widom-Kadanoff 标度假说从经典到量子问题的移植。

Result: 1) 精确对角化与DMRG在重叠区域精确一致，而非仅近似匹配。2) 发现早期数据集中因键维度不足导致的虚假不连续性，通过统一的双键维度诊断方法成功消除该伪影。3) 从修正后的数据集中提取的有效中心荷 c_eff 为 0.51-0.52，在系统排除短程格点修正后，与二维Ising普适类的精确值 c=1/2 高度一致。4) 验证了 Calabrese-Cardy 公式在有限尺寸系统中的适用性，以及量子-经典对应在纠缠标度行为中的体现。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一的、自洽的数值方法，可靠地提取了一维TFIM在临界点的中心荷 c=1/2，与二维经典Ising模型的共形场论预言一致，从而验证了Suzuki-Trotter量子-经典对应和Widom-Kadanoff标度假说。文章强调，谨慎的有限尺寸数值方法(统一的场网格、双键维度收敛诊断)对获得可靠结果至关重要。同时，文中对键维度收敛伪影的识别与纠正，为从事DMRG计算的从业者提供了实用的方法论警示。本文兼具物理结论与方法论示范的双重价值。

Abstract: We present a self-contained numerical study of the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), tracing its ground-state entanglement structure from exact diagonalization at small system size (L=8,14,20) through density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations up to L=100. A single, consistent methodology - identical field grid, identical convergence diagnostic (independent runs at bond dimensions chi=100 and chi=200 at every point) - is used across all seven system sizes, and we show explicitly where exact diagonalization and DMRG must agree exactly rather than merely approximately. Along the way we document and resolve a bond-dimension convergence artifact that produced a spurious discontinuity in an earlier, less systematic dataset, as a worked cautionary example for practitioners. Using the resolved dataset we extract the central charge of the transition via two complementary routes - the leading logarithmic finite-size scaling of the mid-chain entropy, and the full Calabrese-Cardy formula applied to every bond of every system size simultaneously - obtaining c_eff to 0.51-0.52 as short-distance lattice corrections are systematically excluded, consistent with the exact two-dimensional Ising value c=1/2. We review the quantum-classical (Suzuki-Trotter) correspondence that underlies this agreement and discuss how the Widom-Kadanoff scaling hypothesis transplants from the classical to the quantum problem. The manuscript is intended as both a physics result and a worked methodological example of careful finite-size numerics.

</details>


### [200] [Graph Neural Network Force Fields for Spin Dynamics in Metallic Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28537)
*Ali Rayat,Yunhao Fan,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的磁性力场框架，能够直接从电子结构计算中学习描述巡游电子自旋动力学的有效磁能泛函，从而高效评估自旋扭矩并精确复现非平衡自旋动力学，为跨尺度磁性模拟提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属磁体的自旋动力学受电子相互作用支配，传统预测模拟需要在时间演化过程中反复求解电子结构问题，计算成本极高，构成主要瓶颈。因此需要一种能够绕过反复电子求解、同时能捕捉巡游电子产生的非线性及空间扩展相互作用的替代方法。

Method: 作者引入图神经网络磁性力场（GNN magnetic force-field）框架，其概念类似于机器学习原子间势。该框架从电子结构计算数据中学习有效的磁能泛函，直接输出自旋扭矩，避免每次时间步都求解底层电子问题，同时保留巡游电子介导的非线性和非局域相互作用。

Result: 在具有共线、非共线和非共面磁序的代表性金属磁体系统上进行了基准测试。结果表明，学习到的力场能够精确复现电子结构计算产生的自旋扭矩，非平衡自旋动力学结果与直接电子模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 图神经网络是构建机器学习磁性力场的强大框架，为实现跨越多个长度和时间尺度的预测性非平衡磁性大规模模拟开辟了道路。

Abstract: Metallic magnets exhibit complex spin dynamics governed by electronically generated interactions. Predictive simulations of such dynamics typically require repeated solutions of an underlying electronic problem throughout the time evolution, creating a major computational bottleneck. Here we introduce a graph neural network (GNN) magnetic force-field framework that learns the effective magnetic energy functional governing itinerant spin dynamics directly from electronic calculations. Conceptually analogous to machine-learned interatomic potentials, the proposed framework enables efficient evaluation of spin torques while capturing the nonlinear and spatially extended interactions generated by itinerant electrons. We benchmark the method on representative metallic magnetic systems exhibiting collinear, noncollinear, and noncoplanar magnetic order. The learned force fields accurately reproduce electronically generated spin torques and yield nonequilibrium spin dynamics in excellent agreement with direct electronic simulations. Our results establish graph neural networks as a powerful framework for machine-learned magnetic force fields, providing a pathway toward predictive large-scale simulations of nonequilibrium magnetism across multiple length and time scales.

</details>


### [201] [Lattice composite Fermi liquid with broken inversion symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28613)
*Pavel A. Nosov,Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了破缺反演对称性的半填充陈能带中格点复合费米液体的输运性质，揭示了在传统朗道能级设置中被隐藏的奇异动力学响应，包括非解析纵向光电阻率、非互易输运、霍尔电导率的|q|依赖以及由2k_F散射增强的Umklapp弛豫导致的奇异DC电阻率温度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯中观测到了零场复合费米液体的实验证据，但格点系统中的复合费米液体与连续介质量子霍尔体系有本质区别。本文旨在揭示破缺反演对称性的格点复合费米液体中独特的输运特征，这些特征在传统朗道能级设置中不存在，可为正在进行的实验提供可直接验证的理论预言。

Method: 作者采用复合费米液体理论框架，在破缺反演对称性的半填充陈能带中分析规范场涨落对输运的效应。零波矢处计算了由库仑相互作用（门 screened）诱导的纵向光电阻率的频率依赖；有限波矢处分析了非互易输运和霍尔电导率的波矢依赖，并联系表面声波传播探测方案；此外还讨论了复合费米面上2k_F散射的重整化如何增强Umklapp弛豫并导致电阻率的非解析温度依赖。

Result: （1）零波矢下，反演对称性破缺使规范场涨落产生非解析纵向光电阻率，Re ρ^{xx}(ω) ∼ |ω|^{4/3}（门 screened 库仑相互作用）；（2）有限波矢q下，反演破缺导致非互易输运和霍尔电导率的∼|q|非解析依赖，可通过表面声波传播探测；（3）复合费米面上2k_F散射的重整化增强Umklapp弛豫，可导致电阻率的非解析温度依赖。这些特征在连续介质量子霍尔对应体系中不存在。

Conclusion: 格点复合费米液体中破缺的反演对称性暴露了复合费米子的内禀奇异动力学响应，产生了一系列可实验探测的输运特征。这些理论结果为扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯等体系中零场复合费米液体的实验研究提供了明确的可检验预言，有助于区分格点复合费米液体与传统量子霍尔态。

Abstract: We study transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids realized in half-filled Chern bands with broken inversion symmetry. We show that reduced crystalline symmetry exposes intrinsic singular dynamical responses of composite fermions that are otherwise hidden in the conventional Landau-level setting. At zero wave vector, inversion breaking allows gauge-field fluctuations to generate a non-analytic longitudinal optical resistivity, with $\operatorname{Re}ρ^{xx}(ω)\sim |ω|^{4/3}$ for gate-screened Coulomb interactions. At finite wave vector $\mathbf{q}$, inversion breaking leads to nonreciprocal transport and a non-analytic $\sim |\mathbf{q}|$ dependence of the Hall conductivity, both of which can be probed through surface acoustic wave propagation. We also discuss a distinct mechanism for singular DC transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids: renormalization of $2k_F$ scattering at the composite Fermi surface enhances Umklapp relaxation and can lead to a non-analytic temperature dependence of the resistivity. Taken together, our results identify transport signatures of lattice composite Fermi liquids that are absent in their continuum quantum Hall counterparts and can be directly tested in ongoing experiments on twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene, where evidence for zero-field composite Fermi liquids has recently been reported.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Design principles for energy dissipation in viscoelastic network metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27525)
*Niranjan Sarpangala,Sean Fancher,Prashant K. Purohit,Eleni Katifori*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图拉普拉斯算子的高效谱框架，用于粘弹性桁架网络的力学耗散分析，揭示了截面面积重分布对能量耗散的影响规律：随机重分布降低耗散，而梯度优化产生由材料本征衰减长度主导的非平凡构型，最优质量分布从驱动源以衰减长度尺度衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化材料中的机械能耗散对隔振和冲击防护至关重要，但在大规模无序桁架网络中寻找最优耗散构型时，传统有限元方法计算代价过高，亟需一种计算效率更高的分析框架。

Method: 开发了基于图拉普拉斯算子的谱框架，精确保留了每根杆的完整连续动力学，问题规模仅与节点数（而非单元离散点数）成比例。在此框架下，通过重分布网络内截面面积（不改变材料组分），结合随机重分布与梯度优化研究耗散行为，并引入本征衰减长度作为核心设计参数。

Result: （1）随机重分布截面面积通常比均匀基线降低耗散；（2）梯度优化产生非平凡构型，其形态由基体材料的本征衰减长度主导；（3）驱动频率接近全局共振模态时，最优质量分布从驱动节点以衰减长度尺度衰减；（4）在较小衰减长度下，最优构型与边界条件无关。

Conclusion: 衰减长度尺度的设计原理为复杂无序结构提供了新思路，所提谱框架为大规模探索此类结构提供了高效计算工具，未来可进一步推广至更复杂的无序构型研究。

Abstract: Mechanical energy dissipation in networked materials is relevant for applications from vibration isolation to impact protection, yet identifying optimal dissipative architectures in large disordered truss networks is computationally prohibitive with conventional finite element methods. We develop an efficient graph Laplacian-based spectral framework for viscoelastic truss networks, in which the full continuum dynamics of each rod are retained exactly and the problem size scales with the number of joints rather than element-level discretization points. Using this framework, we investigate how redistributing cross-sectional areas within a network (without changing material composition) controls energy dissipation. We find that random redistribution typically reduces dissipation relative to a uniform baseline, while gradient-based optimization yields nontrivial architectures whose form is governed by the intrinsic attenuation length of the base material. Focusing on driving frequencies near a global resonant mode of the network, we show that the optimal mass distribution decays from the source (driven joint) with the attenuation length scale, and at small attenuation lengths the optimal architecture is independent of the boundary conditions. These results motivate future studies of dissipation length scale based design principles on more complex disordered architectures and provide an efficient computational framework for exploring such structures at scale.

</details>


### [203] [Partial vision leads to an unexpected emergent collective behavior in active aligning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27819)
*Raúl Molina-Prados Lallena,José Martín-Roca,Chantal Valeriani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在经典Vicsek模型基础上引入双视角锥（不同张角α和前后朝向β），系统研究了有限感知视野和非互易相互作用对活性粒子集体运动相行为的影响，揭示了受限侧向视野破坏全局有序、前后不对称感知导致迥异的集群结构，并探讨了体积排斥效应的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vicsek模型假设粒子具有各向同性的相互作用场，但真实生物系统中广泛存在感知受限（如视野盲区）和前后不对称感知（如眼睛朝前/朝后）的现象。作者旨在揭示有限视野、空间各向异性感知及由此引入的非互易相互作用如何影响活性物质系统的非平衡相行为和涌现结构。

Method: 作者构建了广义Vicsek模型，引入两个互不重叠的视角锥，通过调节锥体张角α（控制侧向视野范围）和前后朝向参数β（打破前后对称性）系统研究非平衡相行为。进一步引入短程体积排斥效应，考察空间位阻对新兴集群结构的影响。

Result: 1. 限制侧向视野（减小α）会破坏全局有序，改变临界噪声阈值，并诱导高密度行进带；2. 前向偏置视野通过"跟随领头者"对齐机制驱动强聚集；3. 后向偏置视野则稳定出奇均匀的集群态，密度涨落被显著抑制；4. 短程体积排斥的引入表明，这些新型致密集团相的结构完整性对位阻相互作用高度敏感。

Conclusion: 非互易感知、空间各向异性与物理约束（体积排斥）之间的协同作用深刻塑造了活性物质系统的集体动力学和涌现结构，为理解生物集群中有限感知与不对称信息传递的功能意义提供了新见解。

Abstract: The Vicsek Model represents a paradigmatic framework for understanding the collective motion of active aligning particles, traditionally assuming isotropic interaction fields. Inspired by biological systems characterized by limited perception and blind spots, we propose a generalized Vicsek model featuring two distinct, non-overlapping angular vision cones. We systematically investigate the non-equilibrium phase behavior of this system by tuning the aperture area (α) and the front-back orientation (\b{eta}) of the cones. Our results reveal that restricting the lateral vision area destabilizes global order, shifts the critical noise, and induces highly dense traveling bands. Furthermore, breaking the front-back symmetry introduces non-reciprocal interactions that profoundly alter the emergent spatial structures: forward-biased vision drives strong clustering through "follow the leader" alignment, whereas backward-biased alignment stabilizes an exceptionally homogeneous flocking state with suppressed density fluctuations. Finally, we incorporate short-range volume exclusion, demonstrating that the structural integrity of these novel tightly-clustered phases is highly sensitive to steric interactions. Our work provides new insights into the interplay between non-reciprocal perception, spatial anisotropy, and physical constraints in active matter.

</details>


### [204] [Phase transitions and microphases in elastomers. I. Emergence of stable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27516)
*Manu Mannattil,Haim Diamant,David Andelman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非局域热力学-弹性耦合理论，利用体积守恒解释溶胀弹性体中的微相分离现象，成功再现了相变温度和畴尺寸随弹性体刚度变化的实验规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现弹性效应可调控溶胀弹性体中的微相分离，但其物理机制尚不清晰——微相分离源于弹性相互作用与热力学相互作用的特征长度尺度不匹配，需要从经典弹性理论出发建立统一的理论框架来解释这一现象。

Method: 基于经典弹性理论，引入由体积守恒导出的非局域热力学-弹性耦合机制，建立理论模型描述各向同性溶胀弹性体中的微相分离行为。

Result: 该理论成功再现了各向同性溶胀弹性体中相变温度和畴域尺寸对弹性体刚度的依赖关系，与已有实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 溶胀弹性体中的微相分离可通过经典弹性理论结合体积守恒导致的非局域耦合加以解释；本文为第一部分，后续工作将探讨各向异性溶胀和非均匀弹性模量的影响。

Abstract: Elasticity often plays a key role in regulating phase separation in physical systems. Recent experiments have shown that elastic effects can be used to control microphase separation in swollen elastomers. Here, microphase separation arises from a mismatch between the characteristic length scales of elastic and thermodynamic interactions. In this first part of a two-part paper, we show that microphase formation in elastomers can be explained using conventional theories of elasticity through a nonlocal thermodynamic-elastic coupling arising from volume conservation. Our theory reproduces the observed dependence of phase transition temperature and domain size on elastomer stiffness in isotropically swollen elastomers. In the companion paper, we investigate the effects of anisotropic swelling and inhomogeneous elastic moduli.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在跨群体/时间/条件数据池化时，基于案例决策理论（CBDT）的组合公理是否得以保持。作者聚焦于带OLS输出头的固定表示神经网络，证明池化重拟合会改变逆Gram几何从而可能逆转共享偏好，并提出Gram失配度量、几何导向正则化及三阶段审计框架来检测和缓解偏好逆转问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络越来越多地通过池化跨群体、跨时段、跨运行条件的数据来提升泛化能力，但这引发了一个可靠性问题：在池化数据上重训练的模型是否仍能保持各数据源共同支持的行动排序？CBDT的组合公理要求源支持的偏好在其并集上仍然成立，而当前缺乏对固定表示神经网络在该公理下何时成立的系统研究。

Method: 作者首先从理论上分析了带OLS输出头的固定表示神经网络在池化重拟合时的机制，揭示逆Gram几何的重计算是导致共享偏好逆转的根源，并推导了精确和近似的保持条件。随后引入：(1) 尺度不变的Gram失配度量，用于筛选候选池化数据集；(2) 几何导向的正则化方法，在训练中塑造源几何结构；(3) 三阶段审计框架，追踪从严格成对偏好逆转到决策变化再到任务定义效用损失的完整链条。实验覆盖电力负荷竞价代理、医疗和金融决策代理三个领域。

Result: 实验揭示了稳定和易逆转的池化区间：负荷审计在代理效用下识别出可度量的非零类别的源共识相对有害决策；跨领域审计表明，同等的Gram失配可对应截然不同的偏好保持率。几何导向目标在描述准确性、一致性、几何结构和危害程度之间占据不同的运行点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛选、解析认证、几何导向训练和决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可度量和可操作。几何导向正则化与Gram失配度量为安全的数据池化实践提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [206] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了鲁棒优化随时间变化（ROOT）中的生存时间问题，在等向高斯环境动态下，针对固定部署解推导了期望生存时间的严格下界和可计算的多步上界。理论表明期望生存时间在慢变环境中按 Θ(σ⁻²) 缩放，在高维时退化为仅一个未来变化周期，并通过蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROOT领域虽然发展迅速，但大多数贡献停留在算法和实证层面，对生存时间等基本性质缺乏理论理解。生存时间虽被广泛用作时间鲁棒性度量，但其期望值如何依赖于环境动态、部署质量和问题特性仍未得到理论刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将生存时间建模为离散首次逃逸问题（discrete first-exit problem），在等向高斯环境动态假设下，针对固定部署解推导期望生存时间的严格理论下界和可计算的多步上界，通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论预测并分析对建模假设和参数不确定性的敏感性。

Result: 理论推导表明期望生存时间在慢变环境中按 Θ(σ⁻²) 缩放，在高维环境中趋近最小值（仅一个未来变化周期）。蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测，并展示了上下界如何支持优化后的部署决策。

Conclusion: 该框架为部署生命周期提供了分析性刻画，能够识别所需部署期限在何时可以被保证、被排除或仍处于分析未决状态，填补了ROOT领域在理论分析方面的空白。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [207] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文给出了等渗回归在二值样本上最坏情况下自由度的精确有限样本刻画，利用解析数论将自由度上界的主项改进为 $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$，并首次推导出等渗回归的期望校准误差（ECE）的非平凡分布无关保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等渗回归广泛用于单调函数估计与概率预测器校准，但其自由度（模型复杂度）在有限样本下的精确刻画尚不完善，且缺乏分布无关的校准误差理论保证。

Method: 1. 对二值序列进行组合分析，识别使等渗回归产生最多不同拟合值的极端序列；2. 运用解析数论工具推导自由度上界的精确主项；3. 基于确定性自由度界，将结果应用于校准场景，推导期望校准误差（ECE）的分布无关上界。

Result: 1. 得到自由度上界主项为 $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$，优于既有界；2. 首次给出等渗回归的ECE分布无关保证，仅需假设 $Y \in \{0,1\}$。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上精确量化了等渗回归在二值数据上的模型复杂度，并填补了其校准性能分布无关理论保证的空白，为等渗回归的实际应用提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [208] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 HOMER（Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation），一种通过径向 Huber 中心聚合分组均值的稳健估计方法。该方法在有限二阶矩下具备 MOM 量级的偏差界，在重尾数据中保持稳定，同时在干净高斯数据上逼近样本均值的效率。伪 HOMER 支持渐近线性与一致的 sandwich 协方差估计，在有限三阶矩下可实现参数速率的均值推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布下，经验均值的置信控制失效；几何中位数-of-means（MOM）也缺乏向均值效率趋近的调节阈值。需要一种能在稳健性与效率之间平滑插值、且支持推断的估计方法。

Method: 提出 HOMER：将数据分块，计算块内均值，再以径向 Huber 中心（含经典与伪 Huber 两种形式）聚合各块得分。经典 HOMER 在二次区域内恢复样本均值；伪 HOMER 随阈值增大趋近样本均值，并具备渐近线性。理论方面，建立了 Hilbert 空间多数定理与有限二阶矩下的 MOM 阶偏差界；在有限三阶矩下，固定维投影支持参数速率的均值推断（要求块大小增速快于块数）。

Result: 重尾模拟中，少数块摘要被污染时 HOMER 保持稳定；干净高斯数据下两种版本均逼近经验均值效率。有限块 sandwich 区间存在覆盖不足问题，尤其在偏态函数数据中。当污染影响多数块或破坏普通块内均值时方法失效。

Conclusion: HOMER 在稳健性与效率之间取得了灵活平衡，伪 HOMER 更为实用，支持渐近推断。方法在适度污染下表现良好，但在广泛污染或块内均值被破坏时存在局限。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [209] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于组合贝叶斯优化的波长选择方法，将糖度估计中的波长区域选择建模为二元黑箱优化问题，通过稀疏二次代理模型和汤普森采样进行序贯提取，并利用模拟/量子退火求解采集函数的最小化问题。实验表明该方法在PLS回归预测精度和波长区域一致性上优于遗传算法和模拟退火方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析中，波长选择对提高预测精度和可解释性至关重要，但现有方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）存在过拟合和选择结果不一致的问题。研究者希望借助贝叶斯优化的全局搜索能力和对误差景观的平滑探索，实现更鲁棒的特征选择。

Method: 将波长区域选择形式化为二元黑箱优化问题，采用组合贝叶斯优化框架：构建稀疏二次代理模型，通过汤普森采样（Thompson sampling）序贯提取感兴趣的波长区域，将采集函数最小化转化为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，分别使用模拟退火和量子退火求解。

Result: 所提方法在偏最小二乘回归（PLS）中提升了预测精度，选出的波长区域比遗传算法和模拟退火方法更具一致性。在一位局部扰动下，验证集RMSE波动极小，表明方法收敛到更平滑的误差景观，避免了孤立的过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化是光谱预测任务中实现鲁棒特征选择的有效框架，其局部稳定性表明该方法能避开尖锐的局部最优，具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [210] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于深度神经网络实现的Engression方法进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为逼近误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression作为一种通过能量得分拟合生成模型Y=f(X,ε)来学习条件分布的新方法，其理论基础尚不完善。本文旨在填补Engression在深度神经网络实现下的理论空白，为其提供严格的误差分析和收敛性保证。

Method: 作者将Engression的超额风险分解为三个正交分量：逼近误差（衡量深度神经网络对真实条件生成器的逼近能力）、随机误差（源于有限样本带来的统计波动）和蒙特卡洛误差（由噪声变量ε的有限采样引入）。基于此分解框架，在目标条件生成器满足组合光滑性结构的假设下，推导出各分量的收敛速率并综合得到总体收敛速率。

Result: 在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下，成功建立了Engression的收敛速率，从理论上量化了逼近误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差对整体性能的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文为基于深度神经网络的Engression提供了首个系统的理论误差分析，证明了该方法在组合光滑条件下具有良好的收敛性质，为其实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [211] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对多物品用户场景下的本地差分隐私（LDP）投毒攻击防御问题，提出随机投影裁剪（RPC）方法。通过随机二值向量投影和裁剪限制攻击者能力，并利用精确的偏差校正消除偏差-方差权衡，从而可以使用更小的裁剪阈值来缩小输出空间、增强鲁棒性。理论上给出了所有可能攻击下的估计误差保证，实验表明该方法在可信环境下性能相当或更优，在不可信环境下显著更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP投毒攻击防御策略主要针对单物品用户，但实际中用户常持有多个物品。多物品用户场景下输出空间更大，攻击者可实施更强且不易被检测的攻击，使得防御更具挑战性。因此需要针对多物品用户设计鲁棒的稀疏向量均值估计方法。

Method: 提出随机投影裁剪（RPC）方法。服务器首先生成一个随机二值向量发送给每个用户；用户将本地数据投影到该向量上，并对投影值进行裁剪以限制攻击者的操纵能力。针对裁剪引入的偏差，作者通过精确分析给出了偏差的解析表达式，并据此提出校正方法，从而无需进行传统的偏差-方差权衡。这使得裁剪阈值可以进一步降低，有效缩小输出空间，增强鲁棒性。

Result: 理论上：给出了任意攻击策略下估计误差的严格理论保证。实验上：在可信环境中，RPC方法的性能与现有方法相当或更优，表明其本身即是一种高效估计器；在不可信（存在投毒攻击）环境中，RPC方法的鲁棒性显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RPC方法成功解决了多物品用户场景下LDP的投毒攻击防御问题。通过随机投影、裁剪与精确偏差校正的组合，该方法无需偏差-方差权衡，既保持了良好的估计效率，又大幅提升了对投毒攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [212] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间框架下研究对抗训练的泛化行为，推导了与鲁棒性水平、样本量、源光滑性和核谱相关的泛化误差界，揭示了对抗训练中统计精度损失源于鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互，并提出了一种两阶段噪声去偏程序以恢复极小极大最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练虽在实践中广泛有效，但其在非参数统计框架下的泛化理论尚不完善。作者旨在从理论上刻画对抗训练中鲁棒性与泛化性能之间的权衡，并解决由此产生的统计精度损失问题。

Method: 在RKHS框架下，通过核积分算子分析对抗训练估计量；首先推导源一致（source-uniform）泛化误差上界；在固定多项式谱模型上建立匹配的下界；揭示噪声与鲁棒性交互导致精度损失的机制；提出两阶段噪声去偏估计程序，先估计并移除混合项中的噪声贡献，再以样本依赖方式选择鲁棒性水平。

Result: 对抗训练的泛化速率可慢于极小极大预测基准；混合鲁棒性项中的噪声降低了逼近速率但同时降低了估计复杂度；所提两阶段去偏估计器在样本依赖的鲁棒性水平下，可达极小极大多项式速率（至多差对数因子）。数值实验验证了理论发现和方法有效性。

Conclusion: 对抗训练在非参数框架下存在由噪声-鲁棒性交互导致的固有统计精度损失；通过噪声去偏程序可有效恢复最优泛化速率，为对抗鲁棒性与泛化之间的权衡提供了新解释和原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [213] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了熵最优流形回归（EOMR），将熵最优流形聚类扩展至非平稳非线性回归问题，实现子集与子空间的联合识别。EOMR 具有线性迭代和内存复杂度，在 Lorenz-96 强混沌系统和 Hasegawa-Wakatani 等离子体湍流等极具挑战性的问题上，预测精度远超梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络及 TabPFN 等主流 AI/ML 方法，模型复杂度也低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI/ML 方法在处理非平稳、非线性回归问题时，面临高维特征空间中子集与子空间联合识别的难题，尤其在强混沌和流体动力学等极端挑战场景下表现不佳。作者希望将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）框架扩展至回归问题，以实现对相关特征子集和子空间的同时识别，并保持线性复杂度的鲁棒学习能力。

Method: 本文提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），是在 EOMC 基础上的扩展。EOMR 通过联合优化实现特征子集与子空间的同步识别，适用于非平稳非线性回归。方法具有线性扩展的迭代复杂度和内存复杂度，属于计算高效的非参数回归框架，核心依赖于熵最优准则来驱动流形上的简约表示学习。

Result: 在 Lorenz-96 系统（F=8 强混沌、F=12 极强混沌）和 Hasegawa-Wakatani 等离子体边缘模型的预测任务上，EOMR 的均方根预测误差比梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和 TabPFN v.03 低数个数量级，模型复杂度同样低数个数量级。在 Hasegawa-Wakatani 案例中，EOMR 将主导 EOF 动力学蒸馏为一个仅含 8 个参数的线性、因果、弱平稳自回归过程。

Conclusion: EOMR 在强混沌和流体动力学等极具挑战性的非平稳非线性回归问题上，展现出远超当前主流 AI/ML 方法的预测精度和模型简约性。其线性复杂度和熵最优简约表示能力，使其成为高维小样本科学与工程回归问题的有力工具。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [214] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于深度学习不确定性量化的综述，聚焦于集成方法和近似贝叶斯方法，按五大家族组织方法（贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单次传递方法），并统一区分预测分布生成与不确定性度量，涵盖集成多样性理论、不确定性分解及评估方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域部署时缺乏有原则的不确定性量化能力，现有综述缺乏对高效集成近似和单次传递方法的深度讨论，且未能清晰分离预测分布生成机制与不确定性度量机制。

Method: 采用结构化批判性综述方法，将UQ方法按五大家族分类（贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单次传递方法），统一框架分离"产生预测分布的方法"与"总结不确定性的度量"；同时涵盖证据网络、共形预测、后校准、OOD检测与选择性预测等邻近工作；系统回顾集成多样性理论及不确定性度量（熵分解与成对散度度量），并整合评估方法论。

Result: 系统梳理了各方法的理论基础、实现方式、经验性能及局限性；对比了熵分解与成对散度度量两种不确定性分解路径；整合了统一的评估基准以支持定性比较；并对大语言模型中的不确定性及开放研究方向（如分类任务的高效认知度量、最后一层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性与校准、混合架构等）进行了简要展望。

Conclusion: 该综述填补了高效集成近似与单次传递方法在UQ综述中的深度缺口，通过统一框架澄清了方法-度量的概念分离，为研究人员提供了系统的分类体系和评估基准，并指出了未来在LLM不确定性、高效认知度量及混合架构等方面的研究方向。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [215] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DR-FRL 是一种交叉拟合工作流，将不规则的功能性历史数据转化为针对特定估计量的表征状态，通过功能编码器与时序编码器提取特征，配合 EIF 导向的验证诊断，使表征误差落入与普通讨厌参数误差相同的二阶乘积余项中，从而实现渐近线性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究中，实验室值、生理信号、传感器流等数据常以不规则的功能性片段形式记录，测量时间不等距且具有信息性。现有双重稳健估计器通常要求标量汇总，而序列学习器优化的预测损失未必能稳定有效影响函数（EIF），因此需要一种能将不规则历史数据转化为估计量导向表征的方法。

Method: 提出 DR-FRL 交叉拟合工作流：(1) 功能编码器和时序编码器将点云与历史映射为状态表征；(2) 讨厌参数头估计结局、治疗和删失函数；(3) 基于 EIF 的验证、校准、重叠、尾部和消融诊断评估状态是否支持估计方程；(4) 将 Catoni 聚合单独用作有界影响点估计器而非 Wald 推断的替代；(5) 在显式的率条件、重叠条件、校准条件和稳定性条件下证明均值估计量渐近线性。

Result: 模拟实验表明，在功能混杂高维、测量具有信息性、支持较弱或伪结局重尾等场景下，DR-FRL 表现优于现有方法。VitalDB 审计显示，对于 ICU 处置终点，标量实验室汇总已携带大量终点相关信息，DR-FRL 成功识别了这一负向发现。

Conclusion: DR-FRL 能将不规则功能历史转化为适合双重稳健推断的表征，表征误差与讨厌参数误差同为二阶乘积余项，保证了渐近线性。该方法在实际 ICU 数据中展现了区分信息充分性与否的实用价值。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>
